"""Local cooperative cache protocol. No process injection, GC or arbitrary code.

Embed CacheAgent in an application that owns a DiscardableCache implementation.
Only its explicit release callback may discard data. Transport is loopback TCP,
bounded, versioned and authenticated; there is no remote listening address.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import ctypes
import hmac
import math
import os
import secrets
import socket
import threading
import time
from collections import deque
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Protocol

MIB = 1024 * 1024
MAX_BUDGET = 64 * MIB


class DiscardableCache(Protocol):
    def size_bytes(self) -> int: ...
    def release(self, budget_bytes: int) -> int: ...


def private_session(directory: Path) -> str:
    """Create a new current-user-only directory and token. Never reuse a session."""
    token = secrets.token_hex(32)
    directory.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if os.name != "nt":
        directory.mkdir(mode=0o700)
        fd = os.open(directory / "token.txt", os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL, 0o600)
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="ascii") as f:
            f.write(token + "\n")
        return token
    from ctypes import wintypes as w
    kernel = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True)
    advapi = ctypes.WinDLL("advapi32", use_last_error=True)
    advapi.OpenProcessToken.argtypes = [w.HANDLE, w.DWORD, ctypes.POINTER(w.HANDLE)]
    advapi.OpenProcessToken.restype = w.BOOL
    advapi.GetTokenInformation.argtypes = [w.HANDLE, ctypes.c_int, ctypes.c_void_p, w.DWORD, ctypes.POINTER(w.DWORD)]
    advapi.GetTokenInformation.restype = w.BOOL
    advapi.ConvertSidToStringSidW.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.POINTER(w.LPWSTR)]
    advapi.ConvertSidToStringSidW.restype = w.BOOL
    advapi.ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptorW.argtypes = [w.LPCWSTR, w.DWORD, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_void_p), ctypes.POINTER(w.DWORD)]
    advapi.ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptorW.restype = w.BOOL
    kernel.GetCurrentProcess.restype = w.HANDLE
    kernel.CloseHandle.argtypes = [w.HANDLE]
    kernel.CloseHandle.restype = w.BOOL
    kernel.LocalFree.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p]
    kernel.LocalFree.restype = ctypes.c_void_p
    handle = w.HANDLE()
    if not advapi.OpenProcessToken(kernel.GetCurrentProcess(), 0x0008, ctypes.byref(handle)):
        raise ctypes.WinError(ctypes.get_last_error())
    try:
        size = w.DWORD()
        advapi.GetTokenInformation(handle, 1, None, 0, ctypes.byref(size))
        buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(size.value)
        if not advapi.GetTokenInformation(handle, 1, buf, size, ctypes.byref(size)):
            raise ctypes.WinError(ctypes.get_last_error())
        sid_pointer = ctypes.cast(buf, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_void_p))[0]
        sid_string = w.LPWSTR()
        if not advapi.ConvertSidToStringSidW(sid_pointer, ctypes.byref(sid_string)):
            raise ctypes.WinError(ctypes.get_last_error())
        try:
            sid = sid_string.value
        finally:
            kernel.LocalFree(ctypes.cast(sid_string, ctypes.c_void_p))
    finally:
        kernel.CloseHandle(handle)
    descriptor = ctypes.c_void_p()
    # Protected DACL, current user only; inherited by files and directories.
    sddl = f"D:P(A;OICI;FA;;;{sid})"
    if not advapi.ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptorW(sddl, 1, ctypes.byref(descriptor), None):
        raise ctypes.WinError(ctypes.get_last_error())

    class SecurityAttributes(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [("length", w.DWORD), ("descriptor", ctypes.c_void_p), ("inherit", w.BOOL)]

    attrs = SecurityAttributes(ctypes.sizeof(SecurityAttributes), descriptor, False)
    kernel.CreateDirectoryW.argtypes = [w.LPCWSTR, ctypes.POINTER(SecurityAttributes)]
    kernel.CreateDirectoryW.restype = w.BOOL
    try:
        if not kernel.CreateDirectoryW(str(directory.resolve()), ctypes.byref(attrs)):
            raise ctypes.WinError(ctypes.get_last_error())
        with (directory / "token.txt").open("x", encoding="ascii") as f:
            f.write(token + "\n")
    finally:
        kernel.LocalFree(descriptor)
    return token


class CacheAgent:
    """Single-client server: application cache callbacks must be thread-safe.

    latency_probe must return an application-defined recent latency in ms.
    The demo uses scheduling drift; real integrations should use useful-work
    latency (e.g. p95 request latency), not infer it from CPU or RAM.
    """
    def __init__(self, cache: DiscardableCache, token: str, latency_probe, cooldown_seconds: float = 60):
        if len(token) != 64 or any(c not in "0123456789abcdef" for c in token):
            raise ValueError("Invalid token")
        if not math.isfinite(cooldown_seconds) or cooldown_seconds < 0:
            raise ValueError("Invalid cooldown")
        self.cache, self.token, self.latency_probe = cache, token, latency_probe
        self.cooldown = cooldown_seconds
        self.last_release = -math.inf
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._listener = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
        # No SO_REUSEADDR: fail rather than share a port on Windows.
        self._listener.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
        self.port = self._listener.getsockname()[1]
        self._listener.listen(4)
        self._listener.settimeout(0.2)
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._serve, name="AME-cache-agent", daemon=True)

    def start(self):
        self._thread.start()
        return self

    def close(self):
        self._stop.set()
        self._thread.join(timeout=3)
        self._listener.close()
        if self._thread.is_alive():
            raise RuntimeError("Agent callback did not finish; callbacks must be bounded")

    def _serve(self):
        while not self._stop.is_set():
            try:
                client, _ = self._listener.accept()
            except socket.timeout:
                continue
            except OSError:
                break
            with client:
                try:
                    client.settimeout(1)
                    data = bytearray()
                    deadline = time.monotonic() + 1.5
                    while b"\n" not in data and len(data) < 256:
                        part = client.recv(min(256 - len(data), 256))
                        if not part or time.monotonic() > deadline:
                            break
                        data.extend(part)
                    if not data.endswith(b"\n") or data.count(b"\n") != 1 or len(data) >= 256:
                        reply = "AME1 ERR frame\n"
                    else:
                        reply = self._handle(data.decode("ascii", errors="strict").strip())
                    client.sendall(reply.encode("ascii"))
                except (OSError, UnicodeError, ValueError):
                    pass

    def _handle(self, message: str) -> str:
        parts = message.split()
        if len(parts) != 4 or parts[0] != "AME1":
            return "AME1 ERR protocol\n"
        if not hmac.compare_digest(parts[1], self.token):
            return "AME1 ERR auth\n"
        command, raw_budget = parts[2:]
        if not raw_budget.isascii() or not raw_budget.isdigit() or len(raw_budget) > 10:
            return "AME1 ERR budget\n"
        budget = int(raw_budget)
        if command not in ("STATUS", "RECLAIM") or (command == "STATUS" and budget != 0) or (command == "RECLAIM" and not 0 < budget <= MAX_BUDGET):
            return "AME1 ERR request\n"
        try:
            latency = float(self.latency_probe())
            if not math.isfinite(latency) or latency < 0:
                return "AME1 ERR latency\n"
            released = 0
            if command == "RECLAIM":
                now = time.monotonic()
                if latency > 20:
                    return "AME1 ERR busy\n"
                if now - self.last_release < self.cooldown:
                    return "AME1 ERR cooldown\n"
                # Mark before callback so failures cannot cause immediate retries.
                self.last_release = now
                released = self.cache.release(budget)
                if not isinstance(released, int) or not 0 <= released <= budget:
                    return "AME1 ERR callback\n"
            remaining = self.cache.size_bytes()
            if not isinstance(remaining, int) or not 0 <= remaining <= 1024 * 1024 * MIB:
                return "AME1 ERR cache\n"
            return f"AME1 OK {os.getpid()} {remaining} {released} {latency:.3f}\n"
        except Exception:
            # Never send internal exception strings or token contents to clients.
            return "AME1 ERR callback\n"


class SchedulingProbe:
    """Demo heartbeat drift; not a global Windows responsiveness measurement."""
    def __init__(self):
        self._values = deque(maxlen=200)
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._stop = threading.Event()
        self._thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)
        self._thread.start()

    def _run(self):
        target = time.monotonic() + 0.05
        while not self._stop.wait(max(0, target - time.monotonic())):
            now = time.monotonic()
            with self._lock:
                self._values.append(max(0, now - target) * 1000)
            target = now + 0.05

    def latency(self):
        with self._lock:
            values = sorted(self._values)
        return values[max(0, math.ceil(len(values) * 0.95) - 1)] if values else 0.0

    def close(self):
        self._stop.set()
        self._thread.join(timeout=1)


def request(port: int, token: str, command: str = "STATUS", budget: int = 0) -> tuple[int, int, int, float]:
    with socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", port), timeout=2) as conn:
        conn.sendall(f"AME1 {token} {command} {budget}\n".encode("ascii"))
        data = bytearray()
        while b"\n" not in data and len(data) < 512:
            chunk = conn.recv(512 - len(data))
            if not chunk:
                break
            data.extend(chunk)
    text = data.decode("ascii").split()
    if len(text) != 6 or text[:2] != ["AME1", "OK"]:
        raise RuntimeError("Agent refused request: " + " ".join(text[:3]))
    return int(text[2]), int(text[3]), int(text[4]), float(text[5])

"""Demo cache uses explicitly disposable anonymous maps, not arbitrary app RAM."""
import argparse
import json
import mmap
import os
import sys
import threading
import time
from pathlib import Path
from cooperative import CacheAgent, MIB, SchedulingProbe, private_session


class MapCache:
    def __init__(self, megabytes: int):
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._maps = []
        try:
            # Touch all pages so the demo does not report untouched reservations.
            for _ in range(megabytes):
                item = mmap.mmap(-1, MIB)
                try:
                    item[:] = b"X" * MIB
                except BaseException:
                    item.close()
                    raise
                self._maps.append(item)
        except BaseException:
            self.close()
            raise

    def size_bytes(self):
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._maps) * MIB

    def release(self, budget_bytes):
        released = 0
        with self._lock:
            while self._maps and released + MIB <= budget_bytes:
                self._maps[-1].close()
                self._maps.pop()
                released += MIB
        return released

    def close(self):
        with self._lock:
            for item in self._maps:
                item.close()
            self._maps.clear()


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--session", required=True, type=Path)
    p.add_argument("--cache-mb", type=int, default=128)
    args = p.parse_args()
    if not 1 <= args.cache_mb <= 512:
        p.error("--cache-mb must be between 1 and 512")
    token = private_session(args.session)
    cache = MapCache(args.cache_mb)
    probe = SchedulingProbe()
    agent = CacheAgent(cache, token, probe.latency).start()
    try:
        description = {"version": 1, "pid": os.getpid(), "port": agent.port, "cache_bytes": cache.size_bytes()}
        (args.session / "agent.json").write_text(json.dumps(description), encoding="utf-8")
        print(f"Demo PID={os.getpid()} | disposable cache={args.cache_mb} MB | loopback port={agent.port}", flush=True)
        print("Only this demo cache can be released. Ctrl+C stops the demo you started.", flush=True)
        while True:
            time.sleep(1)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        pass
    finally:
        agent.close()
        probe.close()
        cache.close()
        (args.session / "agent.json").unlink(missing_ok=True)
        (args.session / "token.txt").unlink(missing_ok=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())

"""One manual cache release test. Never tries to make system RAM hit 95%."""
import argparse
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path
from cooperative import MIB, request
from windows_metrics import measure


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--session", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    descriptor = json.loads((args.session / "agent.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    token = (args.session / "token.txt").read_text(encoding="ascii").strip()
    before = request(descriptor["port"], token)
    if before[0] != descriptor["pid"]:
        raise RuntimeError("Agent PID changed")
    before_metrics = measure(before[0])
    after = request(descriptor["port"], token, "RECLAIM", 64 * MIB)
    if after[0] != before[0] or after[2] > 64 * MIB or before[1] - after[1] != after[2]:
        raise RuntimeError("Inconsistent release result")
    time.sleep(0.2)
    alive = request(descriptor["port"], token)
    after_metrics = measure(alive[0])
    print(f"PID {before[0]} remains running: {alive[0] == before[0]}")
    print(f"Demo cache: {before[1] // MIB} -> {after[1] // MIB} MB")
    print(f"Explicitly closed cache maps: {after[2] // MIB} MB")
    if before_metrics is not None and after_metrics is not None:
        print(f"Windows process working set: {before_metrics['working_set_bytes'] / MIB:.1f} -> {after_metrics['working_set_bytes'] / MIB:.1f} MB")
        print(f"Windows private commit: {before_metrics['private_commit_bytes'] / MIB:.1f} -> {after_metrics['private_commit_bytes'] / MIB:.1f} MB")
        print(f"System available delta: {(after_metrics['system_available_bytes'] - before_metrics['system_available_bytes']) / MIB:.1f} MB (other apps also affect this)")
    evidence = {"pid": alive[0], "cache_before_bytes": before[1], "cache_after_bytes": after[1],
                "released_cache_bytes": after[2], "process_still_running": alive[0] == before[0],
                "windows_before": before_metrics, "windows_after": after_metrics}
    (args.session / "demo-result.json").write_text(json.dumps(evidence, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print("This is not a measured claim about system-wide RAM savings.")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""Read-only counters for the demo; returns None on non-Windows platforms."""
import ctypes
import os


def measure(pid: int):
    if os.name != "nt":
        return None
    from ctypes import wintypes as w
    kernel = ctypes.WinDLL("kernel32", use_last_error=True)
    psapi = ctypes.WinDLL("psapi", use_last_error=True)
    size_t = ctypes.c_size_t

    class Counters(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [("cb", w.DWORD), ("faults", w.DWORD),
                    ("peak_ws", size_t), ("ws", size_t),
                    ("peak_paged", size_t), ("paged", size_t),
                    ("peak_nonpaged", size_t), ("nonpaged", size_t),
                    ("pagefile", size_t), ("peak_pagefile", size_t),
                    ("private_commit", size_t)]

    class MemoryStatus(ctypes.Structure):
        _fields_ = [("length", w.DWORD), ("load", w.DWORD),
                    ("total_physical", ctypes.c_ulonglong), ("available_physical", ctypes.c_ulonglong),
                    ("total_pagefile", ctypes.c_ulonglong), ("available_pagefile", ctypes.c_ulonglong),
                    ("total_virtual", ctypes.c_ulonglong), ("available_virtual", ctypes.c_ulonglong),
                    ("available_extended", ctypes.c_ulonglong)]

    kernel.OpenProcess.argtypes = [w.DWORD, w.BOOL, w.DWORD]
    kernel.OpenProcess.restype = w.HANDLE
    kernel.CloseHandle.argtypes = [w.HANDLE]
    kernel.CloseHandle.restype = w.BOOL
    psapi.GetProcessMemoryInfo.argtypes = [w.HANDLE, ctypes.POINTER(Counters), w.DWORD]
    psapi.GetProcessMemoryInfo.restype = w.BOOL
    kernel.GlobalMemoryStatusEx.argtypes = [ctypes.POINTER(MemoryStatus)]
    kernel.GlobalMemoryStatusEx.restype = w.BOOL
    process = kernel.OpenProcess(0x1000, False, pid)
    if not process:
        raise ctypes.WinError(ctypes.get_last_error())
    try:
        m = Counters(); m.cb = ctypes.sizeof(m)
        if not psapi.GetProcessMemoryInfo(process, ctypes.byref(m), m.cb):
            raise ctypes.WinError(ctypes.get_last_error())
        s = MemoryStatus(); s.length = ctypes.sizeof(s)
        if not kernel.GlobalMemoryStatusEx(ctypes.byref(s)):
            raise ctypes.WinError(ctypes.get_last_error())
        return {"working_set_bytes": m.ws, "private_commit_bytes": m.private_commit,
                "system_available_bytes": s.available_physical}
    finally:
        kernel.CloseHandle(process)

// Listeners are registered synchronously; persisted opt-in survives worker/browser restarts.
// A single serialized queue prevents a late reconnect from undoing an explicit disconnect.
function enqueue(work) {
  const result = operations.then(work);
  operations = result.catch(() => {});
  return result;
}

}

  await api.storage.local.set({connectionNextAttempt:nextAttemptAt});
  connect(reason);
}

  let doc = documents.get(tab.id);
  if (!doc || doc.url !== tab.url) { doc = { url: tab.url, revision: crypto.randomUUID() }; documents.set(tab.id, doc); }

}

export const defaultProtected = ['chatgpt.com','claude.ai','docs.google.com','drive.google.com','mail.google.com','outlook.live.com','outlook.office.com','teams.microsoft.com','meet.google.com'];
export function blocked(tab, protectedHosts = defaultProtected, browserName = 'Chrome') {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(tab.id) || tab.id < 0) return 'Scheda non identificabile';
  if (tab.active) return 'Scheda attiva nella sua finestra';
  if (tab.pinned) return 'Scheda fissata';
  if (tab.audible) return 'Audio recente';
  if (tab.incognito) return 'Navigazione privata';
  if (tab.discarded) return 'Gia scaricata dalla memoria';
  if (tab.autoDiscardable === false || (browserName !== 'Firefox' && tab.autoDiscardable !== true)) return 'Scaricamento automatico non autorizzato dal browser';
  if (tab.status !== 'complete') return 'Pagina in caricamento';
  let url;
  try { url = new URL(tab.url); } catch { return 'Pagina non identificabile'; }
  if (!['https:', 'http:'].includes(url.protocol)) return 'Pagina interna o non supportata';
  if (protectedHosts.some(host => url.hostname === host || url.hostname.endsWith('.' + host))) return 'Sito protetto';
  return '';
}
export function validateRequest(message, session, now) {
  if (!message || message.type !== 'discard' || message.session !== session) return 'Sessione non valida';
  if (typeof message.request !== 'string' || !/^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/.test(message.request)) return 'Richiesta non valida';
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(message.tab) || typeof message.revision !== 'string') return 'Scheda non valida';
  if (!Number.isFinite(message.deadline) || message.deadline < now || message.deadline > now + 10000) return 'Richiesta scaduta';
  if (message.authorization === 'site_policy_v1') {
    if (message.confirmed !== false) return 'Autorizzazione automatica incoerente';
  } else if (message.authorization !== undefined || message.confirmed !== true) return 'Manca la conferma manuale';
  return '';
}
export function automaticBlocked(tab, allowedHosts, now, browserName='Chrome') {
  const protection=blocked(tab,defaultProtected,browserName);if(protection)return protection;
  let url;try{url=new URL(tab.url);}catch{return 'Pagina non identificabile';}
  if(url.protocol!=='https:')return 'Automatico disponibile solo su siti HTTPS autorizzati';
  if(!Array.isArray(allowedHosts) || !allowedHosts.includes(url.hostname))return 'Sito non autorizzato per interventi automatici';
  if(!Number.isFinite(tab.lastAccessed) || tab.lastAccessed<=0 || !Number.isFinite(now) || now-tab.lastAccessed<15*60*1000)return 'Scheda non inattiva da almeno 15 minuti o dato mancante';
  return '';
}

  operations = result.catch(() => {});

function revision(tab) {

function send(message, expected = port) {
  if (!expected || expected !== port) return false;
  try { expected.postMessage({ ...message, session });return true; }
  catch(e) {fail('send_failed','Invio al componente Windows fallito: '+String(e.message).slice(0,240));return false;}
}
async function snapshot() {
  if (!port || !welcomeAt || snapshotOwner === port || pendingSnapshot) return;
  const expected=port, expectedSession=session;
  snapshotOwner=expected;inventoryStage='reading_protections';
  record('inventory_start','Leggo le protezioni prima dell elenco schede.');
  try {
    const hosts=await protectedHosts();
    const allowed=await automaticHosts();
    if(expected!==port || expectedSession!==session)return;
    inventoryStage='querying_tabs';record('inventory_query','Richiedo l elenco delle schede al browser.');
    const tabs=await api.tabs.query({});
    if(expected!==port || expectedSession!==session) return;
    inventoryStage='building_snapshot';record('inventory_read','Lettura completata: '+tabs.length+' schede.');
    const seen=new Set(tabs.map(t=>t.id));
    for(const id of documents.keys()) if(!seen.has(id)) documents.delete(id);
    const sequence=++nextSequence;
    const items=[];let bytes=1000;const encoder=new TextEncoder();
    for(const tab of tabs.slice(0,MAX_TABS)) {
      let host='';try{host=new URL(tab.url).hostname;}catch{}
      const item={id:tab.id,title:String(tab.title||'Scheda').slice(0,160),revision:revision(tab),blocked:blocked(tab,hosts,browserName),discarded:tab.discarded===true,lastAccessed:tab.lastAccessed||null,host,auto_allowed:allowed.includes(host)};
      const size=encoder.encode(JSON.stringify(item)).length+1;if(bytes+size>240000)break;
      bytes+=size;items.push(item);
    }
    pendingSnapshot={sequence,sent:Date.now(),count:items.length};
    const transmitted=send({type:'snapshot',protocol:PROTOCOL,sequence,total:tabs.length,truncated:items.length<tabs.length,automatic_policy_revision:1,tabs:items},expected);
    if(transmitted){if(pendingSnapshot)inventoryStage='awaiting_ack';record('inventory_sent','Elenco inviato al componente Windows: sequenza '+sequence+', '+items.length+' schede. Attendo conferma.');}
  } catch(e) {
    if(expected===port) fail('snapshot_failed','Lettura delle schede fallita: '+String(e.message).slice(0,240));
  } finally {if(snapshotOwner===expected) snapshotOwner=null;}
}
function disconnect() {
  const old=port;port=null;session='';documents.clear();used.clear();returns.clear();
  pendingSnapshot=null;snapshotOwner=null;welcomeAt=0;lastAck=0;nextSequence=0;agentMode=false;
  if(timer) clearInterval(timer);timer=null;
  if(watchdog) clearInterval(watchdog);watchdog=null;
  if(old) try{old.disconnect();}catch{}
}
function connect(reason) {
  checkHealth();
  if(port) return;
  inventoryStage='not_started';lastError='';session=crypto.randomUUID();connectingAt=Date.now();documents.clear();used.clear();
  record(reason==='manual'?'connect_clicked':'background_attempt',reason==='manual'?'Collega premuto: avvio il componente Windows.':'Tentativo di collegamento in background.');
  change('connecting','Avvio del collegamento Windows...');
  try {
    const expected=api.runtime.connectNative(host);port=expected;
    expected.onDisconnect.addListener(()=>{
      const error=api.runtime.lastError || expected.error;
      if(port===expected) fail('native_disconnect','Collegamento Windows interrotto: '+(error?.message||'AME o il componente locale ha chiuso la connessione.'));
    });

    watchdog=setInterval(checkHealth,1000);
    send({type:'hello',protocol:PROTOCOL,extension:api.runtime.id,version:'0.5.0',connectionReason:reason,browser:browserName,autoReconnect,capabilities:['automatic-site-policy-v1']});
  } catch(e) {fail('native_connect_failed','Impossibile avviare il componente Windows: '+String(e.message).slice(0,240));}
}
async function onNative(message,expected) {
  if(message?.session!==session && message?.type!=='discard') return;
  if(message?.type==='stop_connection') {
    if(message.protocol!==PROTOCOL) return;
    void enqueue(()=>{if(expected===port) return stopBackground('Scollegato dal pannello AME. Premi Collega per riattivare il background.');}).catch(backgroundFailure);
    return;
  }
  if(message?.type==='connection_error') {
    fail(String(message.code||'hub_rejected'), 'AME ha rifiutato il collegamento: '+String(message.reason||'Consulta il registro Windows.').slice(0,300));return;
  }
  if(message?.type==='welcome') {
    if(message.protocol!==PROTOCOL || welcomeAt) {fail('protocol_mismatch','Versioni non compatibili: aggiorna AME e ricarica lâ€™estensione.');return;}
    agentMode=message.agent_mode==='automatic_authorized_sites';
    welcomeAt=Date.now();change('syncing','AME ha risposto. Attendo la conferma dellâ€™elenco schede...');
    timer=setInterval(()=>void snapshot(),10000);
    await snapshot();return;
  }
  if(message?.type==='inventory_ack') {
    if(message.protocol!==PROTOCOL || !pendingSnapshot || message.sequence!==pendingSnapshot.sequence || message.count!==pendingSnapshot.count) {
      fail('invalid_inventory_ack','Conferma dellâ€™elenco non valida. Consulta il registro Windows.');return;
    }
    inventoryStage='confirmed';lastAck=Date.now();const count=pendingSnapshot.count;pendingSnapshot=null;
    change('connected','Collegato: AME ha ricevuto '+count+' schede. '+(agentMode?'Agente automatico attivo: valuta soltanto i siti autorizzati.':'Scegli lâ€™intervento nel pannello Windows.'),'inventory_confirmed');return;
  }
  if(message?.type==='refresh') {await snapshot();return;}
  if (message?.type !== 'discard') return;
  checkHealth();
  if(expected!==port || phase!=='connected') return;
  const expectedSession = session;
  let error = validateRequest(message, expectedSession, Date.now());
  if (!error && used.has(message.request)) error = 'Richiesta gia elaborata';
  if (!error && busy) error = 'Un intervento e gia in corso';
  if (error) { send({ type:'result', request:message.request, tab:message.tab, outcome:'declined', reason:error }, expected); return; }
  if (used.size >= 1000) { send({type:'result',request:message.request,tab:message.tab,outcome:'declined',reason:'Limite sessione raggiunto: ricollega'},expected); return; }
  used.add(message.request); busy = true;
  const began = Date.now(), expectedTabEpoch=tabEpoch, expectedPolicyEpoch=policyEpoch;
  try {
    const hosts = await protectedHosts();
    const tab = await api.tabs.get(message.tab);
    if (expected !== port || expectedSession !== session) return;
    error = validateRequest(message, session, Date.now()) || blocked(tab, hosts, browserName);
    if(!error && message.authorization==='site_policy_v1')error=automaticBlocked(tab,await automaticHosts(),Date.now(),browserName);
    if(expected!==port || expectedSession!==session)return;
    if(tabEpoch!==expectedTabEpoch || policyEpoch!==expectedPolicyEpoch)error='Stato schede o autorizzazioni cambiato durante il controllo';
    if (!error && revision(tab) !== message.revision) error = 'La pagina e cambiata: aggiorna la selezione';
    if (error) throw new Error(error);
    const result = await api.tabs.discard(tab.id);
    const after = await api.tabs.get(tab.id);
    if (expected !== port || expectedSession !== session) return;
    // Discard is confirmed by state, never merely by resolved Promise.
    if ((browserName==='Chrome' && !result) || after.discarded !== true) throw new Error('Il browser non ha confermato lo scaricamento');
    returns.set(tab.id,{request:message.request,start:null});

  } catch(e) {
    send({ type:'result', request:message.request, tab:message.tab, outcome:'declined', reason:String(e.message).slice(0,240) },expected);
  } finally { busy=false; await snapshot(); }
}

  tabEpoch++;

  }
});

export const defaultProtected = ['chatgpt.com','claude.ai','docs.google.com','drive.google.com','mail.google.com','outlook.live.com','outlook.office.com','teams.microsoft.com','meet.google.com'];
export function blocked(tab, protectedHosts = defaultProtected, browserName = 'Chrome') {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(tab.id) || tab.id < 0) return 'Scheda non identificabile';
  if (tab.active) return 'Scheda attiva nella sua finestra';
  if (tab.pinned) return 'Scheda fissata';
  if (tab.audible) return 'Audio recente';
  if (tab.incognito) return 'Navigazione privata';
  if (tab.discarded) return 'Gia scaricata dalla memoria';
  if (tab.autoDiscardable === false || (browserName !== 'Firefox' && tab.autoDiscardable !== true)) return 'Scaricamento automatico non autorizzato dal browser';
  if (tab.status !== 'complete') return 'Pagina in caricamento';
  let url;
  try { url = new URL(tab.url); } catch { return 'Pagina non identificabile'; }
  if (!['https:', 'http:'].includes(url.protocol)) return 'Pagina interna o non supportata';
  if (protectedHosts.some(host => url.hostname === host || url.hostname.endsWith('.' + host))) return 'Sito protetto';
  return '';
}
export function validateRequest(message, session, now) {
  if (!message || message.type !== 'discard' || message.session !== session) return 'Sessione non valida';
  if (typeof message.request !== 'string' || !/^[a-f0-9-]{36}$/.test(message.request)) return 'Richiesta non valida';
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(message.tab) || typeof message.revision !== 'string') return 'Scheda non valida';
  if (!Number.isFinite(message.deadline) || message.deadline < now || message.deadline > now + 10000) return 'Richiesta scaduta';
  if (message.authorization === 'site_policy_v1') {
    if (message.confirmed !== false) return 'Autorizzazione automatica incoerente';
  } else if (message.authorization !== undefined || message.confirmed !== true) return 'Manca la conferma manuale';
  return '';
}
export function automaticBlocked(tab, allowedHosts, now, browserName='Chrome') {
  const protection=blocked(tab,defaultProtected,browserName);if(protection)return protection;
  let url;try{url=new URL(tab.url);}catch{return 'Pagina non identificabile';}
  if(url.protocol!=='https:')return 'Automatico disponibile solo su siti HTTPS autorizzati';
  if(!Array.isArray(allowedHosts) || !allowedHosts.includes(url.hostname))return 'Sito non autorizzato per interventi automatici';
  if(!Number.isFinite(tab.lastAccessed) || tab.lastAccessed<=0 || !Number.isFinite(now) || now-tab.lastAccessed<15*60*1000)return 'Scheda non inattiva da almeno 15 minuti o dato mancante';
  return '';
}

=== FILE: D:\.A2\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0-R22-FIX1\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0\examples\resource_api_demo.cpp ===
#include <windows.h>
#include "../sdk/ame_resource_api.h"
#include "../src/resource_trace.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstring>
#include <iostream>
#include <memory>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <thread>
#include <vector>

namespace {
constexpr uint64_t MiB = 1048576;
unsigned checks = 0;
ame::resource::Trace* trace = nullptr;
AmeResult traced(const char* expression, AmeResult status) {
    const auto windows_error = AmeGetLastWindowsError();
    if (trace) { try { trace->call(expression, status, windows_error); } catch (...) { trace->mark_failed(); } }
    return status;
}
#define AME_TRACE(expression) traced(#expression, (expression))
void check(bool condition, const char* text) {
    ++checks;
    if (trace) trace->check(text, condition);
    if (!condition) throw std::runtime_error(text);
}
void ok(AmeResult r, const char* operation) {
    if (r != AME_OK) throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": AME=" +
        std::to_string(r) + ", Windows=" + std::to_string(AmeGetLastWindowsError()));
    ++checks;
}
std::wstring executable() {
    std::wstring path(32768, L'\0');
    const DWORD length = GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, path.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(path.size()));
    if (!length || length >= path.size()) throw std::runtime_error("GetModuleFileNameW");
    path.resize(length); return path;
}
struct OwnedFixture {
    PROCESS_INFORMATION process{};
    HANDLE input = nullptr, output = nullptr;
    ~OwnedFixture() {
        // Cleanup targets only process handles returned by this fixture's CreateProcessW.
        if (input) { const char command = 'Q'; DWORD sent = 0; WriteFile(input, &command, 1, &sent, nullptr); CloseHandle(input); }
        if (process.hProcess) {
            if (WaitForSingleObject(process.hProcess, 3000) == WAIT_TIMEOUT) {
                TerminateProcess(process.hProcess, 1); WaitForSingleObject(process.hProcess, 1000);
            }
            CloseHandle(process.hProcess);
        }
        if (process.hThread) CloseHandle(process.hThread);
        if (output) CloseHandle(output);
    }
    void read(void* data, DWORD count) {
        const auto deadline = GetTickCount64() + 3000;
        DWORD available = 0;
        while (GetTickCount64() < deadline) {
            if (!PeekNamedPipe(output, nullptr, 0, nullptr, &available, nullptr)) throw std::runtime_error("Fixture pipe closed");
            if (available >= count) {
                DWORD received = 0;
                if (ReadFile(output, data, count, &received, nullptr) && received == count) return;
                throw std::runtime_error("Fixture read failed");
            }
            std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
        }
        throw std::runtime_error("Fixture response timed out");
    }
    void send(char command) {
        DWORD sent = 0;
        if (!WriteFile(input, &command, 1, &sent, nullptr) || sent != 1) throw std::runtime_error("Fixture write failed");
    }
    void ready() { char value = 0; read(&value, 1); check(value == 'R', "fixture allocation succeeded"); }
    void start() {
        SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES attributes{sizeof(attributes), nullptr, TRUE};
        HANDLE child_input = nullptr, child_output = nullptr;
        if (!CreatePipe(&child_input, &input, &attributes, 0)) throw std::runtime_error("CreatePipe stdin");
        if (!CreatePipe(&output, &child_output, &attributes, 0)) { CloseHandle(child_input); throw std::runtime_error("CreatePipe stdout"); }
        if (!SetHandleInformation(input, HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0) || !SetHandleInformation(output, HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0)) {
            CloseHandle(child_input); CloseHandle(child_output); throw std::runtime_error("SetHandleInformation");
        }
        STARTUPINFOW startup{}; startup.cb = sizeof(startup); startup.dwFlags = STARTF_USESTDHANDLES;
        startup.hStdInput = child_input; startup.hStdOutput = child_output; startup.hStdError = child_output;
        std::wstring command = L"\"" + executable() + L"\" --fixture";
        BOOL parent_in_job = FALSE;
        if (!IsProcessInJob(GetCurrentProcess(), nullptr, &parent_in_job)) {
            CloseHandle(child_input); CloseHandle(child_output); throw std::runtime_error("IsProcessInJob parent");
        }
        const DWORD flags = CREATE_NO_WINDOW | (parent_in_job ? CREATE_BREAKAWAY_FROM_JOB : 0);
        const BOOL created = CreateProcessW(nullptr, command.data(), nullptr, nullptr, TRUE, flags, nullptr, nullptr, &startup, &process);
        const DWORD error = GetLastError();
        CloseHandle(child_input); CloseHandle(child_output);
        if (!created) throw std::runtime_error("Fixture creation failed (host job may forbid breakaway): " + std::to_string(error));
        ready();
    }
    void exit() { send('Q'); check(WaitForSingleObject(process.hProcess, 3000) == WAIT_OBJECT_0, "fixture exits cooperatively"); }
};
int fixture() {
    HANDLE input = GetStdHandle(STD_INPUT_HANDLE), output = GetStdHandle(STD_OUTPUT_HANDLE);
    std::vector<void*> allocations;
    PROCESS_INFORMATION leaf{};
    struct Cleanup {
        std::vector<void*>& blocks; PROCESS_INFORMATION& child;
        ~Cleanup() {
            for (void* block : blocks) VirtualFree(block, 0, MEM_RELEASE);
            if (child.hProcess) { TerminateProcess(child.hProcess, 0); WaitForSingleObject(child.hProcess, 1000); CloseHandle(child.hProcess); }
            if (child.hThread) CloseHandle(child.hThread);
        }
    } cleanup{allocations, leaf};
    const auto allocate = [&](SIZE_T bytes) {
        void* block = VirtualAlloc(nullptr, bytes, MEM_RESERVE | MEM_COMMIT, PAGE_READWRITE);
        if (!block) return false;
        allocations.push_back(block);
        std::memset(block, 0x5a, bytes);
        char ready = 'R'; DWORD written = 0;
        return WriteFile(output, &ready, 1, &written, nullptr) && written == 1;
    };
    if (!allocate(8 * MiB)) return 2;
    char command = 0; DWORD received = 0;
    while (ReadFile(input, &command, 1, &received, nullptr) && received == 1) {
        if (command == 'Q') return 0;
        if (command == 'G' && !allocate(64 * MiB)) return 3;
        if (command == 'C') {
            STARTUPINFOW startup{}; startup.cb = sizeof(startup);
            std::wstring line = L"\"" + executable() + L"\" --leaf";
            if (!CreateProcessW(nullptr, line.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE, CREATE_NO_WINDOW, nullptr, nullptr, &startup, &leaf)) return 4;
            DWORD written = 0;
            if (!WriteFile(output, &leaf.dwProcessId, sizeof(leaf.dwProcessId), &written, nullptr) || written != sizeof(leaf.dwProcessId)) return 5;
        }
    }
    return 0;
}
std::unique_ptr<AmeProcessIdentity> identity(DWORD pid) {
    auto value = std::make_unique<AmeProcessIdentity>(); value->size = sizeof(*value);
    ok(AME_TRACE(AmeReadProcessIdentity(pid, value.get())), "AmeReadProcessIdentity");
    if (trace) trace->process(value->pid, value->creation_filetime);
    return value;
}
AmeGroupSnapshot snapshot(AmeGroup group) {
    AmeGroupSnapshot s{}; s.size = sizeof(s);
    ok(AME_TRACE(AmeReadGroupSnapshot(group, &s)), "AmeReadGroupSnapshot");
    if (trace) trace->snapshot("sample", group, s);
    return s;
}
void run(bool quiet) {
    if (trace) trace->phase("contract");
    check(AmeGetApiVersion() == AME_RESOURCE_ABI_VERSION, "DLL ABI version");
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeCreateGroup(nullptr)) == AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT, "null group output rejected");
    auto value = std::make_unique<AmeProcessIdentity>();
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeReadProcessIdentity(0, value.get())) == AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT, "invalid identity rejected");
    value->size = sizeof(*value);
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeReadProcessIdentity(0xffffffffu, value.get())) == AME_WINDOWS_ERROR && AmeGetLastWindowsError() != 0,
          "Win32 errors are available through the public API");
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeCreateGroup(nullptr)) == AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT && AmeGetLastWindowsError() == 0,
          "new argument failure clears stale Windows error");
    AmeGroup group = 0; ok(AME_TRACE(AmeCreateGroup(&group)), "AmeCreateGroup");
    struct Close { AmeGroup& id; ~Close() { if (id) AME_TRACE(AmeCloseGroup(id)); } } close{group};
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeAttachProcess(group, nullptr)) == AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT, "null attachment rejected");
    auto own = identity(GetCurrentProcessId());
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeAttachProcess(group, own.get())) == AME_PROCESS_EXCLUDED, "controller cannot attach itself");
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeSetCommitNotice(group, 1)) == AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT, "tiny threshold rejected");
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeSetCommitNotice(group, (uint64_t{1} << 40u) + 1)) == AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT, "oversized threshold rejected");
    AmeGroupSnapshot wrong{};
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeReadGroupSnapshot(group, &wrong)) == AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT, "ABI size mismatch rejected");
    AmeGroupEvent event{}; event.size = sizeof(event);
    check(AME_TRACE(AmePollGroupEvent(group, &event)) == AME_NO_EVENT, "empty completion port is nonblocking");
    auto empty = snapshot(group);
    check(empty.active_processes == 0 && (empty.flags & AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE), "new group is empty");

    if (trace) trace->phase("identity");
    OwnedFixture first; first.start(); value = identity(first.process.dwProcessId);
    auto changed = std::make_unique<AmeProcessIdentity>(*value); ++changed->creation_filetime;
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeAttachProcess(group, changed.get())) == AME_IDENTITY_CHANGED, "wrong creation time refused");
    *changed = *value; changed->image_path[0] = changed->image_path[0] == L'X' ? L'Y' : L'X';
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeAttachProcess(group, changed.get())) == AME_IDENTITY_CHANGED, "wrong executable path refused");
    *changed = *value; std::fill_n(changed->image_path, AME_RESOURCE_PATH_CAPACITY, L'X');
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeAttachProcess(group, changed.get())) == AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT, "unterminated path refused");
    check(snapshot(group).active_processes == 0, "rejected identities have no attachment side effect");
    ok(AME_TRACE(AmeAttachProcess(group, value.get())), "AmeAttachProcess");
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeAttachProcess(group, value.get())) == AME_ALREADY_MANAGED, "duplicate attachment refused");
    auto before = snapshot(group);
    if (trace) trace->snapshot("before_growth", group, before);
    check(before.active_processes == 1 && before.measured_processes == 1 &&
          (before.flags & AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE) && before.private_commit_sum_bytes >= 8 * MiB,
          "actual child private commit measured");
    check(!(before.flags & AME_RATES_VALID), "first measurement has no invented rates");
    if (trace) trace->phase("memory_notice");
    ok(AME_TRACE(AmeSetCommitNotice(group, 32 * MiB)), "AmeSetCommitNotice");
    first.send('G'); first.ready();
    const auto deadline = GetTickCount64() + 3000;
    bool noticed = false;
    while (GetTickCount64() < deadline && !noticed) {
        event = {}; event.size = sizeof(event);
        const auto status = AME_TRACE(AmePollGroupEvent(group, &event));
        if (trace && status == AME_OK) trace->event(group, event);
        check(status == AME_OK || status == AME_NO_EVENT, "kernel event polling succeeds");
        if (status == AME_OK && event.kind == AME_EVENT_COMMIT_NOTICE) {
            check(event.job_commit_bytes > event.commit_notice_bytes && event.commit_notice_bytes == 32 * MiB,
                  "real Windows commit notification includes exceeded threshold");
            noticed = true;
        }
        if (!noticed) std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(10));
    }
    check(noticed, "kernel memory notification delivered");
    auto after = snapshot(group);
    if (trace) trace->snapshot("after_growth", group, after);
    check(after.private_commit_sum_bytes >= before.private_commit_sum_bytes + 60 * MiB &&
          (after.flags & AME_COMMIT_NOTICE_EXCEEDED), "allocation beyond notice remains permitted");
    check(WaitForSingleObject(first.process.hProcess, 0) == WAIT_TIMEOUT, "notification does not terminate application");
    bool saw_rates = false;
    for (unsigned i = 0; i < 6; ++i) {
        std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(200));
        const auto measured = snapshot(group);
        saw_rates |= (measured.flags & AME_RATES_VALID) && measured.rate_interval_ms >= 1000;
    }
    check(saw_rates, "frequent reads preserve a baseline long enough to produce real rates");
    if (!quiet) {
        std::cout << "API AME nativa + Job Object Windows\n"
                  << "Processo demo: " << first.process.dwProcessId << "\n"
                  << "Memoria privata assegnata: " << before.private_commit_sum_bytes / MiB << " -> "
                  << after.private_commit_sum_bytes / MiB << " MiB\n"
                  << "Avviso Windows ricevuto oltre 32 MiB. Processo ancora attivo.\n"
                  << "Questa demo verifica gestione e misure; non esegue una riduzione RAM.\n";
    }
    if (trace) trace->phase("lifecycle");
    // Test existing-job compatibility without adding a nested job or overriding it.
    AmeGroup other = 0; ok(AME_TRACE(AmeCreateGroup(&other)), "second group");
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeAttachProcess(other, value.get())) == AME_ALREADY_MANAGED, "existing job is preserved");
    ok(AME_TRACE(AmeCloseGroup(other)), "close second group");
    first.send('C'); DWORD leaf_pid = 0; first.read(&leaf_pid, sizeof(leaf_pid));
    auto leaf = identity(leaf_pid);
    ok(AME_TRACE(AmeAttachProcess(group, leaf.get())), "child is outside group until explicitly attached");
    auto two = snapshot(group);
    check(two.active_processes == 2 && two.measured_processes == 2 && !(two.flags & AME_RATES_VALID),
          "new explicit member invalidates previous rates");
    ok(AME_TRACE(AmeSetCommitNotice(group, 0)), "disable notification");
    check(snapshot(group).commit_notice_bytes == 0, "notification removed");
    const auto old_id = group;
    ok(AME_TRACE(AmeCloseGroup(group)), "AmeCloseGroup"); group = 0;
    check(WaitForSingleObject(first.process.hProcess, 0) == WAIT_TIMEOUT, "closing group leaves application alive");
    BOOL still_managed = FALSE;
    check(IsProcessInJob(first.process.hProcess, nullptr, &still_managed) && still_managed,
          "closing monitoring does not claim to detach a live process");
    wrong = {}; wrong.size = sizeof(wrong);
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeReadGroupSnapshot(old_id, &wrong)) == AME_INVALID_GROUP && wrong.active_processes == 0,
          "closed handle refused with cleared output");
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeCloseGroup(old_id)) == AME_INVALID_GROUP, "double close refused");
    first.exit();
    // Exited identity remains distinguishable; no operation can hit a reused PID.
    AmeGroup fresh = 0; ok(AME_TRACE(AmeCreateGroup(&fresh)), "new group");
    check(fresh != old_id, "group IDs are never reused");
    const auto dead_status = AME_TRACE(AmeAttachProcess(fresh, value.get()));
    check(dead_status == AME_IDENTITY_CHANGED || dead_status == AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, "exited process refused");
    ok(AME_TRACE(AmeCloseGroup(fresh)), "close new group");
    if (trace) trace->phase("capacity");
    std::vector<AmeGroup> capacity;
    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < AME_RESOURCE_MAX_GROUPS; ++i) {
        AmeGroup id = 0; ok(AME_TRACE(AmeCreateGroup(&id)), "bounded group creation"); capacity.push_back(id);
    }
    AmeGroup excess = 123;
    check(AME_TRACE(AmeCreateGroup(&excess)) == AME_CAPACITY_REACHED && excess == 0, "group capacity bound");
    for (const auto id : capacity) ok(AME_TRACE(AmeCloseGroup(id)), "bounded group cleanup");
    std::cout << "PASS " << checks << " native API / real Windows job checks\n";
}
}
int wmain(int argc, wchar_t** argv) {
    if (argc == 2 && std::wstring(argv[1]) == L"--fixture") return fixture();
    if (argc == 2 && std::wstring(argv[1]) == L"--leaf") { Sleep(30000); return 0; }
    bool quiet = false;
    std::filesystem::path journal_path;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        const std::wstring option = argv[i];
        if (option == L"--self-test" && !quiet) quiet = true;
        else if (option == L"--trace-json" && journal_path.empty() && i + 1 < argc) journal_path = argv[++i];
        else { std::cerr << "Usage: ame-resource-demo.exe [--self-test] [--trace-json FILE]\n"; return 2; }
    }
    ame::resource::Trace journal;
    try {
        if (!journal_path.empty()) {
            journal.open(journal_path); trace = &journal;
            if (!journal.healthy()) throw std::runtime_error("Cannot start API execution journal");
        }
        run(quiet);
        if (trace) trace->finish(true, checks);
        if (trace && !trace->healthy()) throw std::runtime_error("API operations completed but execution journal could not be saved");
        trace = nullptr;
        return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) {
        if (trace) { try { trace->finish(false, checks, e.what()); } catch (...) {} }
        trace = nullptr;
        std::cerr << "FAIL " << e.what() << '\n'; return 1;
    }
}

#ifndef AME_RESOURCE_API_H
#define AME_RESOURCE_API_H

/* Windows 10/11 x64; C ABI, UTF-16 paths, default packing = 8. */
#include <stdint.h>
#include <stddef.h>
#if defined(_WIN32)
# if defined(AME_RESOURCE_API_EXPORTS)
#  define AME_API __declspec(dllexport)
# else
#  define AME_API __declspec(dllimport)
# endif
# define AME_CALL __cdecl
#else
# define AME_API
# define AME_CALL
#endif
#ifdef __cplusplus
extern "C" {
#endif

#define AME_RESOURCE_ABI_VERSION 1u
#define AME_RESOURCE_PATH_CAPACITY 32768u
#define AME_RESOURCE_MAX_GROUPS 16u
#define AME_RESOURCE_MAX_MEMBERS 64u
typedef uint64_t AmeGroup;
typedef enum AmeResult {
    AME_OK = 0,
    AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT = 1,
    AME_INVALID_GROUP = 2,
    AME_WINDOWS_ERROR = 3,
    AME_IDENTITY_CHANGED = 4,
    AME_PROCESS_EXCLUDED = 5,
    AME_ALREADY_MANAGED = 6,
    AME_CAPACITY_REACHED = 7,
    AME_NO_EVENT = 8
} AmeResult;

enum {
    AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE = 1u,
    AME_RATES_VALID = 2u,
    AME_COMMIT_NOTICE_EXCEEDED = 4u
};
typedef enum AmeEventKind {
    AME_EVENT_PROCESS_STARTED = 1,
    AME_EVENT_PROCESS_EXITED = 2,
    AME_EVENT_GROUP_EMPTY = 3,
    AME_EVENT_COMMIT_NOTICE = 4,
    AME_EVENT_OTHER = 5
} AmeEventKind;

#pragma pack(push, 8)
typedef struct AmeProcessIdentity {
    uint32_t size;
    uint32_t pid;
    uint64_t creation_filetime;
    wchar_t image_path[AME_RESOURCE_PATH_CAPACITY];
} AmeProcessIdentity;

typedef struct AmeGroupSnapshot {
    uint32_t size;
    uint32_t flags;
    uint64_t monotonic_ms;
    uint32_t attached_total;
    uint32_t active_processes;
    uint32_t measured_processes;
    uint32_t missing_processes;
    /* Sum of working sets: shared pages can be counted more than once. */
    uint64_t working_set_sum_bytes;
    uint64_t private_commit_sum_bytes;
    /* Lifetime job counters, including exited processes. I/O is not disk-only. */
    uint64_t cpu_time_100ns;
    uint64_t io_read_bytes;
    uint64_t io_write_bytes;
    uint64_t page_faults_total;
    uint64_t commit_notice_bytes;
    uint64_t membership_revision;
    uint64_t rate_interval_ms;
    double cpu_percent_machine;
    double io_read_bytes_per_second;
    double io_write_bytes_per_second;
    double page_faults_per_second;
    int64_t private_commit_change_bytes;
} AmeGroupSnapshot;

typedef struct AmeGroupEvent {
    uint32_t size;
    uint32_t kind;
    uint32_t pid;
    uint32_t windows_message;
    uint64_t monotonic_ms;
    uint64_t job_commit_bytes;
    uint64_t commit_notice_bytes;
    uint64_t policy_revision;
} AmeGroupEvent;
#pragma pack(pop)

AME_API uint32_t AME_CALL AmeGetApiVersion(void);
/* Thread-local Win32 detail of the last AmeResult-returning call. */
AME_API uint32_t AME_CALL AmeGetLastWindowsError(void);
/* Read-only identity capture. Does not grant permission to mutate the process. */
AME_API AmeResult AME_CALL AmeReadProcessIdentity(uint32_t pid, AmeProcessIdentity* identity);
AME_API AmeResult AME_CALL AmeCreateGroup(AmeGroup* group);
/* Explicit, persistent Windows job membership; no detach API exists.
   Requires the complete freshly captured identity. Existing jobs are refused.
   New children do NOT automatically enter this group. */
AME_API AmeResult AME_CALL AmeAttachProcess(AmeGroup group, const AmeProcessIdentity* identity);
/* 0 disables; otherwise 1 MiB..1 TiB. Notification only, not an allocation cap. */
AME_API AmeResult AME_CALL AmeSetCommitNotice(AmeGroup group, uint64_t bytes);
AME_API AmeResult AME_CALL AmeReadGroupSnapshot(AmeGroup group, AmeGroupSnapshot* snapshot);
/* Nonblocking; caller polls. Every AME_OK returns one real kernel message. */
AME_API AmeResult AME_CALL AmePollGroupEvent(AmeGroup group, AmeGroupEvent* event);
/* Releases AME monitoring; does not detach or terminate live members.
   Notification is cleared first. Retry on error; no stale ID reuse. */
AME_API AmeResult AME_CALL AmeCloseGroup(AmeGroup group);

#ifdef __cplusplus
}
#endif
#endif
=== FILE: D:\.A2\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0-R22-FIX1\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0\sdk\README.md ===
# AME Resource API R1

API nativa Windows 10/11 x64, implementata in C++17 con interfaccia C esportata dalla DLL `ame-resource-api.dll`. ABI versione 1. Lavora insieme ai Job Object Windows: aggiunge identita verificata, lettura dei consumi, calcolo degli andamenti e notifiche di memoria. Non richiede un driver kernel.

Questa prima versione fornisce gestione e osservazione. Non libera allocazioni, non applica EmptyWorkingSet, non cambia priorita CPU/memoria e non stima MB recuperabili. Lo storico degli esiti e la scelta adattiva degli interventi sono sviluppi successivi. Il pannello R14 carica la DLL all'avvio e usa AmeReadProcessIdentity prima di accettare una richiesta e di avviare il relativo motore R10. Non associa le selezioni ai Job Object. Il bridge C# carica il percorso assoluto della DLL validata, controlla ABI 1 e legge una struttura UTF-16 di 65552 byte. Solo la API di identita e esposta al pannello; non esistono chiamate di associazione o mutazione nel bridge.

## Compilazione e prima esecuzione

Apri **DEMO_API.cmd** dalla cartella principale: usa la propria cartella, compila la DLL e l'esempio con Visual Studio Build Tools o MinGW-w64 x64, esegue la verifica e mostra il risultato. Non serve digitare il percorso e non serve avviare come amministratore.

Dalla R13, alla fine si apre automaticamente **resoconto.html** nel browser predefinito. La pagina mostra memoria osservata, avviso Windows, operazioni cronologiche, risultati delle richieste, controlli e codice sorgente con numeri di riga. Le sezioni del codice sono espandibili e il resoconto contiene tutto il necessario per rileggerle senza rete. Non usa script o librerie web.

I file sono conservati in una nuova cartella **reports/api/API-data-ora-id/** per ogni esecuzione: esecuzione.jsonl e resoconto.html; se e stata necessaria la compilazione, anche verifica-compilazione.jsonl. Il registro JSONL contiene le chiamate realmente eseguite dal client, il risultato AME, il codice Windows, tempi monotoni, misure ed eventi. Le spiegazioni dei percorsi interni della DLL sono documentazione del codice, non una traccia di ogni singola istruzione Win32. I Getter versione/errore sono spiegati nel contratto e verificati dai controlli; il registro delle chiamate riguarda le sette operazioni che restituiscono AmeResult.

Il resoconto viene creato anche se la compilazione o la demo falliscono: indica l'errore e le eventuali chiamate registrate fino a quel momento. Se l'apertura automatica non riesce, il percorso resta stampato nella console. Per eseguire senza aprire una finestra: **demo-resource-api.ps1 -NoOpen**. Per rigenerare una pagina da un registro esistente, usare export-resource-api-report.ps1 con TracePath e OutputPath. Una riga finale parziale, una sequenza incoerente, un esito mancante o un errore non diventano una prova completa; misure/tassi mancanti rimangono non disponibili.

La demo crea un proprio processo, misura la memoria assegnata, imposta un avviso a 32 MiB e richiede al processo di assegnare altri 64 MiB. Verifica che Windows notifichi il superamento e che l'applicazione possa continuare ad assegnare memoria. Verifica poi un secondo processo, identita errate, appartenenza preesistente a un job e chiusura del gruppo senza terminazione dell'applicazione. I fixture vengono chiusi al termine. Nessuna applicazione gia aperta dall'utente viene associata o modificata.

La demo e anche una verifica di integrazione: non e una dimostrazione di RAM risparmiata. Fallisce esplicitamente se l'ambiente ospitante impedisce di creare processi fuori dal suo job. Il pannello ordinario resta avviabile con AVVIA.cmd.

I risultati compilati sono in `build/`: DLL, libreria di importazione (`ame-resource-api.lib` con MSVC o `libame-resource-api.a` con MinGW), esempio e test. Le successive aperture riutilizzano la build solo quando corrispondono ABI, revisione e hash di sorgenti/artefatti. `build-resource-api.ps1 -Force` ricompila; `-CheckOnly` restituisce soltanto lo stato di validazione senza eseguire programmi. La build MinGW richiede il relativo runtime disponibile nel PATH. CMake offre i target `ame_resource_api`, `ame_resource_demo` e `ame_resource_analysis_tests`, con test CTest dedicati.

## Chiamate disponibili

| Funzione | Comportamento |
|---|---|
| `AmeGetApiVersion` | Restituisce la versione ABI attuale, 1 |
| `AmeReadProcessIdentity` | Legge PID, tempo di creazione e percorso UTF-16 |
| `AmeCreateGroup` | Crea un Job Object privato, senza nome e senza limiti rigidi |
| `AmeAttachProcess` | Associa esplicitamente l'identita verificata al gruppo |
| `AmeSetCommitNotice` | Imposta una soglia di notifica per memoria assegnata; zero la disabilita |
| `AmeReadGroupSnapshot` | Legge memoria, contatori e tassi di variazione |
| `AmePollGroupEvent` | Restituisce un messaggio reale del kernel, senza attesa |
| `AmeCloseGroup` | Disabilita gli avvisi e rilascia il monitoraggio AME |
| `AmeGetLastWindowsError` | Legge il dettaglio Win32 dell'ultima chiamata sullo stesso thread |

## Contratto di associazione e chiusura

L'associazione a un Job Object Windows dura finche il processo termina: non esiste un detach durante l'esecuzione. `AmeCloseGroup` non stacca i processi e non li termina. Non viene impostato `KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE`. Alla chiusura gli avvisi vengono disabilitati prima di rilasciare gli handle; un errore conserva il gruppo per permettere il retry. Gli altri processi continuano a funzionare anche se il controller termina. Dopo un crash del controller puo rimanere configurata una soglia di sola notifica fino alla fine dei membri; non blocca nuove allocazioni.

La R1 rifiuta processi gia associati a qualsiasi job, compresi altri gruppi AME. Non modifica job esterni e non tenta l'annidamento. Non promette quindi l'associazione di ogni applicazione Windows. Le applicazioni incompatibili restano osservabili tramite il motore AME esistente. I figli creati DOPO l'associazione non entrano automaticamente nel gruppo: e impostato `SILENT_BREAKAWAY_OK`. Ogni identita deve essere associata esplicitamente. Non viene inventata una famiglia di processi in base al solo nome dell'eseguibile.

Prima dell'associazione vengono ricontrollati PID/creazione/percorso sul medesimo handle, esecuzione ancora attiva, stesso proprietario, stessa sessione, criticita nota e assenza di percorso nella cartella Windows. Il controller non puo associare se stesso. Qualsiasi controllo non disponibile produce un errore. Per chiamare AssignProcessToJobObject Windows richiede anche il diritto PROCESS_TERMINATE; la DLL non invoca alcuna terminazione. Questi controlli sono una policy della libreria e non sostituiscono i permessi di Windows o l'autorizzazione del chiamante.

Un gruppo e un insieme esplicito di identita, non un nuovo processo e non una macchina virtuale. Massimo 16 gruppi aperti e 64 associazioni complessive per gruppo, inclusi membri gia terminati. Gli ID sono locali alla DLL caricata e non vengono riutilizzati durante la sua vita. Nessun handle Win32 del job viene esportato. Dopo unload/reload gli ID precedenti non sono validi. Chiudere tutti i gruppi prima di scaricare la DLL e serializzare l'unload con i chiamanti.

## Misure: cosa significano

- `private_commit_sum_bytes`: somma della memoria privata assegnata ai membri attivi misurabili. Non e tutta residente nella RAM fisica.
- `working_set_sum_bytes`: somma dei working set. Le pagine condivise possono essere contate piu volte: non e l'impronta fisica unica del gruppo e non va presentata come RAM liberabile.
- `cpu_time_100ns`, I/O e fault: contatori cumulativi del job, che includono anche i membri terminati. L'I/O non e soltanto disco e i fault non distinguono hard e soft.
- `AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE`: tutti i processi attivi del job sono stati misurati durante la lettura. Le letture non sono atomiche; una terminazione puo rendere il campione incompleto.
- `AME_RATES_VALID`: confronto disponibile tra campioni completi con la stessa composizione, a distanza di 1..6 secondi. Senza questo flag gli zeri nei campi calcolati NON significano attivita nulla. Una lettura iniziale, una nuova associazione, un processo terminato, un contatore inverso o un intervallo troppo lungo invalidano il confronto. Letture piu frequenti mantengono la baseline fino ad almeno un secondo.
- `cpu_percent_machine`: percentuale normalizzata sul numero di processori logici attivi del PC; non e la percentuale di un singolo core.
- `private_commit_change_bytes`: incremento o diminuzione misurata della memoria assegnata rispetto alla baseline. Non attribuisce causalmente la variazione ad AME.
- `AME_COMMIT_NOTICE_EXCEEDED`: confronto locale tra somma privata completa e soglia configurata. Non significa che sia arrivato un messaggio del kernel.

La soglia accetta zero oppure 1 MiB..1 TiB ed e impostata con JobObjectNotificationLimitInformation. Consente ulteriori allocazioni oltre la soglia. I messaggi di avvio/uscita non sono garantiti: usare sempre gli snapshot per conoscere lo stato corrente. Gli avvisi di questa classe sono documentati come garantiti alla completion port; `AmePollGroupEvent` legge e riconosce l'avviso con QueryInformationJobObject per consentire il successivo. I valori dell'avviso descrivono la violazione letta al momento del riconoscimento, non un campione storico salvato all'istante dell'accodamento. Un vecchio messaggio dopo la disabilitazione viene restituito come evento generico, senza inventare una violazione attuale.

## Uso da C/C++ e interoperabilita

Includere `ame_resource_api.h`, collegare la libreria d'importazione x64 e mantenere la DLL vicino all'eseguibile o in un percorso di caricamento affidabile. Il sorgente completo in `examples/resource_api_demo.cpp` chiama soltanto le funzioni pubbliche della DLL per la gestione del gruppo. Da C#, la stessa ABI e utilizzabile con P/Invoke (CallingConvention.Cdecl, campi e packing corrispondenti, stringhe UTF-16); un binding gestito non e incluso nella R1.

Ordine tipico: verificare versione, creare gruppo, leggere identita, associare, impostare avviso, leggere snapshot e messaggi, chiudere. Inizializzare il campo `size = sizeof(struct)` prima di ogni chiamata con una struttura. Non passare puntatori invalidi o buffer insufficienti. Controllare sempre `AmeResult`; i dati d'uscita sono utilizzabili solo con `AME_OK`. `AME_NO_EVENT` non e un errore e non contiene un evento valido.

Le funzioni non fanno propagare eccezioni C++ oltre la DLL. Le operazioni sono serializzate, senza thread interno e senza callback; il polling non blocca. Il client decide la frequenza, indicativamente 1..2 s per snapshot. Evitare cicli senza pausa sugli eventi e usare un worker per le chiamate dal pannello. Non chiamare dalla DllMain, da un signal handler o mentre si scarica la DLL.

## Fonti Windows e verifica

- [Job Objects e durata dell'associazione](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/procthread/job-objects)
- [AssignProcessToJobObject e diritti richiesti](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/jobapi2/nf-jobapi2-assignprocesstojobobject)
- [Soglie di notifica](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winnt/ns-winnt-jobobject_notification_limit_information)
- [Lettura della violazione](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/winnt/ns-winnt-jobobject_limit_violation_information)

La prova R12 condivisa dall'utente il 12 ottobre conferma compilazione MSVC x64, PASS 41 controlli dell'analisi e PASS 96 controlli della demo reale Windows, sia durante la validazione sia durante l'esempio. Riporta memoria assegnata 8 -> 72 MiB e avviso oltre 32 MiB, con processo ancora attivo. Conferma il funzionamento della base API, non un risparmio RAM o un guadagno prestazionale.

In questo ambiente Linux: analisi, produzione/lettura JSONL e resoconto verificati. Client R13 cross-compilato e collegato per Windows x64 con avvisi trattati come errori. Il resoconto R13 ricevuto il 12 ottobre conferma una sessione Windows completata in 1,386 s con 96 controlli e un avviso reale oltre 32 MiB. Il nuovo bridge R14 e stato verificato qui con contratti gestiti e handler reali del pannello, ma il suo caricamento Windows nel pannello e ancora da confermare sul PC. Il test di avvio locale usa un processo figlio sintetico dichiarato come tale, controlla gli argomenti con spazi e intercetta l'apertura invece di avviare un browser.

## Verifica del bridge del pannello R14

`tests/test-resource-api-client.ps1` verifica marshaling, UTF-16, identita cambiate, codici di errore, API non disponibile e blocco prima dell'avvio del motore con un backend sintetico esplicito. Su Windows, dopo la compilazione API, `tests/test-resource-api-client.ps1 -NativeWindows` aggiunge la lettura reale dell'identita del solo processo di test tramite DLL. L'inizializzazione ordinaria del pannello esegue comunque una lettura reale della propria identita: la riga API pronta compare soltanto se riesce. Gli eventi `resource_api/ready`, `identity_verified`, `identity_rejected` e `unavailable` entrano nel registro del pannello. La validazione dell'identita al clic non sostituisce i controlli nativi immediatamente precedenti al trimming.

## Strategia applicativa R15

AVVIA continua a caricare la API di identita nel pannello. La strategia aggiunge uno storico di prove e un percorso applicativo per Chrome nella sezione Rilascio nelle app. Questo percorso usa chrome.tabs.discard e il native messaging bridge gia presenti: non estende l'ABI R1, non assegna i renderer ai Job Object e non rende il trimming una liberazione delle allocazioni. Il journal della demo API resta separato dai report di azioni Chrome.
=== FILE: D:\.A2\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0-R22-FIX1\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0\src\browser_host.cpp ===
#include <windows.h>
#include <sddl.h>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include "browser_identity.hpp"

namespace {
constexpr std::uint32_t limit = 262144;
bool transfer(HANDLE h,void* buffer,DWORD count,DWORD& transferred,bool writing,bool overlapped) {
    if(!overlapped) return writing ? WriteFile(h,buffer,count,&transferred,nullptr)!=FALSE
                                  : ReadFile(h,buffer,count,&transferred,nullptr)!=FALSE;
    OVERLAPPED operation{};
    operation.hEvent=CreateEventW(nullptr,TRUE,FALSE,nullptr);
    if(!operation.hEvent)return false;
    const BOOL immediate=writing ? WriteFile(h,buffer,count,&transferred,&operation)
                                 : ReadFile(h,buffer,count,&transferred,&operation);
    bool ok=immediate!=FALSE;
    if(!ok && GetLastError()==ERROR_IO_PENDING)
        ok=GetOverlappedResult(h,&operation,&transferred,TRUE)!=FALSE;
    // Each direction owns its event and OVERLAPPED until completion.
    CloseHandle(operation.hEvent);
    return ok;
}
bool read_exact(HANDLE h,void* buffer,DWORD length,bool overlapped) {
    auto p=static_cast<unsigned char*>(buffer);
    while(length) { DWORD n=0; if(!transfer(h,p,length,n,false,overlapped)||n==0||n>length)return false; p+=n; length-=n; }
    return true;
}
bool write_exact(HANDLE h,void* buffer,DWORD length,bool overlapped) {
    auto p=static_cast<unsigned char*>(buffer);
    while(length) { DWORD n=0; if(!transfer(h,p,length,n,true,overlapped)||n==0||n>length)return false; p+=n; length-=n; }
    return true;
}
bool forward(HANDLE from,HANDLE to,bool from_overlapped,bool to_overlapped) {
    std::uint32_t length=0;
    if(!read_exact(from,&length,4,from_overlapped)||length==0||length>limit)return false;
    std::vector<unsigned char> data(length);
    return read_exact(from,data.data(),length,from_overlapped)&&write_exact(to,&length,4,to_overlapped)&&write_exact(to,data.data(),length,to_overlapped);
}
struct Channel { HANDLE pipe, output; };
DWORD WINAPI output_loop(void* raw) {
    auto c=static_cast<Channel*>(raw);
    while(forward(c->pipe,c->output,true,false)) { }
    // stdin may be blocked: native host has no state to restore.
    ExitProcess(0);
}
std::wstring pipe_name() {
    HANDLE token=nullptr;
    if(!OpenProcessToken(GetCurrentProcess(),TOKEN_QUERY,&token))return {};
    DWORD size=0; GetTokenInformation(token,TokenUser,nullptr,0,&size);
    std::vector<unsigned char> data(size);
    if(!size||!GetTokenInformation(token,TokenUser,data.data(),size,&size)) { CloseHandle(token); return {}; }
    CloseHandle(token); LPWSTR sid=nullptr;
    if(!ConvertSidToStringSidW(reinterpret_cast<TOKEN_USER*>(data.data())->User.Sid,&sid))return {};
    DWORD session=0;
    if(!ProcessIdToSessionId(GetCurrentProcessId(),&session)) { LocalFree(sid); return {}; }
    std::wstring name=L"\\\\.\\pipe\\AME.v03."+std::wstring(sid)+L"."+std::to_wstring(session);
    LocalFree(sid); return name;
}
}
int wmain(int argc,wchar_t** argv) {
    wchar_t executable[32768]{};
    const DWORD length=GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr,executable,32768);
    bool firefox_manifest_matches=false;
    if(argc==3 && length && length<32768) {
        const std::wstring path(executable,length);
        const auto slash=path.find_last_of(L"\\/");
        if(slash!=std::wstring::npos) {
            const auto expected=path.substr(0,slash+1)+L"firefox-native-host.json";
            firefox_manifest_matches=CompareStringOrdinal(argv[1],-1,expected.c_str(),-1,TRUE)==CSTR_EQUAL;
        }
    }
    if(!ame_browser_launch_allowed(argc,argv,firefox_manifest_matches))return 2;
    const auto name=pipe_name(); if(name.empty())return 3;
    if(!WaitNamedPipeW(name.c_str(),3000))return 4;
    HANDLE pipe=CreateFileW(name.c_str(),GENERIC_READ|GENERIC_WRITE,0,nullptr,OPEN_EXISTING,FILE_FLAG_OVERLAPPED,nullptr);
    if(pipe==INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE)return 5;
    Channel channel{pipe,GetStdHandle(STD_OUTPUT_HANDLE)};
    HANDLE thread=CreateThread(nullptr,0,output_loop,&channel,0,nullptr);
    if(!thread) { CloseHandle(pipe); return 6; }
    while(forward(GetStdHandle(STD_INPUT_HANDLE),pipe,false,true)) { }
    // Process exit tears down the blocked forwarding thread and its handles.
    ExitProcess(0);
}

#pragma once
inline constexpr const wchar_t* ame_browser_origin = L"chrome-extension://gckcbijchchbeeijjcmgaiifcchhhbjl/";
#include <string_view>
inline constexpr const wchar_t* ame_firefox_id = L"ame@advancedmemoryengine.local";
inline bool ame_browser_launch_allowed(int argc, const wchar_t* const* argv, bool firefox_manifest_matches) {
    if(argc < 2 || !argv || !argv[1]) return false;
    if(std::wstring_view(argv[1]) == ame_browser_origin) return true;
    return argc == 3 && argv[2] && std::wstring_view(argv[2]) == ame_firefox_id && firefox_manifest_matches;
}

    public sealed class Sampler {
        [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] struct Memory {
            public uint Cb, Faults;
            public UIntPtr PeakWs, Ws, PeakPaged, Paged, PeakNonPaged, NonPaged, Pagefile, PeakPagefile, PrivateUsage, PrivateWs, SharedCommit;
        }
        [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] struct Status {

        }
        [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] struct Io { public ulong ReadOperations, WriteOperations, OtherOperations, ReadBytes, WriteBytes, OtherBytes; }

        [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError=true)] static extern bool GetProcessIoCounters(IntPtr h, out Io io);
        [DllImport("psapi.dll", SetLastError=true)] static extern bool GetProcessMemoryInfo(IntPtr h, ref Memory m, uint size);
        readonly string browser, enginePath, hostPath;
        readonly int ownPid, session;
        Dictionary<string,Counter> previous;
        long previousAt;
        public Sampler(string processName, string projectRoot) {
            if(processName!="chrome" && processName!="firefox")throw new ArgumentException("Browser non supportato");
            browser=processName; enginePath=Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(projectRoot,"build","ame.exe"));
            hostPath=Path.GetFullPath(Path.Combine(projectRoot,"build","ame-browser-host.exe"));
            using(Process p=Process.GetCurrentProcess()){ownPid=p.Id;session=p.SessionId;}
        }
        public Reading Read() {
            Reading r=new Reading(); Status status=new Status(); status.Length=(uint)Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Status));
            if(!GlobalMemoryStatusEx(ref status))throw new System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception();
            r.AvailableBytes=checked((long)status.Available);r.TotalBytes=checked((long)status.Total);
            long appPrivate=0, ownPrivate=0; bool appComplete=true, ownComplete=true, countersComplete=true, ownFound=false;
            Dictionary<string,Counter> current=new Dictionary<string,Counter>(); List<string> identities=new List<string>();
            List<Process> candidates=new List<Process>();
            candidates.AddRange(Process.GetProcessesByName(browser));
            candidates.AddRange(Process.GetProcessesByName("ame"));candidates.AddRange(Process.GetProcessesByName("ame-browser-host"));
            candidates.Add(Process.GetProcessById(ownPid));
            foreach(Process p in candidates) {
                using(p) {
                    bool app=false; IntPtr h=IntPtr.Zero;
                    try {
                        if(p.SessionId!=session)continue;
                        app=string.Equals(p.ProcessName,browser,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        if(app)r.AppProcesses++;
                        h=OpenProcess(0x410,false,(uint)p.Id);if(h==IntPtr.Zero)throw new IOException("Lettura processo non disponibile");
                        long created,exited,kernel,user;
                        if(!GetProcessTimes(h,out created,out exited,out kernel,out user))throw new IOException("Identita non disponibile");
                        StringBuilder image=new StringBuilder(32768);int count=image.Capacity;
                        if(!QueryFullProcessImageName(h,0,image,ref count))throw new IOException("Percorso non disponibile");
                        if(created<=0 || (app && !string.Equals(Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(image.ToString()),browser,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))throw new IOException("Identita cambiata durante la lettura");
                        bool own=p.Id==ownPid || string.Equals(image.ToString(),enginePath,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) || string.Equals(image.ToString(),hostPath,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
                        if(!app && !own)continue;
                        Memory m=new Memory();m.Cb=(uint)Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(Memory));
                        if(!GetProcessMemoryInfo(h,ref m,m.Cb) || m.PrivateWs.ToUInt64()>m.Ws.ToUInt64())throw new IOException("RAM privata residente non disponibile");
                        long bytes=checked((long)m.PrivateWs.ToUInt64());
                        if(own){ownPrivate=checked(ownPrivate+bytes);if(p.Id==ownPid)ownFound=true;}
                        if(app) {
                            appPrivate=checked(appPrivate+bytes);r.AppCommitBytes=checked(r.AppCommitBytes+(long)m.PrivateUsage.ToUInt64());
                            string key=p.Id+":"+created+":"+image.ToString().ToLowerInvariant();identities.Add(key);
                            Io io;if(GetProcessIoCounters(h,out io))current[key]=new Counter {Cpu=checked(kernel+user),Faults=m.Faults,Reads=io.ReadBytes};
                            else countersComplete=false;
                        }
                    } catch { if(app)appComplete=false;else ownComplete=false; }
                    finally {if(h!=IntPtr.Zero)CloseHandle(h);}
                }
            }
            identities.Sort(StringComparer.Ordinal);r.IdentityKey=string.Join("|",identities.ToArray());
            r.AppPrivateBytes=appComplete?(long?)appPrivate:null;r.OwnPrivateBytes=ownComplete && ownFound?(long?)ownPrivate:null;
            r.Complete=appComplete && ownComplete && ownFound;
            long now=Stopwatch.GetTimestamp();double seconds=(now-previousAt)/(double)Stopwatch.Frequency;
            bool covered=previous!=null && previous.Count==current.Count && countersComplete && appComplete && seconds>0 && seconds<=12;
            double cpu=0,faults=0,reads=0;
            if(covered)foreach(KeyValuePair<string,Counter> item in current) {
                Counter before;if(!previous.TryGetValue(item.Key,out before) || item.Value.Cpu<before.Cpu || item.Value.Reads<before.Reads || item.Value.Faults<before.Faults){covered=false;break;}
                cpu+=(item.Value.Cpu-before.Cpu)/10000000.0;faults+=item.Value.Faults-before.Faults;reads+=item.Value.Reads-before.Reads;
            }
            r.CounterCoverageComplete=covered;
            if(covered){r.CpuPercent=100*cpu/seconds/Environment.ProcessorCount;r.PageFaultsPerSecond=faults/seconds;r.IoReadBytesPerSecond=reads/seconds;}
            previous=current;previousAt=now;return r;
        }
    }

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.IO.Pipes;
using System.Text;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Concurrent;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Security.Principal;
