using Microsoft.Win32.SafeHandles;

namespace AmeHub {
    public static class Frames {
        public const int Limit = 262144;
        static readonly UTF8Encoding Utf8 = new UTF8Encoding(false, true);
        static bool Exact(Stream s, byte[] b, bool eof) {
            int at = 0;
            while (at < b.Length) {
                int n = s.Read(b, at, b.Length-at);
                if (n == 0) { if (at == 0 && eof) return false; throw new EndOfStreamException("Frame incompleto"); }
                at += n;
            }
            return true;
        }
        public static string Read(Stream s) {
            byte[] h = new byte[4]; if (!Exact(s,h,true)) return null;
            uint length = (uint)(h[0] | h[1]<<8 | h[2]<<16 | h[3]<<24);
            if (length == 0 || length > Limit) throw new InvalidDataException("Dimensione frame non valida");
            byte[] b = new byte[(int)length]; Exact(s,b,false); return Utf8.GetString(b);
        }
        public static void Write(Stream s, string value) {
            byte[] b = Utf8.GetBytes(value);
            if (b.Length == 0 || b.Length > Limit) throw new InvalidDataException("Dimensione frame non valida");
            uint n = (uint)b.Length;
            byte[] h = new byte[] {(byte)n,(byte)(n>>8),(byte)(n>>16),(byte)(n>>24)};
            s.Write(h,0,4); s.Write(b,0,b.Length); s.Flush();
        }
        public static string ReadWithTimeout(Stream s,int milliseconds) {
            Task<string> task=Task.Factory.StartNew(delegate { return Read(s); });
            if(!task.Wait(milliseconds))throw new TimeoutException("Risposta host non ricevuta");
            return task.Result;
        }
    }

    // Read bytes in C#: avoid PowerShell overload conversion of mutable buffers.
    public sealed class ReportTail : IDisposable {
        readonly FileStream stream;
        readonly Decoder decoder = new UTF8Encoding(false,true).GetDecoder();
        readonly StringBuilder partial = new StringBuilder();
        public ReportTail(string path) {
            stream = new FileStream(path,FileMode.Open,FileAccess.Read,FileShare.ReadWrite|FileShare.Delete);
        }
        public bool HasUnreadBytes { get { return stream.Position < stream.Length; } }
        public string[] ReadLines() {
            byte[] b = new byte[262144]; int n = stream.Read(b,0,b.Length);
            if (n == 0) return new string[0];
            char[] c = new char[n+2]; int count = decoder.GetChars(b,0,n,c,0,false);
            List<string> result = new List<string>();
            for (int i=0;i<count;i++) {
                if (c[i]=='\n') { result.Add(partial.ToString().TrimEnd('\r').TrimStart('\uFEFF')); partial.Clear(); }
                else { partial.Append(c[i]); if(partial.Length > 4194304) throw new InvalidDataException("Riga report oltre 4 MiB"); }
            }
            return result.ToArray();
        }
        public void Dispose() { stream.Dispose(); }
    }

    public sealed class BrowserBridge : IDisposable {
        [StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential)] struct SecurityAttributes { public int Length; public IntPtr Descriptor; public int Inherit; }
        [DllImport("advapi32.dll",CharSet=CharSet.Unicode,SetLastError=true)] static extern bool ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptor(string s,uint r,out IntPtr d,out uint size);
        [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern IntPtr LocalFree(IntPtr p);
        [DllImport("kernel32.dll",CharSet=CharSet.Unicode,SetLastError=true)] static extern SafePipeHandle CreateNamedPipe(string name,uint open,uint mode,uint max,uint output,uint input,uint timeout,ref SecurityAttributes attributes);
        [DllImport("kernel32.dll",SetLastError=true)] static extern bool GetNamedPipeClientProcessId(SafePipeHandle pipe,out uint pid);
        [DllImport("kernel32.dll",SetLastError=true)] static extern IntPtr OpenProcess(uint access,bool inherit,uint pid);
        [DllImport("kernel32.dll",CharSet=CharSet.Unicode,SetLastError=true)] static extern bool QueryFullProcessImageName(IntPtr process,uint flags,StringBuilder name,ref int size);
        [DllImport("kernel32.dll")] static extern bool CloseHandle(IntPtr handle);
        readonly object sync = new object();
        readonly ConcurrentDictionary<int,Client> clients = new ConcurrentDictionary<int,Client>();
        readonly ConcurrentQueue<BridgeEvent> events = new ConcurrentQueue<BridgeEvent>();
        readonly string pipeName, hostPath;
        Thread acceptThread;
        NamedPipeServerStream waiting;
        volatile bool stopping;
        int nextId, queued;
        sealed class Client {
            public readonly NamedPipeServerStream Pipe;
            public readonly BlockingCollection<string> Outbox = new BlockingCollection<string>(16);
            readonly BrowserBridge owner; readonly int id; int closed;
            public Client(BrowserBridge b,int n,NamedPipeServerStream p) { owner=b; id=n; Pipe=p; }
            public void Start() {
                Thread r = new Thread(delegate() {
                    try { string j; while((j=Frames.Read(Pipe))!=null) owner.Enqueue(new BridgeEvent {Connection=id,Json=j}); }
                    catch(Exception e) { Stop(e.Message); } finally { Stop("Collegamento browser chiuso"); }
                }); r.IsBackground=true; r.Start();
                Thread w = new Thread(delegate() {
                    try { foreach(string j in Outbox.GetConsumingEnumerable()) Frames.Write(Pipe,j); }
                    catch(Exception e) { Stop(e.Message); }
                }); w.IsBackground=true; w.Start();
            }
            public void Stop(string reason) {
                if(Interlocked.Exchange(ref closed,1)!=0)return;
                Outbox.CompleteAdding(); Pipe.Dispose();
                Client unused; owner.clients.TryRemove(id,out unused);
                owner.Enqueue(new BridgeEvent {Connection=id,Error=reason});
            }
        }
        public BrowserBridge(string expectedHostPath) {
            hostPath=Path.GetFullPath(expectedHostPath);
            pipeName="AME.v03."+WindowsIdentity.GetCurrent().User.Value+"."+Process.GetCurrentProcess().SessionId;
            // Create the first listener synchronously so duplicate hubs fail visibly.
            waiting=CreatePipe(true);
            acceptThread=new Thread(Accept); acceptThread.IsBackground=true; acceptThread.Start();
        }
        NamedPipeServerStream CreatePipe(bool first) {
            IntPtr sd; uint length;
            if(!ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptor("D:P(A;;GA;;;"+WindowsIdentity.GetCurrent().User.Value+")",1,out sd,out length)) throw new System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception();
            try {
                SecurityAttributes a = new SecurityAttributes {Length=Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(SecurityAttributes)),Descriptor=sd,Inherit=0};
                SafePipeHandle h=CreateNamedPipe("\\\\.\\pipe\\"+pipeName,0x40000003u|(first?0x00080000u:0u),8u,8u,Frames.Limit,Frames.Limit,0,ref a);
                if(h.IsInvalid) { h.Dispose(); throw new System.ComponentModel.Win32Exception(); }
                try { return new NamedPipeServerStream(PipeDirection.InOut,true,false,h); }
                catch { h.Dispose(); throw; }
            } finally { LocalFree(sd); }
        }
        void CheckPeer(NamedPipeServerStream p) {
            uint pid; if(!GetNamedPipeClientProcessId(p.SafePipeHandle,out pid)) throw new IOException("Identita host non disponibile");
            IntPtr h=OpenProcess(0x1000,false,pid);
            if(h==IntPtr.Zero)throw new IOException("Host non verificabile");
            try {
                StringBuilder s=new StringBuilder(32768); int size=s.Capacity;
                if(!QueryFullProcessImageName(h,0,s,ref size)||!String.Equals(s.ToString(),hostPath,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) throw new IOException("Host diverso da questa installazione: aggiorna il collegamento del browser");
            } finally { CloseHandle(h); }
        }
        void Accept() {
            while(!stopping) {
                NamedPipeServerStream p=null;
                try {
                    lock(sync) { if(stopping)return; if(waiting==null)waiting=CreatePipe(false); p=waiting; }
                    p.WaitForConnection();
                    lock(sync) { waiting=null; }
                    CheckPeer(p);
                    int id=Interlocked.Increment(ref nextId); Client c=new Client(this,id,p); clients[id]=c; c.Start(); p=null;
                } catch(Exception e) {
                    if(p!=null)p.Dispose(); lock(sync) { if(waiting==p)waiting=null; }
                    if(!stopping) { Enqueue(new BridgeEvent {Error=e.Message}); Thread.Sleep(250); }
                }
            }
        }
        void Enqueue(BridgeEvent e) {
            if(Interlocked.Increment(ref queued)>128) { Interlocked.Decrement(ref queued); Client c; if(clients.TryGetValue(e.Connection,out c))c.Stop("Coda eventi oltre il limite"); return; }
            events.Enqueue(e);
        }
        public BridgeEvent[] Drain() {
            List<BridgeEvent> list=new List<BridgeEvent>(); BridgeEvent e;
            while(events.TryDequeue(out e)) { Interlocked.Decrement(ref queued); list.Add(e); }
            return list.ToArray();
        }
        public bool Send(int connection,string json) {
            Client c; if(!clients.TryGetValue(connection,out c))return false;
            try { return c.Outbox.TryAdd(json); } catch(InvalidOperationException) { return false; }
        }
        public void Disconnect(int id) { Client c; if(clients.TryGetValue(id,out c))c.Stop("Collegamento interrotto dall'hub"); }
        public void Dispose() {
            stopping=true; lock(sync) { if(waiting!=null)waiting.Dispose(); }
            foreach(Client c in clients.Values)c.Stop("Hub chiuso");
            acceptThread.Join(500);
        }
    }

namespace {
using Clock = std::chrono::steady_clock;
std::atomic<bool> stop{false};
BOOL WINAPI control(DWORD event) {
    if (event == CTRL_C_EVENT || event == CTRL_BREAK_EVENT) { stop.store(true); return TRUE; }
    return FALSE;
}
struct Handle {
    HANDLE value = nullptr;
    explicit Handle(HANDLE h) : value(h) {}
    ~Handle() { if (value && value != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) CloseHandle(value); }
    Handle(const Handle&) = delete; Handle& operator=(const Handle&) = delete;
};
std::uint64_t ticks(const FILETIME& t) {
    return (static_cast<std::uint64_t>(t.dwHighDateTime) << 32U) | t.dwLowDateTime;
}
std::string utf8(const wchar_t* str) {
    const int n = WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, str, -1, nullptr, 0, nullptr, nullptr);
    if (n <= 1) return {};
    std::string s(static_cast<std::size_t>(n), '\0');
    WideCharToMultiByte(CP_UTF8, 0, str, -1, s.data(), n, nullptr, nullptr);
    s.pop_back(); return s;
}
std::string quote(const std::string& value) {
    std::ostringstream o; o << '"';
    for (unsigned char c : value) {
        if (c == '\\' || c == '"') o << '\\' << static_cast<char>(c);
        else if (c < 32) o << "\\u" << std::hex << std::setw(4) << std::setfill('0') << static_cast<unsigned>(c) << std::dec;
        else o << static_cast<char>(c);
    }
    o << '"'; return o.str();
}
ame::SystemSample sample_system() {
    PERFORMANCE_INFORMATION p{}; p.cb = sizeof(p);
    if (!GetPerformanceInfo(&p, sizeof(p))) throw std::runtime_error("GetPerformanceInfo failed: " + std::to_string(GetLastError()));
    const auto bytes = [page = static_cast<std::uint64_t>(p.PageSize)](SIZE_T n) { return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(n) * page; };
    return {bytes(p.PhysicalTotal), bytes(p.PhysicalAvailable), bytes(p.CommitTotal), bytes(p.CommitLimit),
        bytes(p.SystemCache), bytes(p.KernelPaged), bytes(p.KernelNonpaged)};
}
struct CountersEx2 { PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS_EX base; SIZE_T private_ws; ULONG64 shared_commit; };
bool same_user(HANDLE target);
struct Process {
    DWORD pid = 0, parent = 0, session = 0;
    bool own_user = false;
    std::string name;
    std::wstring image;
    std::uint64_t creation = 0, cpu_ticks = 0, ws = 0, private_ws = 0, commit = 0;
    DWORD faults = 0;
    bool memory_known = false, private_ws_known = false, cpu_known = false, rate_known = false;
    bool foreground = false, critical_known = false, critical = false;
    double cpu_percent = 0, faults_per_second = 0, low_activity_seconds = 0;
};
struct History { std::uint64_t creation, cpu; DWORD faults; bool cpu_known, memory_known; double time, low_activity; };
bool process_critical(HANDLE process, PBOOL critical) {
    using CriticalFn = BOOL(WINAPI*)(HANDLE, PBOOL);
    static CriticalFn fn = []() {
        const auto symbol = GetProcAddress(GetModuleHandleW(L"kernel32.dll"), "IsProcessCritical");
        CriticalFn result = nullptr;
        static_assert(sizeof(result) == sizeof(symbol), "Windows function pointer size");
        std::memcpy(&result, &symbol, sizeof(result));
        return result;
    }();
    return fn && fn(process, critical) != FALSE;
}
class Collector {
    std::map<DWORD, History> previous_;
    unsigned processors_ = 1;
    bool strict_ = false;
public:
    explicit Collector(bool strict = false) : strict_(strict) { SYSTEM_INFO s{}; GetSystemInfo(&s); processors_ = std::max(1UL, s.dwNumberOfProcessors); }
    std::vector<Process> capture(double now) {
        Handle snapshot(CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0));
        if (snapshot.value == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) throw std::runtime_error("Process snapshot failed");
        DWORD foreground = 0; GetWindowThreadProcessId(GetForegroundWindow(), &foreground);
        std::map<DWORD, History> next;
        std::vector<Process> out;
        PROCESSENTRY32W entry{}; entry.dwSize = sizeof(entry);
        if (!Process32FirstW(snapshot.value, &entry)) return out;
        do {
            Process p; p.pid = entry.th32ProcessID; p.parent = entry.th32ParentProcessID;
            p.name = utf8(entry.szExeFile); p.foreground = foreground != 0 && p.pid == foreground;
            Handle h(OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, p.pid));
            if (h.value) {
                p.own_user = same_user(h.value);
                ProcessIdToSessionId(p.pid, &p.session);
                std::wstring image(32768, L'\0'); DWORD image_size = static_cast<DWORD>(image.size());
                if (QueryFullProcessImageNameW(h.value, 0, image.data(), &image_size)) {
                    image.resize(image_size);
                    for (auto& c : image) c = static_cast<wchar_t>(std::towlower(c));
                    p.image = std::move(image);
                }
                FILETIME created{}, exited{}, kernel{}, user{};
                if (GetProcessTimes(h.value, &created, &exited, &kernel, &user)) {
                    p.creation = ticks(created); p.cpu_ticks = ticks(kernel) + ticks(user); p.cpu_known = true;
                }
                BOOL critical = FALSE;
                if (process_critical(h.value, &critical)) { p.critical_known = true; p.critical = critical != FALSE; }
                CountersEx2 m{}; m.base.cb = sizeof(m);
                if (GetProcessMemoryInfo(h.value, reinterpret_cast<PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS*>(&m), sizeof(m))) {
                    p.private_ws_known = m.private_ws <= m.base.WorkingSetSize;
                    p.private_ws = p.private_ws_known ? m.private_ws : 0;
                    p.memory_known = true;
                } else {
                    m = {}; m.base.cb = sizeof(m.base);
                    p.memory_known = GetProcessMemoryInfo(h.value, reinterpret_cast<PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS*>(&m.base), sizeof(m.base)) != FALSE;
                }
                if (p.memory_known) { p.ws = m.base.WorkingSetSize; p.commit = m.base.PrivateUsage; p.faults = m.base.PageFaultCount; }
            }
            auto old = previous_.find(p.pid);
            if (old != previous_.end() && p.creation && old->second.creation == p.creation) {
                const double dt = now - old->second.time;
                if (dt > 0 && dt <= 6 && p.cpu_known && old->second.cpu_known && p.memory_known && old->second.memory_known && p.cpu_ticks >= old->second.cpu) {
                    p.rate_known = true;
                    p.cpu_percent = static_cast<double>(p.cpu_ticks - old->second.cpu) / 1e7 / dt / processors_ * 100.0;
                    p.faults_per_second = static_cast<double>(static_cast<DWORD>(p.faults - old->second.faults)) / dt;
                    if (!p.foreground && p.cpu_percent < (strict_ ? 0.1 : 0.5) && p.faults_per_second < (strict_ ? 10.0 : 100.0))
                        p.low_activity_seconds = old->second.low_activity + dt;
                }
            }
            next.emplace(p.pid, History{p.creation, p.cpu_ticks, p.faults, p.cpu_known, p.memory_known, now, p.low_activity_seconds});
            out.push_back(std::move(p));
        } while (Process32NextW(snapshot.value, &entry));
        previous_ = std::move(next);
        std::sort(out.begin(), out.end(), [](const Process& a, const Process& b) { return a.ws > b.ws; });
        return out;
    }
};
#include "priority_lease.hpp"
#include "priority_tests.hpp"
#include "recover_controller.hpp"
#include "recover_tests.hpp"
std::string read_token(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
    std::ifstream in(path); std::string s;
    if (!in || !std::getline(in, s)) throw std::runtime_error("Token file not readable");
    if (!s.empty() && s.back() == '\r') s.pop_back();
    if (s.size() != 64 || s.find_first_not_of("0123456789abcdef") != std::string::npos)
        throw std::runtime_error("Invalid token file (expected 64 lowercase hex digits)");
    return s;
}
class AgentClient {
    unsigned short port_;
    std::string token_;
public:
    AgentClient(unsigned short port, std::string token) : port_(port), token_(std::move(token)) {}
    ame::AgentStatus request(bool release) const {
        SOCKET s = socket(AF_INET, SOCK_STREAM, IPPROTO_TCP);
        if (s == INVALID_SOCKET) throw std::runtime_error("Agent socket failed");
        struct Cleanup { SOCKET s; ~Cleanup() { closesocket(s); } } cleanup{s};
        u_long nonblocking = 1;
        if (ioctlsocket(s, FIONBIO, &nonblocking) != 0) throw std::runtime_error("Agent nonblocking setup failed");
        sockaddr_in addr{}; addr.sin_family = AF_INET; addr.sin_port = htons(port_); addr.sin_addr.s_addr = htonl(INADDR_LOOPBACK);
        if (connect(s, reinterpret_cast<sockaddr*>(&addr), sizeof(addr)) == SOCKET_ERROR) {
            if (WSAGetLastError() != WSAEWOULDBLOCK) throw std::runtime_error("Agent not reachable");
            fd_set writable, errors; FD_ZERO(&writable); FD_ZERO(&errors); FD_SET(s, &writable); FD_SET(s, &errors);
            timeval timeout{1, 0};
            if (select(0, nullptr, &writable, &errors, &timeout) <= 0 || FD_ISSET(s, &errors)) throw std::runtime_error("Agent connect timeout");
            int error = 0, size = sizeof(error);
            if (getsockopt(s, SOL_SOCKET, SO_ERROR, reinterpret_cast<char*>(&error), &size) != 0 || error) throw std::runtime_error("Agent connect failed");
        }
        nonblocking = 0;
        if (ioctlsocket(s, FIONBIO, &nonblocking) != 0) throw std::runtime_error("Agent blocking setup failed");
        DWORD timeout = 1500;
        if (setsockopt(s, SOL_SOCKET, SO_RCVTIMEO, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&timeout), sizeof(timeout)) != 0 ||
            setsockopt(s, SOL_SOCKET, SO_SNDTIMEO, reinterpret_cast<const char*>(&timeout), sizeof(timeout)) != 0)
            throw std::runtime_error("Agent timeout setup failed");
        const std::string message = "AME1 " + token_ + (release ? " RECLAIM 67108864\n" : " STATUS 0\n");
        std::size_t sent = 0;
        while (sent < message.size()) {
            const int n = send(s, message.data() + sent, static_cast<int>(message.size() - sent), 0);
            if (n <= 0) throw std::runtime_error("Agent send failed");
            sent += static_cast<std::size_t>(n);
        }
        std::string reply;
        const auto deadline = Clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(2);
        while (reply.size() < 512 && Clock::now() < deadline) {
            char buffer[512]; int n = recv(s, buffer, static_cast<int>(sizeof(buffer)), 0);
            if (n <= 0) throw std::runtime_error("Agent reply timeout or disconnect");
            reply.append(buffer, static_cast<std::size_t>(n));
            const auto newline = reply.find('\n');
            if (newline != std::string::npos) {
                if (newline != reply.size() - 1) throw std::runtime_error("Unexpected extra reply");
                return ame::parse_agent_reply(reply, release ? 64 * ame::MiB : 0);
            }
        }
        throw std::runtime_error("Agent oversized/incomplete reply");
    }
};
struct Options { bool cooperative = false, optimize = false, recover = false, single_test = false, inventory = false; std::vector<std::wstring> allowed; std::vector<std::pair<std::uint32_t,std::uint64_t>> instances; unsigned seconds = 120; unsigned short port = 0; std::filesystem::path token, stop_file, report = L"ame-report.jsonl"; };
unsigned integer(const std::string& value, unsigned max) {
    if (value.empty() || value.find_first_not_of("0123456789") != std::string::npos) throw std::runtime_error("Invalid integer option");
    const auto n = std::stoul(value); if (!n || n > max) throw std::runtime_error("Option outside range"); return static_cast<unsigned>(n);
}
Options parse(int argc, wchar_t** argv) {
    Options o; bool observe = false;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        const auto arg = utf8(argv[i]);
        if (arg == "--help") {
            std::cout << "AdvancedMemoryEngine 0.5.0\n"
                "ame.exe [--observe | --cooperative | --optimize | --recover | --recover-test] [--seconds 120] [--report path.jsonl]\n"
                "        [--agent-port PORT --token-file PATH] [--allow-exe FULL_PATH (repeatable)]\n"
                "Default: read-only. Optimize: leased memory priorities for selected background apps.\n"
                "Recover: selected PID:creation identities, single-process resident trim, no allocation release.\n"
                "Recover-test: exactly one selected identity, one attempt, no pressure requirement, other guards unchanged.\n"
                "        --allow-process PID:creation_filetime (repeatable); --inventory for 10-second process snapshots.\n"
                "Cooperative: releases only agent-registered cache. Optional --stop-file PATH.\n";
            std::exit(0);
        } else if (arg == "--observe") observe = true;
        else if (arg == "--cooperative") o.cooperative = true;
        else if (arg == "--optimize") o.optimize = true;
        else if (arg == "--recover") { if(o.single_test)throw std::runtime_error("Choose one recovery mode"); o.recover = true; }
        else if (arg == "--recover-test") { if(o.recover)throw std::runtime_error("Choose one recovery mode"); o.recover = true; o.single_test = true; }
        else if (arg == "--inventory") o.inventory = true;
        else if (i + 1 < argc && arg == "--allow-exe") {
            std::filesystem::path image(argv[++i]);
            if (!valid_selected_image(image)) throw std::runtime_error("allow-exe must be an absolute .exe path: " + image.u8string());
            o.allowed.push_back(lower_image(image.wstring()));
        }
        else if (i + 1 < argc && arg == "--allow-process") o.instances.push_back(ame::selected_identity(utf8(argv[++i])));
        else if (i + 1 < argc && arg == "--seconds") o.seconds = integer(utf8(argv[++i]), 86400);
        else if (i + 1 < argc && arg == "--agent-port") o.port = static_cast<unsigned short>(integer(utf8(argv[++i]), 65535));
        else if (i + 1 < argc && arg == "--token-file") o.token = argv[++i];
        else if (i + 1 < argc && arg == "--stop-file") o.stop_file = argv[++i];
        else if (i + 1 < argc && arg == "--report") o.report = argv[++i];
        else throw std::runtime_error("Unknown or incomplete option: " + arg);
    }
    if (static_cast<int>(observe) + static_cast<int>(o.cooperative) + static_cast<int>(o.optimize) + static_cast<int>(o.recover) > 1) throw std::runtime_error("Choose one mode");
    if ((o.port != 0) != !o.token.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("Agent requires both port and token file");
    if (o.cooperative && !o.port) throw std::runtime_error("Cooperative mode requires a configured agent");
    if ((o.optimize || o.recover) && o.allowed.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("Mutation mode requires at least one --allow-exe");
    if (!o.optimize && !o.recover && !o.allowed.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("allow-exe requires optimize or recover mode");
    if (o.recover && o.instances.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("Recover requires explicit --allow-process PID:creation_filetime identities");
    if (!o.recover && !o.instances.empty()) throw std::runtime_error("allow-process requires recover mode");
    if (o.single_test && (o.instances.size()!=1 || o.allowed.size()!=1 || o.seconds>180)) throw std::runtime_error("Recover-test requires one identity, one image and at most 180 seconds");
    return o;
}
}
int wmain(int argc, wchar_t** argv) {
    try {
        if (argc >= 2 && std::wstring(argv[1]) == L"--priority-watch") return priority_watch(argc, argv);
        if (argc == 2 && std::wstring(argv[1]) == L"--test-priority-lease") return test_priority_lease();
        if (argc >= 2 && std::wstring(argv[1]) == L"--test-owner-exit") return test_owner_exit(argc, argv);
        if (argc == 2 && std::wstring(argv[1]) == L"--test-recovery") return test_recovery();
        const auto options = parse(argc, argv);
        WSADATA wsa{};
        if (WSAStartup(MAKEWORD(2, 2), &wsa)) throw std::runtime_error("WSAStartup failed");
        struct WsaCleanup { ~WsaCleanup() { WSACleanup(); } } wsa_cleanup;
        SetConsoleCtrlHandler(control, TRUE);
        std::ofstream report(options.report, std::ios::out | std::ios::trunc);
        if (!report) throw std::runtime_error("Report not writable");
        std::optional<AgentClient> agent;
        if (options.port) agent.emplace(options.port, read_token(options.token));
        Collector collector(options.recover); ame::PressurePolicy policy; ame::ActionGate gate;
        std::unique_ptr<NativeController> native;
        if (options.optimize) native = std::make_unique<NativeController>(options.allowed, report);
        std::unique_ptr<RecoverController> recovery;
        if (options.recover) recovery = std::make_unique<RecoverController>(options.allowed, options.instances, report, options.single_test);
        struct Pending { double time, latency; ame::SystemSample before; std::uint64_t released; };
        std::optional<Pending> feedback;
        std::optional<std::uint32_t> expected_pid;
        const auto start = Clock::now();
        auto next = start;
        unsigned iteration = 0;
        SetConsoleOutputCP(CP_UTF8);
        std::cout << "AdvancedMemoryEngine 0.5.0 - " << (options.single_test ? "ONE SELECTED PROCESS / ONE ATTEMPT / MEASURED TEST" : options.recover ? "WINDOWS CONTROLLED RESIDENT MEMORY RECOVERY" : options.optimize ? "WINDOWS MEMORY PRIORITY CONTROLLER" : options.cooperative ? "COOPERATIVE CACHE ONLY" : "OBSERVE / NO MEMORY CHANGES") << "\nCtrl+C: stop. Report: " << options.report.u8string() << '\n';
        while (!stop.load()) {
            const double now = std::chrono::duration<double>(Clock::now() - start).count();
            if (!ame::recover_observation_allowed(now,options.seconds,recovery&&recovery->feedback_pending()) ||
                (!options.stop_file.empty() && std::filesystem::exists(options.stop_file))) break;
            const auto system = sample_system(); const auto phase = policy.update(system, now);
            auto processes = collector.capture(now);
            report << std::fixed << std::setprecision(3) << "{\"type\":\"sample\",\"elapsed_seconds\":" << now << ",\"phase\":" << quote(ame::phase_name(phase))
                << ",\"physical_total_bytes\":" << system.total << ",\"physical_available_bytes\":" << system.available
                << ",\"commit_bytes\":" << system.commit << ",\"commit_limit_bytes\":" << system.commit_limit
                << ",\"system_cache_bytes\":" << system.cache << ",\"kernel_paged_bytes\":" << system.kernel_paged
                << ",\"kernel_nonpaged_bytes\":" << system.kernel_nonpaged << ",\"processes\":[";
            const bool complete_snapshot = options.inventory || options.recover ? iteration % 5 == 0 : !options.optimize || iteration % 30 == 0;
            bool first = true;
            for (const auto& p : processes) {
                if (!complete_snapshot) break;
                if (!first) report << ',';
                first = false;
                report << "{\"pid\":" << p.pid << ",\"parent_pid\":" << p.parent << ",\"name\":" << quote(p.name)
                    << ",\"image_path\":" << quote(utf8(p.image.c_str())) << ",\"same_user\":" << (p.own_user ? "true" : "false") << ",\"session_id\":" << p.session
                    << ",\"creation_filetime\":\"" << p.creation << "\",\"memory_available\":" << (p.memory_known ? "true" : "false")
                    << ",\"working_set_bytes\":" << p.ws << ",\"private_working_set_bytes\":";
                if (p.private_ws_known) report << p.private_ws; else report << "null";
                report << ",\"private_commit_bytes\":" << p.commit << ",\"foreground_window_owner\":" << (p.foreground ? "true" : "false")
                    << ",\"cpu_percent\":";
                if (p.rate_known) report << p.cpu_percent; else report << "null";
                report << ",\"page_faults_per_second\":";
                if (p.rate_known) report << p.faults_per_second; else report << "null";
                report << ",\"low_activity_seconds\":" << p.low_activity_seconds << ",\"critical\":";
                if (p.critical_known) report << (p.critical ? "true" : "false"); else report << "null";
                report << '}';
            }
            report << "],\"process_snapshot_complete\":" << (complete_snapshot ? "true" : "false") << "}\n";
            std::cout << std::fixed << std::setprecision(1) << '[' << std::setw(6) << now << "s] RAM " << system.used_percent()
                << "% | available " << system.available / ame::MiB << " MB | commit " << system.commit_percent()
                << "% | " << ame::phase_name(phase) << '\n';
            if (iteration++ % 5 == 0) {
                std::cout << "    PID        WS_MB   private_commit_MB  CPU%  process (no reclaim estimate)\n";
                unsigned count = 0;
                for (const auto& p : processes) {
                    if (!p.memory_known) continue;
                    std::cout << "    " << std::setw(7) << p.pid << std::setw(10) << p.ws / ame::MiB << std::setw(20) << p.commit / ame::MiB << "  ";
                    if (p.rate_known) std::cout << std::setw(5) << p.cpu_percent; else std::cout << "  N/D";
                    std::cout << "  " << p.name << (p.foreground ? " [foreground]" : "") << '\n';
                    if (++count == 8) break;
                }
            }
            if (native) native->update(system, processes, now);
            if (recovery) recovery->update(system, processes, now,now<options.seconds);
            if (agent && !gate.disabled()) {
                try {
                    const auto status = agent->request(false);
                    if (expected_pid && *expected_pid != status.pid) throw std::runtime_error("Agent identity changed; restart controller");
                    expected_pid = status.pid;
                    if (feedback && now - feedback->time >= 15.0) {
                        const bool degraded = ame::feedback_degraded(feedback->latency, status.latency_ms);
                        if (degraded) gate.disable();
                        const auto available_delta = static_cast<std::int64_t>(system.available) - static_cast<std::int64_t>(feedback->before.available);
                        const auto commit_delta = static_cast<std::int64_t>(system.commit) - static_cast<std::int64_t>(feedback->before.commit);
                        report << "{\"type\":\"feedback\",\"elapsed_seconds\":" << now << ",\"pid\":" << status.pid
                            << ",\"agent_released_bytes\":" << feedback->released << ",\"system_available_delta_bytes\":" << available_delta
                            << ",\"system_commit_delta_bytes\":" << commit_delta << ",\"latency_ms\":" << status.latency_ms
                            << ",\"further_actions_disabled\":" << (degraded ? "true" : "false") << "}\n";
                        std::cout << "    Feedback: released cache " << feedback->released / ame::MiB << " MB; system available delta " << available_delta / static_cast<std::int64_t>(ame::MiB)
                            << " MB (not proof of causation). Latency " << status.latency_ms << " ms" << (degraded ? " - further requests disabled" : "") << '\n';
                        feedback.reset();
                    }
                    if (gate.allowed(phase, options.cooperative, status, now) && !feedback) {
                        gate.action_started(now);
                        const auto result = agent->request(true);
                        if (result.pid != status.pid) throw std::runtime_error("Agent identity changed during request");
                        feedback = Pending{now, status.latency_ms, system, result.released_bytes};
                        report << "{\"type\":\"cooperative_release\",\"elapsed_seconds\":" << now << ",\"pid\":" << result.pid << ",\"released_bytes\":" << result.released_bytes << "}\n";
                        std::cout << "    Cooperative agent released " << result.released_bytes / ame::MiB << " MB of registered cache.\n";
                    }
                } catch (const std::exception& e) {
                    // If a reply is lost, the agent may have applied the request.
                    // Disable instead of retrying an ambiguous mutation.
                    gate.disable();
                    report << "{\"type\":\"agent_error\",\"elapsed_seconds\":" << now << ",\"message\":" << quote(e.what()) << ",\"further_actions_disabled\":true}\n";
                    std::cout << "    Agent disabled: " << e.what() << '\n';
                }
            }
            report.flush(); if (!report) throw std::runtime_error("Report write failed");
            if (recovery && recovery->finished()) break;
            next += std::chrono::seconds(2);
            if (next < Clock::now()) next = Clock::now();
            while (!stop.load() && Clock::now() < next) std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(100));
        }
        if (native) native->shutdown(std::chrono::duration<double>(Clock::now() - start).count());
        if (recovery) recovery->shutdown(std::chrono::duration<double>(Clock::now() - start).count());
        report << "{\"type\":\"end\",\"feedback_pending\":" << (feedback ? "true" : "false") << "}\n";
        report.flush(); if (!report) throw std::runtime_error("Final report write failed");
        return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) { std::cerr << "ERROR: " << e.what() << '\n'; return 1; }
}

#pragma once
#include "policy.hpp"
#include <set>
#include <vector>

namespace ame {
struct Identity {
    std::uint32_t pid = 0, parent = 0;
    std::uint64_t creation = 0;
    std::string image;
    bool foreground = false;
    bool launcher = false;
};
// Parent PIDs are trusted only when the parent's creation precedes the child.
// Protect same-image app siblings as well: browser descendants may be reparented.
// Shared shells may belong to the actual branch, but their image must never
// join separate console instances and hence unrelated applications.
inline std::set<std::uint32_t> foreground_family(const std::vector<Identity>& rows) {
    std::set<std::uint32_t> protected_ids;
    std::set<std::string> images;
    for (const auto& p : rows) if (p.foreground) {
        protected_ids.insert(p.pid);
        if (!p.launcher && !p.image.empty()) images.insert(p.image);
    }
    bool changed = true;
    while (changed) {
        changed = false;
        for (const auto& p : rows) {
            if (protected_ids.count(p.pid) || (!p.launcher && images.count(p.image) && !p.image.empty())) {
                changed |= protected_ids.insert(p.pid).second;
                if (!p.launcher && !p.image.empty()) changed |= images.insert(p.image).second;
                for (const auto& parent : rows) {
                    if (p.parent == parent.pid && p.creation && parent.creation && parent.creation <= p.creation) {
                        // Stop at shared desktop/shell launchers, otherwise include helpers
                        // whose image differs from their application's root executable.
                        if (!parent.launcher)
                            changed |= protected_ids.insert(parent.pid).second;
                    }
                }
            }
            for (const auto& parent : rows) {
                if (p.parent == parent.pid && protected_ids.count(parent.pid) && p.creation && parent.creation && parent.creation <= p.creation)
                    changed |= protected_ids.insert(p.pid).second;
            }
        }
    }
    return protected_ids;
}
class NativePressure {
    std::optional<double> since_, previous_;
    bool active_ = false;
public:
    bool update(const SystemSample& s, double now) {
        if (!s.valid() || !std::isfinite(now) || (previous_ && (now < *previous_ || now - *previous_ > 6))) {
            since_.reset(); previous_.reset(); return active_ = false;
        }
        previous_ = now;
        if (active_) {
            if (s.used_percent() < 75.0) { active_ = false; since_.reset(); }
            return active_;
        }
        if (s.used_percent() < 80.0) { since_.reset(); return false; }
        if (!since_) since_ = now;
        return active_ = now - *since_ >= 15;
    }
};
inline bool native_candidate(bool active, bool allowed_image, bool protected_family,
    bool known, bool critical_known, bool critical, double idle, std::uint64_t ws) {
    return active && allowed_image && !protected_family && known && critical_known && !critical &&
        std::isfinite(idle) && idle >= 30 && ws >= 128 * MiB;
}
inline bool native_regression(double cpu, double faults, double baseline_faults) {
    return !std::isfinite(cpu) || !std::isfinite(faults) || !std::isfinite(baseline_faults) ||
        cpu >= 1.0 || faults > std::max(500.0, 3.0 * baseline_faults);
}
}

#pragma once
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <string>

namespace ame {
constexpr std::uint64_t MiB = 1024ULL * 1024;
struct SystemSample {
    std::uint64_t total = 0, available = 0, commit = 0, commit_limit = 0;
    std::uint64_t cache = 0, kernel_paged = 0, kernel_nonpaged = 0;
    double used_percent() const { return total ? 100.0 * static_cast<double>(total - available) / static_cast<double>(total) : 0; }
    double commit_percent() const { return commit_limit ? 100.0 * static_cast<double>(commit) / static_cast<double>(commit_limit) : 0; }
    bool valid() const { return total > 0 && available <= total && commit_limit > 0; }
};
enum class Phase { unknown, normal, pending, pressure };
inline const char* phase_name(Phase p) {
    switch (p) { case Phase::normal:return "NORMAL"; case Phase::pending:return "PENDING"; case Phase::pressure:return "PRESSURE"; default:return "UNKNOWN"; }
}
class PressurePolicy {
    std::optional<double> since_, previous_;
    Phase phase_ = Phase::unknown;
public:
    Phase update(const SystemSample& s, double now) {
        if (!s.valid() || !std::isfinite(now) || (previous_ && (now < *previous_ || now - *previous_ > 6.0))) {
            since_.reset(); phase_ = Phase::unknown; previous_.reset();
            return phase_;
        }
        previous_ = now;
        const auto low_floor = std::min<std::uint64_t>(512 * MiB, s.total * 3 / 100);
        const bool high = s.used_percent() >= 95.0 || s.available < low_floor || s.commit_percent() >= 90.0;
        const bool recovered = s.used_percent() < 88.0 && s.available >= s.total * 6 / 100 && s.commit_percent() < 85.0;
        if (phase_ == Phase::pressure) {
            if (recovered) { phase_ = Phase::normal; since_.reset(); }
            return phase_;
        }
        if (!high) { since_.reset(); return phase_ = Phase::normal; }
        if (!since_) since_ = now;
        return phase_ = now - *since_ >= 15.0 ? Phase::pressure : Phase::pending;
    }
};
struct AgentStatus {
    std::uint32_t pid = 0;
    std::uint64_t cache_bytes = 0, released_bytes = 0;
    double latency_ms = 0;
};
inline bool feedback_degraded(double before, double after) {
    return !std::isfinite(before) || !std::isfinite(after) || before < 0 || after < 0 || after > std::max(20.0, before * 2.0);
}
class ActionGate {
    std::optional<double> last_action_;
    bool disabled_ = false;
public:
    bool allowed(Phase phase, bool opted_in, const AgentStatus& s, double now) const {
        return !disabled_ && opted_in && phase == Phase::pressure && s.pid > 0 && s.cache_bytes > 0 &&
            std::isfinite(now) && std::isfinite(s.latency_ms) && s.latency_ms >= 0 && s.latency_ms <= 20.0 &&
            (!last_action_ || now - *last_action_ >= 60.0);
    }
    void action_started(double now) { last_action_ = now; }
    void disable() { disabled_ = true; }
    bool disabled() const { return disabled_; }
};
}

#pragma once
// Included after main.cpp's Win32 utilities. A separate process owns each change.
// Only duplicated, explicitly inherited handles cross the process boundary.

std::wstring lower_image(std::wstring image) {
    for (auto& c : image) c = static_cast<wchar_t>(std::towlower(c));
    return image;
}
bool valid_selected_image(const std::filesystem::path& image) {
    // Do not stat the executable: a live WindowsApps image can be visible through
    // QueryFullProcessImageName even when filesystem metadata is inaccessible.
    // The target must still match this exact path through its opened process handle.
    const auto text = image.wstring();
    return image.is_absolute() && lower_image(image.extension().wstring()) == L".exe" &&
        text.find(L'"') == std::wstring::npos && text.find(L'\0') == std::wstring::npos;
}
std::wstring process_image(HANDLE h) {
    std::wstring result(32768, L'\0'); DWORD size = static_cast<DWORD>(result.size());
    if (!QueryFullProcessImageNameW(h, 0, result.data(), &size)) return {};
    result.resize(size); return lower_image(result);
}
std::vector<unsigned char> process_user(HANDLE process) {
    HANDLE raw = nullptr;
    if (!OpenProcessToken(process, TOKEN_QUERY, &raw)) return {};
    Handle token(raw); DWORD size = 0;
    GetTokenInformation(token.value, TokenUser, nullptr, 0, &size);
    if (!size) return {};
    std::vector<unsigned char> buffer(size);
    if (!GetTokenInformation(token.value, TokenUser, buffer.data(), size, &size)) return {};
    return buffer;
}
bool same_user(HANDLE target) {
    static const auto self = process_user(GetCurrentProcess());
    const auto other = process_user(target);
    if (self.empty() || other.empty()) return false;
    return EqualSid(reinterpret_cast<const TOKEN_USER*>(self.data())->User.Sid,
        reinterpret_cast<const TOKEN_USER*>(other.data())->User.Sid) != FALSE;
}
unsigned memory_priority(HANDLE h) {
    MEMORY_PRIORITY_INFORMATION p{};
    if (!GetProcessInformation(h, ProcessMemoryPriority, &p, sizeof(p))) return 0;
    return p.MemoryPriority;
}
bool set_memory_priority(HANDLE h, unsigned value) {
    MEMORY_PRIORITY_INFORMATION p{value};
    return SetProcessInformation(h, ProcessMemoryPriority, &p, sizeof(p)) != FALSE;
}
int priority_watch(int argc, wchar_t** argv) {
    if (argc != 7) return 5;
    HANDLE handles[4]{};
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
        std::wstring value(argv[i + 2]);
        if (value.empty() || value.find_first_not_of(L"0123456789") != std::wstring::npos) return 5;
        handles[i] = reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(static_cast<std::uintptr_t>(std::stoull(value)));
    }
    const unsigned seconds = static_cast<unsigned>(std::stoul(argv[6]));
    if (seconds < 1 || seconds > 120) return 5;
    Handle target(handles[0]), owner(handles[1]), ready(handles[2]), cancel(handles[3]);
    // Respect existing application policies; lower NORMAL only to BELOW_NORMAL.
    if (WaitForSingleObject(owner.value, 0) != WAIT_TIMEOUT ||
        WaitForSingleObject(target.value, 0) != WAIT_TIMEOUT ||
        memory_priority(target.value) != MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL) return 5;
    if (!set_memory_priority(target.value, MEMORY_PRIORITY_BELOW_NORMAL)) return 3;
    const auto applied_priority = memory_priority(target.value);
    if (applied_priority != MEMORY_PRIORITY_BELOW_NORMAL) {
        if (!applied_priority) set_memory_priority(target.value, MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL);
        return 3;
    }
    // If readiness cannot be reported, immediately roll back.
    const bool notified = SetEvent(ready.value) != FALSE;
    if (notified) {
        HANDLE waits[]{owner.value, cancel.value, target.value};
        WaitForMultipleObjects(3, waits, FALSE, seconds * 1000);
    }
    if (WaitForSingleObject(target.value, 0) == WAIT_OBJECT_0) return 4;
    // Preserve a different priority chosen subsequently by the application.
    // Windows exposes no atomic compare-and-set; a concurrent identical value is indistinguishable.
    const auto current = memory_priority(target.value);
    if (!current) return 3;
    if (current != MEMORY_PRIORITY_BELOW_NORMAL) return 2;
    return set_memory_priority(target.value, MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL) &&
        memory_priority(target.value) == MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL ? 0 : 3;
}
class PriorityLease {
    HANDLE child_ = nullptr, cancel_ = nullptr;
public:
    DWORD pid = 0; std::uint64_t creation = 0, ws_before = 0, available_before = 0;
    double began = 0, baseline_faults = 0;
    bool feedback_done = false;
    ~PriorityLease() {
        if (cancel_) SetEvent(cancel_);
        if (child_) { WaitForSingleObject(child_, 5000); CloseHandle(child_); }
        if (cancel_) CloseHandle(cancel_);
    }
    PriorityLease() = default;
    PriorityLease(const PriorityLease&) = delete;
    PriorityLease& operator=(const PriorityLease&) = delete;
    bool begin(HANDLE target, unsigned seconds = 120) {
        SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES sa{sizeof(sa), nullptr, TRUE};
        Handle ready(CreateEventW(&sa, TRUE, FALSE, nullptr));
        cancel_ = CreateEventW(&sa, TRUE, FALSE, nullptr);
        HANDLE target_copy = nullptr, owner_copy = nullptr;
        if (!ready.value || !cancel_ ||
            !DuplicateHandle(GetCurrentProcess(), target, GetCurrentProcess(), &target_copy, 0, TRUE, DUPLICATE_SAME_ACCESS)) return false;
        Handle inherited_target(target_copy);
        if (!DuplicateHandle(GetCurrentProcess(), GetCurrentProcess(), GetCurrentProcess(), &owner_copy, SYNCHRONIZE, TRUE, 0)) return false;
        Handle inherited_owner(owner_copy);
        HANDLE inherited[]{inherited_target.value, inherited_owner.value, ready.value, cancel_};
        SIZE_T size = 0;
        InitializeProcThreadAttributeList(nullptr, 1, 0, &size);
        if (!size) return false;
        std::vector<unsigned char> attributes(size);
        auto list = reinterpret_cast<LPPROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_LIST>(attributes.data());
        if (!InitializeProcThreadAttributeList(list, 1, 0, &size)) return false;
        struct Cleanup { LPPROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_LIST p; ~Cleanup() { DeleteProcThreadAttributeList(p); } } cleanup{list};
        if (!UpdateProcThreadAttribute(list, 0, PROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_HANDLE_LIST, inherited, sizeof(inherited), nullptr, nullptr)) return false;
        std::wstring exe(32768, L'\0');
        DWORD count = GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, exe.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(exe.size()));
        if (!count || count >= exe.size()) return false;
        exe.resize(count);
        std::wstring command = L"\"" + exe + L"\" --priority-watch";
        for (const auto h : inherited) command += L" " + std::to_wstring(reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(h));
        command += L" " + std::to_wstring(seconds);
        STARTUPINFOEXW startup{}; startup.StartupInfo.cb = sizeof(startup); startup.lpAttributeList = list;
        PROCESS_INFORMATION child{};
        if (!CreateProcessW(exe.c_str(), command.data(), nullptr, nullptr, TRUE,
            EXTENDED_STARTUPINFO_PRESENT | CREATE_NO_WINDOW, nullptr, nullptr, &startup.StartupInfo, &child)) return false;
        child_ = child.hProcess; CloseHandle(child.hThread);
        HANDLE waits[]{ready.value, child_};
        return WaitForMultipleObjects(2, waits, FALSE, 5000) == WAIT_OBJECT_0;
    }
    bool finished() const { return child_ && WaitForSingleObject(child_, 0) == WAIT_OBJECT_0; }
    DWORD finish() {
        if (!child_) return 5;
        SetEvent(cancel_);
        if (WaitForSingleObject(child_, 5000) != WAIT_OBJECT_0) return 6;
        DWORD code = 3; GetExitCodeProcess(child_, &code); return code;
    }
};
class NativeController {
    std::set<std::wstring> allowed_;
    std::map<DWORD, std::unique_ptr<PriorityLease>> leases_;
    std::map<std::pair<DWORD, std::uint64_t>, double> attempts_;
    ame::NativePressure pressure_;
    std::ofstream& report_;
    bool disabled_ = false;
    DWORD session_ = 0;
    Handle mutex_{nullptr};
    void event(const char* type, DWORD pid, double now, const char* reason, DWORD result = 0) {
        report_ << "{\"type\":" << quote(type) << ",\"pid\":" << pid << ",\"elapsed_seconds\":" << now
            << ",\"reason\":" << quote(reason) << ",\"result_code\":" << result << "}\n";
        std::cout << "    " << type << " PID " << pid << ": " << reason << " (" << result << ")\n";
    }
public:
    NativeController(const std::vector<std::wstring>& allowed, std::ofstream& report) : report_(report) {
        mutex_.value = CreateMutexW(nullptr, FALSE, L"Local\\AME-MemoryPriority-Controller");
        const DWORD mutex_error = GetLastError();
        if (!mutex_.value || mutex_error == ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS) throw std::runtime_error("A memory-priority controller is already running in this desktop session");
        wchar_t windows[MAX_PATH]{};
        if (!GetWindowsDirectoryW(windows, MAX_PATH)) throw std::runtime_error("Windows directory unavailable");
        const auto system_path = lower_image(windows) + L"\\";
        for (const auto& image : allowed) {
            const auto normalized = lower_image(image);
            if (normalized.rfind(system_path, 0) == 0) throw std::runtime_error("Windows system images are excluded");
            allowed_.insert(normalized);
        }
        if (!ProcessIdToSessionId(GetCurrentProcessId(), &session_)) throw std::runtime_error("Own session unavailable");
    }
    ~NativeController() { shutdown(0); }
    void shutdown(double now) {
        for (auto& item : leases_) {
            DWORD code = item.second->finish();
            event("priority_finished", item.first, now, "controller_stop", code);
            if (code == 3 || code == 6) disabled_ = true;
        }
        leases_.clear();
    }
    bool disabled() const { return disabled_; }
    void update(const ame::SystemSample& system, const std::vector<Process>& rows, double now) {
        const bool pressure = pressure_.update(system, now);
        std::vector<ame::Identity> identities;
        bool foreground_known = false;
        for (const auto& p : rows) {
            const auto basename = lower_image(std::filesystem::path(p.image).filename().wstring());
            const bool launcher = basename == L"explorer.exe" || basename == L"windowsterminal.exe" ||
                basename == L"powershell.exe" || basename == L"pwsh.exe" || basename == L"cmd.exe" ||
                basename == L"conhost.exe" || !p.critical_known || p.critical;
            identities.push_back({p.pid, p.parent, p.creation, utf8(p.image.c_str()), p.foreground, launcher});
            if (p.foreground && !p.image.empty() && p.creation) foreground_known = true;
        }
        const auto protected_ids = ame::foreground_family(identities);
        for (auto it = leases_.begin(); it != leases_.end();) {
            auto& lease = *it->second;
            const Process* row = nullptr;
            for (const auto& p : rows) if (p.pid == lease.pid && p.creation == lease.creation) { row = &p; break; }
            const char* reason = nullptr;
            if (!row || !row->rate_known) reason = "identity_or_telemetry_lost";
            else if (!foreground_known || protected_ids.count(row->pid)) reason = "foreground_protected";
            else if (!pressure) reason = "pressure_recovered";
            else if (ame::native_regression(row->cpu_percent, row->faults_per_second, lease.baseline_faults)) reason = "activity_or_fault_guard";
            else if (lease.finished()) reason = "lease_expired";
            if (row && row->rate_known && !lease.feedback_done && now - lease.began >= 15) {
                report_ << "{\"type\":\"priority_feedback\",\"pid\":" << row->pid << ",\"elapsed_seconds\":" << now
                    << ",\"working_set_delta_bytes\":" << static_cast<std::int64_t>(row->ws) - static_cast<std::int64_t>(lease.ws_before)
                    << ",\"system_available_delta_bytes\":" << static_cast<std::int64_t>(system.available) - static_cast<std::int64_t>(lease.available_before)
                    << ",\"page_faults_per_second\":" << row->faults_per_second << ",\"causation_established\":false}\n";
                lease.feedback_done = true;
            }
            if (reason) {
                const auto code = lease.finish(); event("priority_finished", row ? row->pid : lease.pid, now, reason, code);
                if (code == 3 || code == 6) disabled_ = true;
                if (std::string(reason) == "activity_or_fault_guard") attempts_[{lease.pid, lease.creation}] = now + 86400;
                it = leases_.erase(it);
            } else ++it;
        }
        const auto write_state = [&]() {
            std::cout << "    Native: " << (pressure ? "PRESSURE" : "WAITING") << " | active leases " << leases_.size() << (disabled_ ? " | DISABLED" : "") << '\n';
            report_ << "{\"type\":\"native_state\",\"elapsed_seconds\":" << now << ",\"pressure\":" << (pressure ? "true" : "false")
                << ",\"active_leases\":" << leases_.size() << ",\"disabled\":" << (disabled_ ? "true" : "false") << "}\n";
        };
        if (disabled_ || !pressure || !foreground_known) { write_state(); return; }
        for (const auto& p : rows) {
            if (leases_.size() >= 3) break;
            if (leases_.count(p.pid) || !ame::native_candidate(pressure, allowed_.count(p.image) != 0,
                protected_ids.count(p.pid) != 0, p.rate_known && p.memory_known, p.critical_known, p.critical, p.low_activity_seconds, p.ws)) continue;
            const auto key = std::make_pair(p.pid, p.creation);
            const auto old = attempts_.find(key);
            if (old != attempts_.end() && now - old->second < 300) continue;
            attempts_[key] = now;
            Handle target(OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION | PROCESS_SET_INFORMATION | SYNCHRONIZE, FALSE, p.pid));
            if (!target.value) { event("priority_skipped", p.pid, now, "access_denied", GetLastError()); continue; }
            FILETIME created{}, exited{}, kernel{}, user{}; DWORD target_session = 0; BOOL critical = TRUE;
            if (!GetProcessTimes(target.value, &created, &exited, &kernel, &user) || ticks(created) != p.creation ||
                process_image(target.value) != p.image || !same_user(target.value) ||
                !ProcessIdToSessionId(p.pid, &target_session) || target_session != session_ ||
                !process_critical(target.value, &critical) || critical || memory_priority(target.value) != MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL) {
                event("priority_skipped", p.pid, now, "identity_owner_or_existing_policy"); continue;
            }
            // Recheck foreground immediately before launch, including same-image siblings.
            DWORD foreground = 0; GetWindowThreadProcessId(GetForegroundWindow(), &foreground);
            Handle foreground_handle(OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION, FALSE, foreground));
            if (!foreground_handle.value || foreground == p.pid || process_image(foreground_handle.value) == p.image) continue;
            auto latest_identities = identities;
            bool latest_known = false;
            for (auto& identity : latest_identities) {
                identity.foreground = identity.pid == foreground;
                if (identity.foreground && identity.creation && !identity.image.empty()) latest_known = true;
            }
            if (!latest_known || ame::foreground_family(latest_identities).count(p.pid)) continue;
            auto lease = std::make_unique<PriorityLease>();
            lease->pid = p.pid; lease->creation = p.creation; lease->began = now;
            lease->baseline_faults = p.faults_per_second; lease->ws_before = p.ws; lease->available_before = system.available;
            if (!lease->begin(target.value)) {
                const auto code = lease->finish(); event("priority_skipped", p.pid, now, "watchdog_not_ready", code);
                if (code == 3 || code == 6) disabled_ = true;
                continue;
            }
            event("priority_applied", p.pid, now, "memory_priority_5_to_4_lease_120s");
            leases_.emplace(p.pid, std::move(lease));
        }
        write_state();
    }
};

#pragma once
// Integration tests mutate only this test process, and use a dedicated child.
DWORD direct_parent() {
    Handle snapshot(CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS, 0));
    if (snapshot.value == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return 0;
    PROCESSENTRY32W entry{}; entry.dwSize = sizeof(entry);
    if (!Process32FirstW(snapshot.value, &entry)) return 0;
    do { if (entry.th32ProcessID == GetCurrentProcessId()) return entry.th32ParentProcessID; }
    while (Process32NextW(snapshot.value, &entry));
    return 0;
}
int test_owner_exit(int argc, wchar_t** argv) {
    if (argc != 4) return 1;
    const DWORD pid = static_cast<DWORD>(std::stoul(argv[2]));
    if (!pid || pid != direct_parent()) return 1;
    Handle parent(OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION | PROCESS_SET_INFORMATION | SYNCHRONIZE, FALSE, pid));
    FILETIME created{}, exited{}, kernel{}, user{};
    if (!parent.value || !same_user(parent.value) || process_image(parent.value) != process_image(GetCurrentProcess()) ||
        !GetProcessTimes(parent.value, &created, &exited, &kernel, &user) || ticks(created) != std::stoull(argv[3])) return 1;
    PriorityLease lease;
    if (!lease.begin(parent.value, 10)) return 1;
    // Intentionally bypass C++ cleanup in this dedicated test child only.
    ExitProcess(77);
    return 1;
}
int test_priority_lease() {
    const auto require = [](bool ok, const char* message) { if (!ok) throw std::runtime_error(message); };
    require(valid_selected_image(L"C:\\Program Files\\WindowsApps\\AME.Path.Fixture_1.0_x64\\app\\Claude.exe"),
        "Live image selection must not depend on filesystem access");
    require(!valid_selected_image(L"Claude.exe") && !valid_selected_image(L"C:\\apps\\data.txt"),
        "Image selection must reject relative and non-executable paths");
    require(memory_priority(GetCurrentProcess()) == MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL, "Test process needs default memory priority");
    struct Restore { ~Restore() { set_memory_priority(GetCurrentProcess(), MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL); } } restore;
    {
        PriorityLease lease;
        require(lease.begin(GetCurrentProcess(), 10), "Watchdog did not become ready");
        require(memory_priority(GetCurrentProcess()) == MEMORY_PRIORITY_BELOW_NORMAL, "Priority not applied");
        require(lease.finish() == 0 && memory_priority(GetCurrentProcess()) == MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL, "Normal rollback failed");
    }
    {
        PriorityLease lease;
        require(lease.begin(GetCurrentProcess(), 1), "Expiry test could not start");
        const auto deadline = Clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(5);
        while (!lease.finished() && Clock::now() < deadline) Sleep(20);
        require(lease.finished() && lease.finish() == 0 && memory_priority(GetCurrentProcess()) == MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL, "Bounded lease rollback failed");
    }
    {
        PriorityLease lease;
        require(lease.begin(GetCurrentProcess(), 10), "Application override test could not start");
        require(set_memory_priority(GetCurrentProcess(), MEMORY_PRIORITY_MEDIUM), "Test override failed");
        require(lease.finish() == 2 && memory_priority(GetCurrentProcess()) == MEMORY_PRIORITY_MEDIUM, "Application override was overwritten");
        require(set_memory_priority(GetCurrentProcess(), MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL), "Test override cleanup failed");
    }
    FILETIME created{}, exited{}, kernel{}, user{};
    require(GetProcessTimes(GetCurrentProcess(), &created, &exited, &kernel, &user) != FALSE, "Test identity unavailable");
    std::wstring exe(32768, L'\0');
    const DWORD count = GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, exe.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(exe.size()));
    require(count && count < exe.size(), "Test executable path unavailable"); exe.resize(count);
    std::wstring command = L"\"" + exe + L"\" --test-owner-exit " + std::to_wstring(GetCurrentProcessId()) + L" " + std::to_wstring(ticks(created));
    STARTUPINFOW startup{}; startup.cb = sizeof(startup); PROCESS_INFORMATION child{};
    require(CreateProcessW(exe.c_str(), command.data(), nullptr, nullptr, FALSE, CREATE_NO_WINDOW, nullptr, nullptr, &startup, &child) != FALSE, "Crash fixture launch failed");
    Handle child_process(child.hProcess), child_thread(child.hThread);
    require(WaitForSingleObject(child_process.value, 10000) == WAIT_OBJECT_0, "Crash fixture timed out");
    DWORD result = 0; require(GetExitCodeProcess(child_process.value, &result) && result == 77, "Crash fixture did not apply lease");
    const auto deadline = Clock::now() + std::chrono::seconds(5);
    while (memory_priority(GetCurrentProcess()) != MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL && Clock::now() < deadline) Sleep(20);
    require(memory_priority(GetCurrentProcess()) == MEMORY_PRIORITY_NORMAL, "Owner-death rollback failed");
    std::cout << "PASS Windows priority apply, rollback, expiry, app override and owner-death recovery\n";
    return 0;
}

#pragma once
#include "policy.hpp"
#include <sstream>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace ame {
inline AgentStatus parse_agent_reply(const std::string& line, std::uint64_t budget = 64 * MiB) {
    if (line.size() > 512) throw std::runtime_error("Agent reply too large");
    // One exact versioned line: AME1 OK pid cache_bytes released_bytes latency_ms.
    // Numeric fields allow neither sign nor exponent. No JSON dependency.
    std::istringstream input(line);
    std::string version, result, pid, cache, released, latency, extra;
    if (!(input >> version >> result >> pid >> cache >> released >> latency) || input >> extra || version != "AME1" || result != "OK")
        throw std::runtime_error("Invalid agent reply");
    auto number = [](const std::string& value) -> std::uint64_t {
        if (value.empty() || value.find_first_not_of("0123456789") != std::string::npos) throw std::runtime_error("Invalid integer");
        std::size_t used = 0; auto n = std::stoull(value, &used);
        if (used != value.size()) throw std::runtime_error("Invalid integer");
        return n;
    };
    auto id = number(pid);
    if (id == 0 || id > 0xffffffffULL) throw std::runtime_error("Invalid PID");
    if (latency.empty() || latency.find_first_not_of("0123456789.") != std::string::npos) throw std::runtime_error("Invalid latency");
    std::size_t used = 0; const auto ms = std::stod(latency, &used);
    if (used != latency.size() || !std::isfinite(ms) || ms < 0) throw std::runtime_error("Invalid latency");
    AgentStatus s{static_cast<std::uint32_t>(id), number(cache), number(released), ms};
    if (s.cache_bytes > 1024ULL * 1024 * MiB || s.released_bytes > budget) throw std::runtime_error("Invalid agent counters");
    return s;
}
}

bool read_memory(HANDLE h, CountersEx2& counters) {
    counters={}; counters.base.cb=sizeof(counters);
    return GetProcessMemoryInfo(h,reinterpret_cast<PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS*>(&counters),sizeof(counters)) &&
        counters.private_ws<=counters.base.WorkingSetSize;
}
class RecoverController {
    std::set<std::wstring> allowed_;
    std::set<std::pair<std::uint32_t,std::uint64_t>> selected_;

        const auto peak=std::max(p.benefit.best_drop(),remaining);

            <<",\"working_set_delta_bytes\":"<<static_cast<std::int64_t>(row.ws)-static_cast<std::int64_t>(p.ws)
            <<",\"private_commit_delta_bytes\":"<<static_cast<std::int64_t>(row.commit)-static_cast<std::int64_t>(p.commit)
            <<",\"system_available_delta_bytes\":"<<static_cast<std::int64_t>(system.available)-static_cast<std::int64_t>(p.available)

    void update(const ame::SystemSample& system,const std::vector<Process>& rows,double now,bool actions_open=true) {
        const bool pressure=pressure_.update(system,now);
        std::vector<ame::Identity> identities;
        bool foreground_known=false;
        for(const auto& row:rows) {
            const auto base=lower_image(std::filesystem::path(row.image).filename().wstring());
            const bool launcher=base==L"explorer.exe"||base==L"windowsterminal.exe"||base==L"powershell.exe"||base==L"pwsh.exe"||base==L"cmd.exe"||base==L"conhost.exe"||!row.critical_known||row.critical;

            if(pending_)return "feedback_pending";

            if(!actions_open)return "action_window_closed";

                row.rate_known&&row.memory_known&&system.valid(),row.own_user,row.session==session_,row.critical_known,row.critical,
                row.low_activity_seconds,row.ws,row.private_ws_known?std::optional<std::uint64_t>(row.private_ws):std::nullopt,single_test_);
            if(std::string(reason)=="eligible"&&!gate_.allowed(row.pid,row.creation,now,false))return "session_limit_or_already_attempted";
            return reason;
        };
        if(pending_) {
            const auto p=*pending_; const Process* row=nullptr;
            for(const auto& r:rows)if(r.pid==p.pid&&r.creation==p.creation) { row=&r; break; }
            const bool valid=row&&row->rate_known&&row->memory_known&&row->private_ws_known&&system.valid()&&foreground_known;
            const char* guard=nullptr;
            if(!valid)guard="feedback_telemetry_or_identity_lost";

            if(!guard) {

                    // continue so that resuming the app cannot hide an immediate refill.

                // FaultCount combines soft and hard faults. Stop mutations immediately,
                // but retain the read-only observation; do not assert a performance gain.
                pending_->activity_warning=true;disable("activity_or_fault_increase_stop_further_actions");
                feedback(*row,system,now,"activity_warning");
                event("recover_activity_warning",p.pid,now,"activity_or_fault_increase_stop_further_actions");
            }
            if(guard) {
                if(valid)feedback(*row,system,now,"guard");

            } else if(early) {

                disable(early); assessed(*row,now,early); pending_.reset();
            } else {

                if((elapsed>=5&&!pending_->at5)||(elapsed>=15&&!pending_->at15)||

                        // establish nor invalidate this process's observed resident reduction.

            if(!allowed_.count(row.image)||!selected_.count({row.pid,row.creation}))continue;

            gate_.attempt(row.pid,row.creation,now);

                event("recover_skipped",row.pid,now,"resident_memory_changed"); break;
            }
            // Fresh activity counters reduce races with a workload restarting.
            const auto current_cpu=ticks(kernel)+ticks(user);
            if(current_cpu<row.cpu_ticks||current_cpu-row.cpu_ticks>100000||
                static_cast<DWORD>(before.base.PageFaultCount-row.faults)>10) {
                event("recover_skipped",row.pid,now,"activity_changed_since_sample"); break;

            Handle fg(OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION,FALSE,foreground));
            bool latest_known=false;
            for(auto& id:identities) { id.foreground=id.pid==foreground; if(id.foreground&&id.creation&&!id.image.empty())latest_known=true; }
            if(!fg.value||!latest_known||process_image(fg.value)==row.image||ame::foreground_family(identities).count(row.pid)) {
                event("recover_skipped",row.pid,now,"foreground_changed"); break;
            }
            const auto before_system=sample_system();
            if(!before_system.valid()||(!single_test_&&before_system.used_percent()<85)) { event("recover_skipped",row.pid,now,"pressure_changed"); break; }
            // Persist the intent before the irreversible resident-set change.
            event("recover_intent",row.pid,now,"single_process_empty_working_set"); report_.flush();
            if(!report_)throw std::runtime_error("Cannot record recovery intent");
            if(!EmptyWorkingSet(target.value)) { event("recover_skipped",row.pid,now,"empty_working_set_failed",GetLastError()); break; }

            event("recover_applied",row.pid,now,"resident_pages_trimmed_allocations_not_freed");

        report_<<"}}\n";
    }

#pragma once
#include "native_policy.hpp"
#include <map>
#include <deque>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace ame {
inline std::pair<std::uint32_t,std::uint64_t> selected_identity(const std::string& text) {
    const auto colon=text.find(':');
    if(colon==std::string::npos||text.find(':',colon+1)!=std::string::npos)throw std::runtime_error("allow-process requires PID:creation_filetime");
    const auto pid=text.substr(0,colon),created=text.substr(colon+1);
    if(pid.empty()||created.empty()||pid.find_first_not_of("0123456789")!=std::string::npos||created.find_first_not_of("0123456789")!=std::string::npos)throw std::runtime_error("Invalid process identity");
    const auto p=std::stoull(pid),c=std::stoull(created);
    if(p==0||p>0xffffffffULL||c==0)throw std::runtime_error("Invalid process identity range");
    return {static_cast<std::uint32_t>(p),c};
}
class RecoverPressure {
    std::optional<double> since_, previous_;
    bool active_ = false;
public:
    bool update(const SystemSample& s, double now) {
        if (!s.valid() || !std::isfinite(now) || (previous_ && (now < *previous_ || now-*previous_>6))) {
            since_.reset(); previous_.reset(); return active_=false;
        }
        previous_=now;
        if(active_) { if(s.used_percent()<80) { active_=false; since_.reset(); } return active_; }
        if(s.used_percent()<85) { since_.reset(); return false; }
        if(!since_)since_=now;
        return active_=now-*since_>=30;
    }
};
inline const char* recover_reason(bool pressure,bool allowed,bool protected_family,bool known,
    bool same_owner,bool same_session,bool critical_known,bool critical,double idle,
    std::uint64_t ws,std::optional<std::uint64_t> private_ws,bool single_test=false) {
    if(!allowed)return "not_selected";
    if(!same_owner||!same_session)return "owner_or_session";
    if(!critical_known||critical)return "critical_or_unknown";
    if(protected_family)return "foreground_family";
    if(!known||!std::isfinite(idle)||!private_ws||*private_ws>ws)return "telemetry_missing";
    if(ws>2ULL*1024*MiB)return "target_over_2_GiB";
    if(*private_ws<128*MiB)return "private_resident_below_128_MiB";
    if(idle<60)return "low_activity_under_60_seconds";
    if(!pressure&&!single_test)return "waiting_pressure_85_percent_30_seconds";
    return "eligible";
}
inline bool recover_activity(double cpu,double faults,double baseline) {
    return !std::isfinite(cpu)||!std::isfinite(faults)||!std::isfinite(baseline)||
        cpu>=0.5||faults>std::max(100.0,5*baseline);
}
// An expired action window may only finish an already-started measurement.
constexpr double recover_observation_seconds=300;
inline bool recover_observation_allowed(double now,double deadline,bool pending) {
    return std::isfinite(now)&&std::isfinite(deadline)&&now>=0&&deadline>0&&
        (now<deadline||(pending&&now<deadline+recover_observation_seconds+6));
}
// Conservative observations, not a prediction of the application's future needs.
class RecoverBenefit {
    std::uint64_t before_, best_=0, remaining_=0;
    double began_;
    std::optional<double> previous_, rebound_since_;
    std::deque<std::pair<double,std::uint64_t>> recent_;
public:
    RecoverBenefit(std::uint64_t before,double began):before_(before),began_(began) {}
    bool update(std::uint64_t after,double now) {
        if(!std::isfinite(now)||now<began_||(previous_&&(now<*previous_||now-*previous_>6)))return false;
        previous_=now;
        remaining_=before_>after?before_-after:0;
        best_=std::max(best_,remaining_);
        if(!recent_.empty()&&recent_.back().first==now)recent_.back().second=remaining_;
        else recent_.push_back({now,remaining_});
        // Keep one boundary sample and at most 128 entries, independent of sample rate.
        while(recent_.size()>1&&recent_[1].first<=now-60)recent_.pop_front();
        while(recent_.size()>128)recent_.pop_front();
        if(now-began_>=5&&best_>=16*MiB&&remaining_<=best_/5) {
            if(!rebound_since_)rebound_since_=now;
        } else rebound_since_.reset();
        return true;
    }
    const char* early_reason(double now) const {
        if(!std::isfinite(now)||!previous_||now!=*previous_)return nullptr;
        if(now-began_>=5&&best_<16*MiB)return "no_meaningful_resident_reduction";
        if(rebound_since_&&now-*rebound_since_>=4)return "rapid_resident_rebound";
        return nullptr;
    }
    bool retained() const { return remaining_>=16*MiB&&remaining_>best_/5; }
    std::uint64_t minimum_recent_drop() const {
        if(recent_.empty())return 0;
        auto minimum=recent_.front().second;
        for(const auto& point:recent_)minimum=std::min(minimum,point.second);
        return minimum;
    }
    bool retained_last_minute() const {
        return previous_&&*previous_-began_>=recover_observation_seconds&&!recent_.empty()&&
            recent_.front().first<=*previous_-60&&minimum_recent_drop()>=16*MiB&&minimum_recent_drop()>best_/5;
    }
    std::uint64_t best_drop() const { return best_; }
    std::uint64_t remaining_drop() const { return remaining_; }
    double retained_percent() const { return best_?100.0*static_cast<double>(remaining_)/static_cast<double>(best_):0; }
};
class RecoverGate {
    std::set<std::pair<std::uint32_t,std::uint64_t>> attempts_;
    std::optional<double> last_;
    unsigned successes_=0;
    bool disabled_=false;
    unsigned maximum_=3;
public:
    explicit RecoverGate(unsigned maximum=3):maximum_(maximum) {
        if(maximum<1||maximum>3)throw std::runtime_error("Invalid recovery action limit");
    }
    bool allowed(std::uint32_t pid,std::uint64_t creation,double now,bool pending) const {
        return !disabled_ && !pending && successes_<maximum_ && pid!=0 && creation!=0 && std::isfinite(now) &&
            (!last_ || (now>=*last_ && now-*last_>=60)) && !attempts_.count({pid,creation});
    }
    void attempt(std::uint32_t pid,std::uint64_t creation,double now) { attempts_.insert({pid,creation}); last_=now; }
    void succeeded() { ++successes_; }
    void disable() { disabled_=true; }
    bool disabled() const { return disabled_; }
    unsigned count() const { return successes_; }
    bool attempted() const { return !attempts_.empty(); }
};
}

#pragma once
// Only our own test allocation and process are touched.
int test_recovery() {
    constexpr SIZE_T bytes=64*1024*1024;
    auto memory=static_cast<unsigned char*>(VirtualAlloc(nullptr,bytes,MEM_RESERVE|MEM_COMMIT,PAGE_READWRITE));
    if(!memory)throw std::runtime_error("Recovery fixture allocation failed");
    struct Free { void* p; ~Free() { VirtualFree(p,0,MEM_RELEASE); } } release{memory};
    for(SIZE_T i=0;i<bytes;i+=4096)memory[i]=static_cast<unsigned char>((i/4096)%251);
    CountersEx2 before{},after{};
    if(!read_memory(GetCurrentProcess(),before))throw std::runtime_error("Recovery fixture memory unavailable");
    if(!EmptyWorkingSet(GetCurrentProcess()))throw std::runtime_error("EmptyWorkingSet failed on own fixture");
    if(!read_memory(GetCurrentProcess(),after))throw std::runtime_error("Recovery fixture after read failed");
    // Commit and data must remain: lower residency must never mean discarded data.
    if(after.base.PrivateUsage+ame::MiB<before.base.PrivateUsage)throw std::runtime_error("Unexpected fixture commit decrease");
    if(after.private_ws>=before.private_ws)throw std::runtime_error("Own fixture did not reduce resident memory");
    for(SIZE_T i=0;i<bytes;i+=4096)if(memory[i]!=static_cast<unsigned char>((i/4096)%251))throw std::runtime_error("Fixture data changed");
    if(GetCurrentProcessId()==0)throw std::runtime_error("Fixture process identity lost");
    std::cout<<"PASS own-process resident trim, private residency reduction, data preserved, allocation retained\n";
    return 0;
}
