using System;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.IO;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;

namespace AmeHub {
    // Read-only bridge. No Job Object association or memory mutation is exposed.
    public sealed class ResourceIdentity {
        public uint Pid;
        public ulong Creation;
        public string Path;
    }
    public sealed class ResourceApiClient : IDisposable {
        internal const int IdentitySize = 65552;
        [UnmanagedFunctionPointer(CallingConvention.Cdecl)]
        internal delegate uint Getter();
        [UnmanagedFunctionPointer(CallingConvention.Cdecl)]
        internal delegate int ReadIdentity(uint pid, IntPtr identity);
        [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Unicode, SetLastError=true)]
        private static extern IntPtr LoadLibraryExW(string path, IntPtr reserved, uint flags);
        [DllImport("kernel32.dll", CharSet=CharSet.Ansi, ExactSpelling=true, SetLastError=true)]
        private static extern IntPtr GetProcAddress(IntPtr module, string name);
        [DllImport("kernel32.dll", SetLastError=true)]
        private static extern bool FreeLibrary(IntPtr module);
        private IntPtr module;
        private Getter version, error;
        private ReadIdentity read;
        private bool disposed;
        private readonly object sync = new object();
        public uint ApiVersion { get; private set; }
        public int SuccessfulReads { get; private set; }
        public string LibraryPath { get; private set; }

        public ResourceApiClient(string path) {
            if (IntPtr.Size != 8) throw new NotSupportedException("API AME: serve un processo x64.");
            if (!System.IO.Path.IsPathRooted(path)) throw new ArgumentException("Percorso DLL assoluto richiesto.");
            LibraryPath = System.IO.Path.GetFullPath(path);
            // Search dependencies only beside this DLL and in default safe directories.
            module = LoadLibraryExW(LibraryPath, IntPtr.Zero, 0x100u | 0x1000u);
            if (module == IntPtr.Zero) throw new Win32Exception(Marshal.GetLastWin32Error(), "Caricamento API AME fallito.");
            try {
                version = (Getter)Resolve("AmeGetApiVersion", typeof(Getter));
                error = (Getter)Resolve("AmeGetLastWindowsError", typeof(Getter));
                read = (ReadIdentity)Resolve("AmeReadProcessIdentity", typeof(ReadIdentity));
                CheckVersion();
            } catch { Dispose(); throw; }
        }
        // Injection is internal and used only by the managed contract tests.
        internal ResourceApiClient(Getter v, Getter e, ReadIdentity r) {
            version=v; error=e; read=r; CheckVersion();
        }
        private Delegate Resolve(string name, Type type) {
            IntPtr address=GetProcAddress(module, name);
            if(address==IntPtr.Zero) throw new EntryPointNotFoundException(name);
            return Marshal.GetDelegateForFunctionPointer(address,type);
        }
        private void CheckVersion() {
            ApiVersion=version();
            if(ApiVersion!=1) throw new NotSupportedException("Versione API AME incompatibile: "+ApiVersion);
        }
        public ResourceIdentity Read(uint pid) {
            lock(sync) {
                if(disposed) throw new ObjectDisposedException("ResourceApiClient");
                if(pid==0) throw new ArgumentException("PID non valido.");
                IntPtr buffer=Marshal.AllocHGlobal(IdentitySize);
                try {
                    Marshal.Copy(new byte[IdentitySize],0,buffer,IdentitySize);
                    Marshal.WriteInt32(buffer,IdentitySize);
                    int status=read(pid,buffer);
                    uint winError=error(); // Same thread, immediately after the API operation.
                    if(status!=0) throw new InvalidOperationException("Identita PID "+pid+" non verificabile: API "+status+", Windows "+winError+".");
                    uint actualPid=unchecked((uint)Marshal.ReadInt32(buffer,4));
                    ulong creation=unchecked((ulong)Marshal.ReadInt64(buffer,8));
                    string bounded=Marshal.PtrToStringUni(IntPtr.Add(buffer,16),32768);
                    int end=bounded.IndexOf('\0');
                    if(Marshal.ReadInt32(buffer)!=IdentitySize || actualPid!=pid || creation==0 || end<=0)
                        throw new InvalidDataException("Risposta identita API non valida.");
                    SuccessfulReads++;
                    return new ResourceIdentity { Pid=actualPid, Creation=creation, Path=bounded.Substring(0,end) };
                } finally { Marshal.FreeHGlobal(buffer); }
            }
        }
        public void Validate(string key, string expectedPath) {
            uint pid; ulong creation;
            string[] parts=(key??"").Split(':');
            if(parts.Length!=2 || !uint.TryParse(parts[0],out pid) || !ulong.TryParse(parts[1],out creation) ||
               pid==0 || creation==0 || String.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(expectedPath))
                throw new ArgumentException("Identita selezionata incompleta.");
            ResourceIdentity actual=Read(pid);
            if(actual.Creation!=creation || !String.Equals(actual.Path,expectedPath,StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
                throw new InvalidOperationException("Processo cambiato (PID "+pid+"): aggiorna la selezione. Nessun intervento avviato.");
        }
        public void Dispose() {
            lock(sync) {
                if(disposed) return;
                disposed=true;
                if(module!=IntPtr.Zero) { FreeLibrary(module); module=IntPtr.Zero; }
                version=null; error=null; read=null;
            }
        }
    }
}

#pragma once
#include "../sdk/ame_resource_api.h"
#include <limits>

namespace ame::resource {
// Used by the actual DLL. No rates across missing samples or membership changes.
inline void calculate_rates(const AmeGroupSnapshot* previous, AmeGroupSnapshot& now,
                            uint32_t processors) {
    now.flags &= ~static_cast<uint32_t>(AME_RATES_VALID);
    now.rate_interval_ms = 0;
    now.cpu_percent_machine = 0;
    now.io_read_bytes_per_second = 0;
    now.io_write_bytes_per_second = 0;
    now.page_faults_per_second = 0;
    now.private_commit_change_bytes = 0;
    if (!previous || !processors || !(previous->flags & AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE) ||
        !(now.flags & AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE) || now.membership_revision != previous->membership_revision ||
        now.active_processes != previous->active_processes || now.monotonic_ms <= previous->monotonic_ms)
        return;
    const auto dt = now.monotonic_ms - previous->monotonic_ms;
    if (dt < 1000 || dt > 6000 || now.cpu_time_100ns < previous->cpu_time_100ns ||
        now.io_read_bytes < previous->io_read_bytes || now.io_write_bytes < previous->io_write_bytes ||
        now.page_faults_total < previous->page_faults_total)
        return;
    const auto change = now.private_commit_sum_bytes >= previous->private_commit_sum_bytes
        ? now.private_commit_sum_bytes - previous->private_commit_sum_bytes
        : previous->private_commit_sum_bytes - now.private_commit_sum_bytes;
    if (change > static_cast<uint64_t>(std::numeric_limits<int64_t>::max())) return;
    const double seconds = static_cast<double>(dt) / 1000.0;
    const double cpu = static_cast<double>(now.cpu_time_100ns - previous->cpu_time_100ns) /
                       1e7 / seconds / processors * 100.0;
    if (cpu > 100.0) return;
    now.cpu_percent_machine = cpu;
    now.io_read_bytes_per_second = static_cast<double>(now.io_read_bytes - previous->io_read_bytes) / seconds;
    now.io_write_bytes_per_second = static_cast<double>(now.io_write_bytes - previous->io_write_bytes) / seconds;
    now.page_faults_per_second = static_cast<double>(now.page_faults_total - previous->page_faults_total) / seconds;
    now.private_commit_change_bytes = now.private_commit_sum_bytes >= previous->private_commit_sum_bytes
        ? static_cast<int64_t>(change) : -static_cast<int64_t>(change);
    now.rate_interval_ms = dt;
    now.flags |= AME_RATES_VALID;
}
}
=== FILE: D:\.A2\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0-R22-FIX1\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0\src\resource_api.cpp ===
#include <windows.h>
#include <psapi.h>
#include "../sdk/ame_resource_api.h"
#include "resource_analysis.hpp"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cwchar>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <map>
#include <memory>
#include <mutex>
#include <new>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

static_assert(sizeof(void*) == 8 && sizeof(wchar_t) == 2, "Windows x64 ABI required");
static_assert(sizeof(AmeProcessIdentity) == 65552, "Identity ABI layout");
static_assert(sizeof(AmeGroupSnapshot) == 144, "Snapshot ABI layout");
static_assert(sizeof(AmeGroupEvent) == 48, "Event ABI layout");

namespace {
thread_local DWORD last_error = ERROR_SUCCESS;
AmeResult result(AmeResult code, DWORD error = ERROR_SUCCESS) { last_error = error; return code; }
struct Handle {
    HANDLE value = nullptr;
    explicit Handle(HANDLE h = nullptr) : value(h) {}
    ~Handle() { if (value && value != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) CloseHandle(value); }
    Handle(const Handle&) = delete;
    Handle& operator=(const Handle&) = delete;
    Handle(Handle&& other) noexcept : value(other.value) { other.value = nullptr; }
    Handle& operator=(Handle&& other) noexcept {
        if (this != &other) {
            if (value && value != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) CloseHandle(value);
            value = other.value; other.value = nullptr;
        }
        return *this;
    }
};
struct Group {
    Handle job, port;
    std::vector<Handle> members;
    uint64_t notice = 0, membership = 0, policy = 0;
    AmeGroupSnapshot previous{};
    bool has_previous = false;
};
// All calls are short/nonblocking and serialized. No worker thread or callback.
std::mutex registry_mutex;
std::map<AmeGroup, std::unique_ptr<Group>> groups;
AmeGroup next_id = 1;

template<class F> AmeResult boundary(F&& operation) noexcept {
    try { std::lock_guard<std::mutex> lock(registry_mutex); return operation(); }
    catch (const std::bad_alloc&) { return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, ERROR_NOT_ENOUGH_MEMORY); }
    catch (...) { return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, ERROR_GEN_FAILURE); }
}
uint64_t ticks(const FILETIME& f) {
    return (static_cast<uint64_t>(f.dwHighDateTime) << 32u) | f.dwLowDateTime;
}
AmeResult read_identity(HANDLE process, DWORD pid, AmeProcessIdentity& identity) {
    FILETIME creation{}, exit{}, kernel{}, user{};
    if (!GetProcessTimes(process, &creation, &exit, &kernel, &user))
        return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
    std::vector<wchar_t> path(AME_RESOURCE_PATH_CAPACITY);
    DWORD length = static_cast<DWORD>(path.size());
    if (!QueryFullProcessImageNameW(process, 0, path.data(), &length))
        return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
    if (!length || length >= AME_RESOURCE_PATH_CAPACITY) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, ERROR_BUFFER_OVERFLOW);
    identity = {};
    identity.size = sizeof(identity); identity.pid = pid; identity.creation_filetime = ticks(creation);
    std::copy_n(path.data(), length, identity.image_path);
    return result(AME_OK);
}
bool valid_identity(const AmeProcessIdentity* identity) {
    return identity && identity->size == sizeof(*identity) && identity->pid && identity->creation_filetime &&
        identity->image_path[0] && std::wmemchr(identity->image_path, L'\0', AME_RESOURCE_PATH_CAPACITY);
}
bool same_path(const wchar_t* a, const wchar_t* b) {
    return CompareStringOrdinal(a, -1, b, -1, TRUE) == CSTR_EQUAL;
}
AmeResult owner_and_session(HANDLE process, DWORD pid) {
    DWORD own_session = 0, target_session = 0;
    if (!ProcessIdToSessionId(GetCurrentProcessId(), &own_session) || !ProcessIdToSessionId(pid, &target_session))
        return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
    if (own_session != target_session) return result(AME_PROCESS_EXCLUDED);
    HANDLE own_raw = nullptr, target_raw = nullptr;
    if (!OpenProcessToken(GetCurrentProcess(), TOKEN_QUERY, &own_raw)) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
    Handle own(own_raw);
    if (!OpenProcessToken(process, TOKEN_QUERY, &target_raw)) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
    Handle target(target_raw);
    DWORD own_size = 0, target_size = 0;
    GetTokenInformation(own.value, TokenUser, nullptr, 0, &own_size);
    GetTokenInformation(target.value, TokenUser, nullptr, 0, &target_size);
    if (!own_size || !target_size || own_size > 65536 || target_size > 65536)
        return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, ERROR_INVALID_DATA);
    std::vector<unsigned char> own_data(own_size), target_data(target_size);
    if (!GetTokenInformation(own.value, TokenUser, own_data.data(), own_size, &own_size) ||
        !GetTokenInformation(target.value, TokenUser, target_data.data(), target_size, &target_size))
        return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
    if (!EqualSid(reinterpret_cast<TOKEN_USER*>(own_data.data())->User.Sid,
                  reinterpret_cast<TOKEN_USER*>(target_data.data())->User.Sid)) return result(AME_PROCESS_EXCLUDED);
    return result(AME_OK);
}
AmeResult check_process(HANDLE process, const AmeProcessIdentity& identity) {
    if (identity.pid <= 4 || identity.pid == GetCurrentProcessId()) return result(AME_PROCESS_EXCLUDED);
    const DWORD wait = WaitForSingleObject(process, 0);
    if (wait == WAIT_OBJECT_0) return result(AME_IDENTITY_CHANGED);
    if (wait != WAIT_TIMEOUT) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
    const auto ownership = owner_and_session(process, identity.pid);
    if (ownership != AME_OK) return ownership;
    BOOL critical = FALSE;
    using CriticalFunction = BOOL(WINAPI*)(HANDLE, PBOOL);
    const auto symbol = GetProcAddress(GetModuleHandleW(L"kernel32.dll"), "IsProcessCritical");
    CriticalFunction is_critical = nullptr;
    static_assert(sizeof(is_critical) == sizeof(symbol), "Windows function pointer size");
    std::memcpy(&is_critical, &symbol, sizeof(symbol));
    if (!is_critical) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, ERROR_PROC_NOT_FOUND);
    if (!is_critical(process, &critical)) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
    if (critical) return result(AME_PROCESS_EXCLUDED);
    wchar_t windows[MAX_PATH]{};
    const UINT length = GetWindowsDirectoryW(windows, MAX_PATH);
    if (!length || length >= MAX_PATH) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, ERROR_INVALID_DATA);
    const std::wstring prefix = std::wstring(windows) + L"\\";
    const std::size_t path_length = std::wcslen(identity.image_path);
    if (path_length >= prefix.size() &&
        CompareStringOrdinal(identity.image_path, static_cast<int>(prefix.size()), prefix.data(),
                             static_cast<int>(prefix.size()), TRUE) == CSTR_EQUAL)
        return result(AME_PROCESS_EXCLUDED);
    return result(AME_OK);
}
Group* find_group(AmeGroup id) {
    const auto found = groups.find(id);
    return found == groups.end() ? nullptr : found->second.get();
}
bool add(uint64_t& sum, uint64_t value) {
    if (value > std::numeric_limits<uint64_t>::max() - sum) return false;
    sum += value; return true;
}
AmeResult set_notice(Group& group, uint64_t bytes) {
    JOBOBJECT_NOTIFICATION_LIMIT_INFORMATION notice{};
    if (bytes) { notice.LimitFlags = JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_JOB_MEMORY; notice.JobMemoryLimit = bytes; }
    if (!SetInformationJobObject(group.job.value, JobObjectNotificationLimitInformation, &notice, sizeof(notice)))
        return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
    group.notice = bytes; ++group.policy;
    return result(AME_OK);
}
}

uint32_t AME_CALL AmeGetApiVersion(void) { return AME_RESOURCE_ABI_VERSION; }
uint32_t AME_CALL AmeGetLastWindowsError(void) { return last_error; }

AmeResult AME_CALL AmeReadProcessIdentity(uint32_t pid, AmeProcessIdentity* identity) {
    return boundary([&] {
        if (!pid || !identity || identity->size != sizeof(*identity)) return result(AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT);
        *identity = {}; identity->size = sizeof(*identity);
        Handle process(OpenProcess(PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION | SYNCHRONIZE, FALSE, pid));
        if (!process.value) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
        const DWORD wait = WaitForSingleObject(process.value, 0);
        if (wait == WAIT_OBJECT_0) return result(AME_IDENTITY_CHANGED);
        if (wait != WAIT_TIMEOUT) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
        return read_identity(process.value, pid, *identity);
    });
}
AmeResult AME_CALL AmeCreateGroup(AmeGroup* output) {
    return boundary([&] {
        if (!output) return result(AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT);
        *output = 0;
        if (groups.size() >= AME_RESOURCE_MAX_GROUPS || next_id == std::numeric_limits<AmeGroup>::max())
            return result(AME_CAPACITY_REACHED);
        auto group = std::make_unique<Group>();
        group->members.reserve(AME_RESOURCE_MAX_MEMBERS);
        group->job = Handle(CreateJobObjectW(nullptr, nullptr));
        if (!group->job.value) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
        // Only explicitly attached identities belong to this first API version.
        // Do not inherit membership into newly created descendants.
        JOBOBJECT_EXTENDED_LIMIT_INFORMATION limits{};
        limits.BasicLimitInformation.LimitFlags = JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_SILENT_BREAKAWAY_OK;
        if (!SetInformationJobObject(group->job.value, JobObjectExtendedLimitInformation, &limits, sizeof(limits)))
            return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
        group->port = Handle(CreateIoCompletionPort(INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE, nullptr, 0, 1));
        if (!group->port.value) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
        JOBOBJECT_ASSOCIATE_COMPLETION_PORT port{};
        port.CompletionPort = group->port.value;
        if (!SetInformationJobObject(group->job.value, JobObjectAssociateCompletionPortInformation, &port, sizeof(port)))
            return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
        const auto id = next_id++;
        groups.emplace(id, std::move(group));
        *output = id; return result(AME_OK);
    });
}
AmeResult AME_CALL AmeAttachProcess(AmeGroup id, const AmeProcessIdentity* identity) {
    return boundary([&] {
        if (!valid_identity(identity)) return result(AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT);
        auto* group = find_group(id);
        if (!group) return result(AME_INVALID_GROUP);
        if (group->members.size() >= AME_RESOURCE_MAX_MEMBERS) return result(AME_CAPACITY_REACHED);
        // AssignProcessToJobObject requires SET_QUOTA and TERMINATE access.
        // TERMINATE is never invoked by this library.
        Handle process(OpenProcess(PROCESS_SET_QUOTA | PROCESS_TERMINATE | PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION |
                                   PROCESS_VM_READ | SYNCHRONIZE, FALSE, identity->pid));
        if (!process.value) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
        AmeProcessIdentity actual{};
        auto status = read_identity(process.value, identity->pid, actual);
        if (status != AME_OK) return status;
        if (actual.creation_filetime != identity->creation_filetime || !same_path(actual.image_path, identity->image_path))
            return result(AME_IDENTITY_CHANGED);
        status = check_process(process.value, actual);
        if (status != AME_OK) return status;
        BOOL managed = FALSE;
        if (!IsProcessInJob(process.value, nullptr, &managed)) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
        if (managed) return result(AME_ALREADY_MANAGED);
        if (!AssignProcessToJobObject(group->job.value, process.value)) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
        // Capacity reserved before assigning: no throwing allocation after attachment.
        group->members.push_back(std::move(process));
        ++group->membership; group->has_previous = false;
        return result(AME_OK);
    });
}
AmeResult AME_CALL AmeSetCommitNotice(AmeGroup id, uint64_t bytes) {
    return boundary([&] {
        if (bytes && (bytes < 1048576 || bytes > (uint64_t{1} << 40u))) return result(AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT);
        auto* group = find_group(id);
        if (!group) return result(AME_INVALID_GROUP);
        return set_notice(*group, bytes);
    });
}
AmeResult AME_CALL AmeReadGroupSnapshot(AmeGroup id, AmeGroupSnapshot* output) {
    return boundary([&] {
        if (!output || output->size != sizeof(*output)) return result(AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT);
        *output = {}; output->size = sizeof(*output);
        auto* group = find_group(id);
        if (!group) return result(AME_INVALID_GROUP);
        JOBOBJECT_BASIC_AND_IO_ACCOUNTING_INFORMATION counters{};
        if (!QueryInformationJobObject(group->job.value, JobObjectBasicAndIoAccountingInformation,
                                       &counters, sizeof(counters), nullptr)) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
        AmeGroupSnapshot sample{}; sample.size = sizeof(sample);
        sample.monotonic_ms = GetTickCount64();
        sample.attached_total = static_cast<uint32_t>(group->members.size());
        sample.active_processes = counters.BasicInfo.ActiveProcesses;
        sample.commit_notice_bytes = group->notice; sample.membership_revision = group->membership;
        sample.cpu_time_100ns = static_cast<uint64_t>(counters.BasicInfo.TotalKernelTime.QuadPart) +
                              static_cast<uint64_t>(counters.BasicInfo.TotalUserTime.QuadPart);
        sample.page_faults_total = counters.BasicInfo.TotalPageFaultCount;
        sample.io_read_bytes = counters.IoInfo.ReadTransferCount;
        sample.io_write_bytes = counters.IoInfo.WriteTransferCount;
        for (const auto& process : group->members) {
            const DWORD wait = WaitForSingleObject(process.value, 0);
            if (wait == WAIT_OBJECT_0) continue;
            if (wait != WAIT_TIMEOUT) { ++sample.missing_processes; continue; }
            PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS_EX memory{}; memory.cb = sizeof(memory);
            if (!GetProcessMemoryInfo(process.value, reinterpret_cast<PROCESS_MEMORY_COUNTERS*>(&memory), sizeof(memory))) {
                ++sample.missing_processes; continue;
            }
            if (!add(sample.working_set_sum_bytes, memory.WorkingSetSize) ||
                !add(sample.private_commit_sum_bytes, memory.PrivateUsage)) return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, ERROR_ARITHMETIC_OVERFLOW);
            ++sample.measured_processes;
        }
        // Processes may exit during capture: never assert an exact complete sample in that case.
        if (!sample.missing_processes && sample.measured_processes == sample.active_processes)
            sample.flags |= AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE;
        if ((sample.flags & AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE) && group->notice && sample.private_commit_sum_bytes > group->notice)
            sample.flags |= AME_COMMIT_NOTICE_EXCEEDED;
        const DWORD processors = GetActiveProcessorCount(ALL_PROCESSOR_GROUPS);
        ame::resource::calculate_rates(group->has_previous ? &group->previous : nullptr, sample, processors);
        // Keep the baseline when clients read faster than once per second.
        if (!group->has_previous || sample.monotonic_ms - group->previous.monotonic_ms >= 1000 ||
            !(sample.flags & AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE) || !(group->previous.flags & AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE)) {
            group->previous = sample; group->has_previous = true;
        }
        *output = sample; return result(AME_OK);
    });
}
AmeResult AME_CALL AmePollGroupEvent(AmeGroup id, AmeGroupEvent* output) {
    return boundary([&] {
        if (!output || output->size != sizeof(*output)) return result(AME_INVALID_ARGUMENT);
        *output = {}; output->size = sizeof(*output);
        auto* group = find_group(id);
        if (!group) return result(AME_INVALID_GROUP);
        DWORD message = 0; ULONG_PTR key = 0; LPOVERLAPPED data = nullptr;
        if (!GetQueuedCompletionStatus(group->port.value, &message, &key, &data, 0)) {
            const DWORD error = GetLastError();
            return result(error == WAIT_TIMEOUT ? AME_NO_EVENT : AME_WINDOWS_ERROR,
                          error == WAIT_TIMEOUT ? ERROR_SUCCESS : error);
        }
        output->windows_message = message; output->monotonic_ms = GetTickCount64();
        output->policy_revision = group->policy; output->kind = AME_EVENT_OTHER;
        if (message == JOB_OBJECT_MSG_NEW_PROCESS) output->kind = AME_EVENT_PROCESS_STARTED;
        if (message == JOB_OBJECT_MSG_EXIT_PROCESS || message == JOB_OBJECT_MSG_ABNORMAL_EXIT_PROCESS)
            output->kind = AME_EVENT_PROCESS_EXITED;
        if (message == JOB_OBJECT_MSG_ACTIVE_PROCESS_ZERO) output->kind = AME_EVENT_GROUP_EMPTY;
        if (output->kind == AME_EVENT_PROCESS_STARTED || output->kind == AME_EVENT_PROCESS_EXITED)
            output->pid = static_cast<uint32_t>(reinterpret_cast<ULONG_PTR>(data));
        if (message == JOB_OBJECT_MSG_NOTIFICATION_LIMIT) {
            JOBOBJECT_LIMIT_VIOLATION_INFORMATION violation{};
            // Query acknowledges notification and permits the next notification.
            if (!QueryInformationJobObject(group->job.value, JobObjectLimitViolationInformation,
                                           &violation, sizeof(violation), nullptr)) {
                *output = {}; output->size = sizeof(*output);
                return result(AME_WINDOWS_ERROR, GetLastError());
            }
            if (group->notice && (violation.ViolationLimitFlags & JOB_OBJECT_LIMIT_JOB_MEMORY)) {
                output->kind = AME_EVENT_COMMIT_NOTICE;
                output->job_commit_bytes = violation.JobMemory;
                output->commit_notice_bytes = violation.JobMemoryLimit;
            }
        }
        return result(AME_OK);
    });
}
AmeResult AME_CALL AmeCloseGroup(AmeGroup id) {
    return boundary([&] {
        auto* group = find_group(id);
        if (!group) return result(AME_INVALID_GROUP);
        const auto status = set_notice(*group, 0);
        if (status != AME_OK) return status;
        groups.erase(id); return result(AME_OK);
    });
}
=== FILE: D:\.A2\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0-R22-FIX1\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0\src\resource_trace.hpp ===
#pragma once
#include "../sdk/ame_resource_api.h"
#include <chrono>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <sstream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>

namespace ame::resource {
// Optional journal of the demo client. No changes to the DLL's C ABI or policy.
class Trace {
    std::ofstream output_;
    std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point began_ = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
    std::string phase_ = "setup";
    uint64_t sequence_ = 0;
    bool failed_ = false;
    static std::string quoted(const std::string& text) {
        std::ostringstream out; out << '"';
        for (const unsigned char c : text) {
            if (c == '"' || c == '\\') out << '\\' << static_cast<char>(c);
            else if (c < 32) out << "\\u" << std::hex << std::setw(4) << std::setfill('0') << static_cast<unsigned>(c) << std::dec;
            else out << static_cast<char>(c);
        }
        out << '"'; return out.str();
    }
    void entry(const char* type, const std::string& fields) {
        if (!output_.is_open() || failed_) return;
        if (sequence_ >= 8192) { failed_ = true; return; }
        const auto elapsed = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
            std::chrono::steady_clock::now() - began_).count();
        output_ << "{\"seq\":" << ++sequence_ << ",\"type\":" << quoted(type)
                << ",\"elapsed_ms\":" << elapsed << ",\"phase\":" << quoted(phase_)
                << fields << "}\n";
        output_.flush();
        if (!output_) failed_ = true;
    }
public:
    void open(const std::filesystem::path& path, const char* origin = "windows_demo") {
        output_.open(path, std::ios::binary | std::ios::trunc);
        if (!output_) throw std::runtime_error("Cannot create API execution journal");
        entry("session_start", ",\"trace_version\":1,\"api_abi\":1,\"data_origin\":" + quoted(origin));
    }
    bool healthy() const { return !failed_; }
    void mark_failed() noexcept { failed_ = true; }
    void phase(const char* value) {
        phase_ = value;
        entry("phase", "");
    }
    void call(const char* expression, AmeResult status, uint32_t windows_error) {
        entry("call", ",\"expression\":" + quoted(expression) + ",\"result\":" +
            std::to_string(status) + ",\"windows_error\":" + std::to_string(windows_error));
    }
    void check(const char* message, bool passed) {
        entry("check", ",\"message\":" + quoted(message) + ",\"passed\":" + (passed ? "true" : "false"));
    }
    void process(uint32_t pid, uint64_t creation) {
        entry("process", ",\"pid\":" + std::to_string(pid) + ",\"creation_filetime\":" + std::to_string(creation));
    }
    void snapshot(const char* label, AmeGroup group, const AmeGroupSnapshot& s) {
        std::ostringstream out;
        out << ",\"label\":" << quoted(label) << ",\"group\":" << group << ",\"flags\":" << s.flags
            << ",\"active_processes\":" << s.active_processes << ",\"measured_processes\":" << s.measured_processes
            << ",\"missing_processes\":" << s.missing_processes << ",\"working_set_sum_bytes\":" << s.working_set_sum_bytes
            << ",\"private_commit_sum_bytes\":" << s.private_commit_sum_bytes
            << ",\"commit_notice_bytes\":" << s.commit_notice_bytes << ",\"rate_interval_ms\":" << s.rate_interval_ms
            << ",\"cpu_percent_machine\":" << std::setprecision(17) << s.cpu_percent_machine
            << ",\"io_read_bytes_per_second\":" << s.io_read_bytes_per_second
            << ",\"io_write_bytes_per_second\":" << s.io_write_bytes_per_second
            << ",\"page_faults_per_second\":" << s.page_faults_per_second
            << ",\"private_commit_change_bytes\":" << s.private_commit_change_bytes;
        entry("snapshot", out.str());
    }
    void event(AmeGroup group, const AmeGroupEvent& e) {
        entry("event", ",\"group\":" + std::to_string(group) + ",\"kind\":" + std::to_string(e.kind) +
            ",\"pid\":" + std::to_string(e.pid) + ",\"windows_message\":" + std::to_string(e.windows_message) +
            ",\"job_commit_bytes\":" + std::to_string(e.job_commit_bytes) +
            ",\"commit_notice_bytes\":" + std::to_string(e.commit_notice_bytes));
    }
    void finish(bool passed, unsigned checks, const std::string& error = "") {
        entry("session_end", ",\"passed\":" + std::string(passed ? "true" : "false") +
            ",\"checks\":" + std::to_string(checks) + ",\"error\":" + quoted(error));
    }
};
}

            check(controller.finished()&&!controller.feedback_pending(),"complete assessment at five minutes");

        auto result=read(path);
        check(result.find("\"engine_revision\":\"R10\"")!=std::string::npos,"diagnosis identifies the corrected foreground engine");
        check(occurrences(result,"\"type\":\"recover_activity_warning\"")==1,"one warning event despite repeated spike");
        check(result.find("\"phase\":\"5s\"")!=std::string::npos&&result.find("\"phase\":\"15s\"")!=std::string::npos&&result.find("\"phase\":\"60s\"")!=std::string::npos,"full observation milestones survive warning");
        check(result.find("resident_reduction_retained_5m_with_activity")!=std::string::npos,"resident-only outcome after warning");
        check(result.find("\"reason\":\"resident_reduction_retained_5m\"")==std::string::npos,"warning cannot become qualified success");
        reset();{

        reset();{

#include "../src/resource_analysis.hpp"
#include <cmath>
#include <iostream>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace {
unsigned checks = 0;
void check(bool condition, const char* text) {
    ++checks;
    if (!condition) throw std::runtime_error(text);
}
bool near(double a, double b) { return std::abs(a - b) < 0.00001; }
AmeGroupSnapshot baseline() {
    AmeGroupSnapshot s{}; s.size = sizeof(s); s.flags = AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE;
    s.monotonic_ms = 10000; s.membership_revision = 2; s.active_processes = 2;
    s.measured_processes = 2; s.private_commit_sum_bytes = 100000;
    s.cpu_time_100ns = 50000000; s.io_read_bytes = 2000; s.io_write_bytes = 3000;
    s.page_faults_total = 100;
    return s;
}
AmeGroupSnapshot next() {
    auto s = baseline(); s.monotonic_ms += 2000;
    s.private_commit_sum_bytes += 30000; s.cpu_time_100ns += 10000000;
    s.io_read_bytes += 4000; s.io_write_bytes += 6000; s.page_faults_total += 200;
    return s;
}
void invalid(const AmeGroupSnapshot* before, AmeGroupSnapshot current, uint32_t processors = 4) {
    current.flags |= AME_RATES_VALID; current.cpu_percent_machine = 99;
    current.private_commit_change_bytes = 999; current.rate_interval_ms = 999;
    current.io_read_bytes_per_second = current.io_write_bytes_per_second = current.page_faults_per_second = 999;
    ame::resource::calculate_rates(before, current, processors);
    check(!(current.flags & AME_RATES_VALID), "invalid sample cannot report valid rates");
    check(current.cpu_percent_machine == 0 && current.private_commit_change_bytes == 0 &&
          current.rate_interval_ms == 0 && current.io_read_bytes_per_second == 0 &&
          current.io_write_bytes_per_second == 0 && current.page_faults_per_second == 0,
          "invalid sample must clear stale calculated outputs");
}
}
int main() {
    try {
        const auto before = baseline(); auto current = next();
        ame::resource::calculate_rates(&before, current, 4);
        check((current.flags & AME_RATES_VALID) != 0, "complete two-second sample");
        check(current.rate_interval_ms == 2000 && near(current.cpu_percent_machine, 12.5), "whole-machine CPU normalization");
        check(near(current.io_read_bytes_per_second, 2000) && near(current.io_write_bytes_per_second, 3000), "I/O rates");
        check(near(current.page_faults_per_second, 100), "all page faults, not disk faults");
        check(current.private_commit_change_bytes == 30000, "allocation growth");
        current = next(); current.private_commit_sum_bytes = 50000;
        ame::resource::calculate_rates(&before, current, 4);
        check(current.private_commit_change_bytes == -50000, "allocation decrease keeps sign");
        invalid(nullptr, next()); invalid(&before, next(), 0);
        current = next(); current.flags = 0; invalid(&before, current);
        auto missing = before; missing.flags = 0; invalid(&missing, next());
        current = next(); ++current.membership_revision; invalid(&before, current);
        current = next(); --current.active_processes; invalid(&before, current);
        for (const uint64_t interval : {uint64_t{0}, uint64_t{999}, uint64_t{6001}}) {
            current = next(); current.monotonic_ms = before.monotonic_ms + interval; invalid(&before, current);
        }
        current = next(); current.monotonic_ms = 1; invalid(&before, current);
        current = next(); current.cpu_time_100ns = 1; invalid(&before, current);
        current = next(); current.io_read_bytes = 1; invalid(&before, current);
        current = next(); current.io_write_bytes = 1; invalid(&before, current);
        current = next(); current.page_faults_total = 1; invalid(&before, current);
        current = next(); current.private_commit_sum_bytes = std::numeric_limits<uint64_t>::max(); invalid(&before, current);
        current = next(); current.cpu_time_100ns += 1000000000; invalid(&before, current);
        for (const uint64_t interval : {uint64_t{1000}, uint64_t{6000}}) {
            current = next(); current.monotonic_ms = before.monotonic_ms + interval;
            current.flags |= AME_COMMIT_NOTICE_EXCEEDED;
            ame::resource::calculate_rates(&before, current, 4);
            check((current.flags & AME_RATES_VALID) && (current.flags & AME_COMMIT_NOTICE_EXCEEDED), "valid interval endpoints keep notice flag");
        }
        current = next(); current.private_commit_sum_bytes = before.private_commit_sum_bytes;
        ame::resource::calculate_rates(&before, current, 4);
        check(current.private_commit_change_bytes == 0 && (current.flags & AME_RATES_VALID), "unchanged allocation is a real measured zero");
        std::cout << "PASS " << checks << " resource analysis checks\n";
        return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) { std::cerr << "FAIL " << e.what() << '\n'; return 1; }
}
=== FILE: D:\.A2\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0-R22-FIX1\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0\tests\resource_api_client_tests.cs ===
namespace AmeHub {
    public static class ResourceApiClientTests {
        private static int count;
        private static void Check(bool value) { count++; if(!value) throw new System.Exception("API client contract check "+count); }
        private static void Refuses(System.Action action) {
            bool refused=false; try { action(); } catch { refused=true; } Check(refused);
        }
        private static int Fill(uint pid, System.IntPtr output) {
            Check(System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.ReadInt32(output)==65552);
            System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.WriteInt32(output,4,unchecked((int)pid));
            System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.WriteInt64(output,8,134362770626194055L);
            byte[] path=System.Text.Encoding.Unicode.GetBytes("D:\\Apps con spazi\\caff\u00e8.exe\0");
            System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.Copy(path,0,System.IntPtr.Add(output,16),path.Length);
            return 0;
        }
        public static ResourceApiClient ValidClient() {
            return new ResourceApiClient(delegate {return 1;},delegate {return 0;},Fill);
        }
        public static int Run() {
            count=0;
            Refuses(delegate { using(var c=new ResourceApiClient(delegate {return 2;},delegate {return 0;},Fill)) {} });
            var client=ValidClient();
            var id=client.Read(22452);
            Check(id.Pid==22452 && id.Creation==134362770626194055UL && id.Path=="D:\\Apps con spazi\\caff\u00e8.exe");
            client.Validate("22452:134362770626194055","d:\\apps con spazi\\CAFF\u00c8.exe");
            Refuses(delegate {client.Validate("22452:134362770626194054",id.Path);});
            Refuses(delegate {client.Validate("22452:134362770626194055","D:\\different.exe");});
            Refuses(delegate {client.Validate("22452",id.Path);});
            Refuses(delegate {client.Validate("22452:0",id.Path);});
            Refuses(delegate {client.Validate("4294967296:134362770626194055",id.Path);});
            Refuses(delegate {client.Validate("22452:134362770626194055","");});
            Refuses(delegate {client.Read(0);});
            Check(client.SuccessfulReads==4);
            client.Dispose();client.Dispose();
            Refuses(delegate {client.Read(22452);});
            using(var bad=new ResourceApiClient(delegate {return 1;},delegate {return 5;},delegate(uint p,System.IntPtr b){return 3;})) {
                string message="";try {bad.Read(22452);} catch(System.Exception e){message=e.Message;}
                Check(message.Contains("API 3, Windows 5") && bad.SuccessfulReads==0);
            }
            ResourceApiClient.ReadIdentity[] invalid={
                delegate(uint p,System.IntPtr b){Fill(p,b);System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.WriteInt32(b,1);return 0;},
                delegate(uint p,System.IntPtr b){Fill(p,b);System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.WriteInt32(b,4,7);return 0;},
                delegate(uint p,System.IntPtr b){Fill(p,b);System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.WriteInt64(b,8,0);return 0;},
                delegate(uint p,System.IntPtr b){Fill(p,b);System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.WriteInt16(b,16,0);return 0;},
                delegate(uint p,System.IntPtr b){Fill(p,b);for(int i=16;i<65552;i+=2)System.Runtime.InteropServices.Marshal.WriteInt16(b,i,65);return 0;}
            };
            foreach(var r in invalid) using(var bad=new ResourceApiClient(delegate {return 1;},delegate {return 0;},r)) {
                Refuses(delegate {bad.Read(22452);});Check(bad.SuccessfulReads==0);
            }
            return count;
        }
    }
}

#include "../src/resource_trace.hpp"
#include <iostream>

// Generates only synthetic observations, for the report/serialization tests.
int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    if (argc != 2) return 2;
    try {
        ame::resource::Trace trace;
        trace.open(std::filesystem::u8path(argv[1]), "synthetic_test");
        trace.phase("identity");
        trace.call("AmeAttachProcess(group, identity) <script>alert(\"x\")</script>\n\t", AME_IDENTITY_CHANGED, 0);
        trace.check("wrong identity refused: \"quote\" \\ slash\nnew line", true);
        trace.process(42, 134000000000000000ull);
        AmeGroupSnapshot before{}; before.size = sizeof(before); before.flags = AME_MEMORY_COMPLETE;
        before.active_processes = 1; before.measured_processes = 1;
        before.private_commit_sum_bytes = 8 * 1048576ull; before.working_set_sum_bytes = 10 * 1048576ull;
        trace.snapshot("before_growth", 1, before);
        trace.phase("memory_notice");
        auto after = before; after.private_commit_sum_bytes = 72 * 1048576ull;
        after.working_set_sum_bytes = 74 * 1048576ull; after.commit_notice_bytes = 32 * 1048576ull;
        after.flags |= AME_RATES_VALID; after.cpu_percent_machine = 1.25;
        after.page_faults_per_second = 2.5; after.rate_interval_ms = 2000;
        trace.snapshot("after_growth", 1, after);
        AmeGroupEvent event{}; event.size = sizeof(event); event.kind = AME_EVENT_COMMIT_NOTICE;
        event.windows_message = 11; event.job_commit_bytes = 72 * 1048576ull;
        event.commit_notice_bytes = 32 * 1048576ull;
        trace.event(1, event);
        trace.finish(true, 1);
        if (!trace.healthy()) return 1;
        std::cout << "PASS synthetic trace fixture generated\n";
        return 0;
    } catch (const std::exception& e) { std::cerr << e.what() << '\n'; return 1; }
}

$script:checks=0
function Assert($ok,[string]$message){$script:checks++;if(!$ok){throw $message}}
function Reject([scriptblock]$Action,[string]$Message){$failed=$false;try{& $Action|Out-Null}catch{$failed=$true};Assert $failed $Message}
$temp=Join-Path ([IO.Path]::GetTempPath()) ('ame automatic '+[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
[void][IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($temp)
$config=Get-AgentConfig (Join-Path $root 'agent-config.json')
function Reading([double]$PrivateMiB=1000,[double]$OwnMiB=100){
    return [pscustomobject]@{Complete=$true;AppPrivateBytes=$PrivateMiB*1MB;OwnPrivateBytes=$OwnMiB*1MB;AppCommitBytes=1500MB;AvailableBytes=2GB;TotalBytes=16GB;AppProcesses=3;IdentityKey='a';CounterCoverageComplete=$true;CpuPercent=1;PageFaultsPerSecond=1;IoReadBytesPerSecond=0}
}
function Ready-State {
    $state=New-AgentState $config
    $sample=[pscustomobject]@{type='sample';physical_total_bytes=16GB;physical_available_bytes=1GB;process_snapshot_complete=$true;processes=@()}
    foreach($at in @(0,10,20,30)){Update-AgentTelemetry $state $sample $at $at}
    $state.Profiles[1]=@{Ready=$true;AutoCapable=$true;Truncated=$false;Seen=30;Browser='Chrome';Session=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString();LastSequence=1;Tabs=@([pscustomobject]@{id=42;revision=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString();host='example.org';title='Example';auto_allowed=$true;blocked='';discarded=$false;lastAccessed=[DateTimeOffset]::UtcNow.AddMinutes(-20).ToUnixTimeMilliseconds()})}
    return $state
}
function Reset-Runtime {
    $script:agent=Ready-State;$script:agentDirectory=$temp;$script:agentRunId=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString();$script:agentProcessId=$PID;$script:agentCreation='1'
    $script:agentBrowserConfig=Get-Content (Join-Path $root 'browser/config.json') -Raw|ConvertFrom-Json
    $script:agentBridge=[pscustomobject]@{Sent=(New-Object Collections.ArrayList);Allow=$true;Entries=@();Disconnected=(New-Object Collections.ArrayList)}
    $script:agentBridge|Add-Member ScriptMethod Send {param($connection,$json);[void]$this.Sent.Add(@{connection=$connection;message=($json|ConvertFrom-Json)});return $this.Allow}
    $script:agentBridge|Add-Member ScriptMethod Drain {return $this.Entries}
    $script:agentBridge|Add-Member ScriptMethod Disconnect {param($connection);[void]$this.Disconnected.Add($connection)}
}

function Assert($Ok,[string]$Message){if(!$Ok){throw $Message}}
function Reject([scriptblock]$Work,[string]$Message){$failed=$false;try{& $Work}catch{$failed=$true};Assert $failed $Message}
function Profile([string]$Browser='Chrome',[int]$Count=1){
    $tabs=@(for($n=1;$n -le $Count;$n++){@{id=[long]$n;revision=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('D');title=('Pagina '+$n);blocked='';discarded=$false}})
    return @{Browser=$Browser;Ready=$true;Session=[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('D');Seen=[DateTime]::UtcNow;LastSequence=1L;Tabs=$tabs}
}
function Sample([double]$Private=2GB,[double]$Own=50MB,[string]$Identity='1:created:path'){
    return [pscustomobject]@{AppPrivateBytes=$Private;AppCommitBytes=3GB;OwnPrivateBytes=$Own;AvailableBytes=2GB;TotalBytes=16GB;AppProcesses=1;
        Complete=$true;IdentityKey=$Identity;CpuPercent=0.1;PageFaultsPerSecond=1.0;IoReadBytesPerSecond=0.0;CounterCoverageComplete=$true}
}
function Pair([double]$After=1GB,[double]$Own=50MB,[int]$Spike=-1){
    $p=Profile;$s=New-ComparisonSession $p 1 $p.Tabs 'Stesse pagine e attivita'
    for($t=0;$t -le 900;$t+=5){[void](Add-ComparisonSample $s (Sample) $t)}
    Assert ($s.Phase -eq 'ready' -and $s.Applied -eq 0) 'Baseline claims or performs an intervention'
    $s.Phase='ame';$s.LastSeconds=$null;$s.Applied=1
    for($t=0;$t -le 900;$t+=5){$bytes=if($t -eq $Spike){2GB}else{$After};[void](Add-ComparisonSample $s (Sample $bytes $Own) $t)}
    return $s
}
$s=Pair

function Begin([string]$Browser='Chrome',[int]$Count=1){

function Acknowledge([string]$Outcome='discarded'){
    $r=@($script:comparison.Requests.GetEnumerator()|Where-Object {$_.Value.Outcome -eq 'pending'})[0]
    Assert ($null -ne $r) 'No serial pending request'
    [void](Receive-ComparisonResult 1 ([pscustomobject]@{request=$r.Key;tab=$r.Value.Tab;outcome=$Outcome;reason='fixture';operation_ms=1}))
    if($Outcome -eq 'discarded'){$profiles[1].Tabs|Where-Object {$_.id -eq $r.Value.Tab}|ForEach-Object {$_.discarded=$true};$profiles[1].LastSequence++}
    return $r.Key
}

function Assert($ok,[string]$message){if(!$ok){throw $message}}

[void][IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($temp)
$writer=$null
function Write-Events([string]$path,$events){
    $lines=@($events|ForEach-Object {$_|ConvertTo-Json -Compress -Depth 6})
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($path,($lines -join "`n")+"`n",(New-Object Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))
}
function Read-Zip([string]$path){
    $zip=[IO.Compression.ZipFile]::OpenRead($path)
    try{
        $result=@{}
        foreach($entry in $zip.Entries){$stream=$entry.Open();$reader=New-Object IO.StreamReader($stream,[Text.Encoding]::UTF8,$true);try{$result[$entry.FullName.Replace('\','/')]=$reader.ReadToEnd()}finally{$reader.Dispose()}}
        return $result
    }finally{$zip.Dispose()}
}

    Flush-RamSort
}

function New-FakeEngine {
    $p=[pscustomobject]@{HasExited=$false;ExitCode=0;StandardError=[pscustomobject]@{}}
    $p.StandardError|Add-Member ScriptMethod ReadToEnd {return ''}
    $p|Add-Member ScriptMethod Dispose {}
    return $p
}
function Start-Engine([string]$mode){
    Assert ($mode -eq 'recover-test') 'La coda ha avviato un osservatore o un recupero automatico'
    Assert ($script:runSelection.Count -eq 1) 'Il motore singolo ha ricevuto piu identita'
    $script:starts.Add($script:runSelection[0])
    $script:engine=New-FakeEngine;$script:mode=$mode;$script:trimmed=0;$script:userStopping=$false
    $script:reportPath=Join-Path $temp ('run-'+[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N')+'.jsonl')
    $script:stopFile=$script:reportPath+'.stop';$script:ends=[DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(180)
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($script:reportPath,'')
}
function Reset-Fixture([int]$count=3){
    if($script:tail){$script:tail.Dispose()};$script:tail=$null
    $script:starts=New-Object 'Collections.Generic.List[object]';$script:exports=New-Object 'Collections.Generic.List[object]'
    $script:messages=New-Object 'Collections.Generic.List[string]'
    $script:selection=@{};$script:targetStates=@{};$script:batchActive=$false;$script:batchQueue=@()
    $script:restartMode=$null;$script:mode='observe';$script:userStopping=$false;$script:trimmed=0
    $script:currentOutcome='';$script:currentFeedback='';$script:currentWait=''
    $script:lastComplete=[DateTime]::UtcNow;$script:engine=New-FakeEngine
    $script:reportPath=Join-Path $temp 'observe.jsonl';$script:stopFile=Join-Path $temp 'observe.stop'
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($script:reportPath,'')
    $script:ends=[DateTime]::UtcNow.AddMinutes(10)
    $rows=@()
    foreach($i in 1..$count){
        $row=[pscustomobject]@{Key=([string]($i+100)+':'+$i);Pid=($i+100);Name=('app'+$i+'.exe');Path=('D:\Apps\app'+$i+'.exe');PrivateBytes=((7-$i)*128MB);Blocked=''}
        $script:selection[$row.Key]=$row
        $cells=@{};foreach($key in @('choose','state','idle','cpu','faults')){$cells[$key]=[pscustomobject]@{Value=$null;ReadOnly=$false;ToolTipText=''}}
        $rows+=[pscustomobject]@{Tag=$row;Cells=$cells}
    }
    $script:grid=[pscustomobject]@{Rows=$rows}
    foreach($button in @('start','single','stopButton','clear','duration')){Set-Variable -Scope Script -Name $button -Value ([pscustomobject]@{Enabled=$true})}
    $script:pause=[pscustomobject]@{Enabled=$true;Text=''}
    $script:state=[pscustomobject]@{Text=''};$script:result=[pscustomobject]@{Text=''};$script:ram=[pscustomobject]@{Text='Interventi: 0'}
}
function Complete-Current($events,[int]$code=0){
    $lines=@($events|ForEach-Object {$_|ConvertTo-Json -Compress -Depth 6})
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($script:reportPath,($lines -join "`n")+"`n")
    $script:engine.HasExited=$true;$script:engine.ExitCode=$code
    & $callback
}

    & $callback
    Assert ($script:starts.Count -eq 1 -and $script:runSelection[0].Key -eq '101:1') 'Il primo processo non e quello con piu RAM'
}

if ($Bridge) {
    if ($env:OS -ne 'Windows_NT') { throw 'Il test del bridge richiede Windows.' }
    $exe=Join-Path $root 'build\ame-browser-host.exe'
    $config=Get-Content (Join-Path $root 'browser\config.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
    foreach($browser in @('Chrome','Firefox')){
    $server=New-Object AmeHub.BrowserBridge($exe)
    $process=$null
    try {
        $info=New-Object Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
        $info.FileName=$exe; $info.Arguments=if($browser -eq 'Firefox'){'"'+(Join-Path $root 'build\firefox-native-host.json')+'" '+$config.firefox_extension_id}else{'chrome-extension://'+$config.extension_id+'/'}
        $info.UseShellExecute=$false; $info.CreateNoWindow=$true
        $info.RedirectStandardInput=$true; $info.RedirectStandardOutput=$true; $info.RedirectStandardError=$true
        $process=New-Object Diagnostics.Process; $process.StartInfo=$info; [void]$process.Start()
        [AmeHub.Frames]::Write($process.StandardInput.BaseStream,'{"type":"fixture","value":"caffè"}')
        $deadline=[DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(8); $entry=$null
        do {
            foreach ($e in $server.Drain()) { if ($e.Error) { throw $e.Error }; if ($e.Json) { $entry=$e } }
            if (!$entry) { Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 40 }
        } while (!$entry -and [DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $deadline)
        Assert ($null -ne $entry) 'Host non collegato'
        Assert (($entry.Json|ConvertFrom-Json).value -eq 'caffè') 'Host ha corrotto il frame'
        Assert ($server.Send($entry.Connection,'{"type":"reply"}')) 'Invio bridge fallito'
        # Native reader has a deadline: a broken bridge must not hang the test.
        $reply=[AmeHub.Frames]::ReadWithTimeout($process.StandardOutput.BaseStream,5000)
        Assert ($reply -eq '{"type":"reply"}') 'Risposta bridge alterata'
        for($cycle=1;$cycle -le 8;$cycle++){
            # A small input fits the redirected stdin buffer even when the native host is blocked.
            # Reply timeout therefore catches a stalled native writer without hanging this test.
            Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 80
            $frame='{"type":"inventory-probe","cycle":'+$cycle+',"payload":"'+('x'*1024)+'"}'
            [AmeHub.Frames]::Write($process.StandardInput.BaseStream,$frame)
            $deadline=[DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(5);$next=$null
            do{
                foreach($e in $server.Drain()){if($e.Error){throw $e.Error};if($e.Json){$next=$e}}
                if(!$next){Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 20}
            }while(!$next -and [DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $deadline)
            Assert ($next -and $next.Json -ceq $frame) ('Native pipe stalled after welcome, cycle '+$cycle)
            $ack='{"type":"inventory_ack","cycle":'+$cycle+'}'
            Assert ($server.Send($next.Connection,$ack)) 'Repeat bridge send failed'
            Assert ([AmeHub.Frames]::ReadWithTimeout($process.StandardOutput.BaseStream,5000) -ceq $ack) 'Repeat native reply failed'
        }
        $process.StandardInput.Close()
        Assert ($process.WaitForExit(5000)) 'Host non termina con EOF'
        Write-Host ('PASS '+$browser+' Windows native-host identity + 8 post-welcome exchanges + bidirectional framing + EOF shutdown')
    } finally {
        $server.Dispose()
        if ($process) { if (!$process.HasExited) { $process.Kill() }; $process.Dispose() }
    }
}
}

=== FILE: D:\.A2\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0-R22-FIX1\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0\tests\test-process-reduction-api.ps1 ===
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$root=Split-Path $PSScriptRoot -Parent
. (Join-Path $root 'windows-model.ps1')
. (Join-Path $root 'process-reduction-api.ps1')
if(!('AmeHub.ReportTail' -as [type])){Add-Type -Path (Join-Path $root 'src/hub-runtime.cs')}
$script:count=0
function Check($ok,[string]$message){$script:count++;if(!$ok){throw $message}}
function Reject([scriptblock]$Action,[string]$Message){$rejected=$false;try{& $Action|Out-Null}catch{$rejected=$true};Check $rejected $Message}
$target=[pscustomobject]@{Pid=123;Key='123:456';Path='D:\Apps con spazi\caffe.exe';Name='caffe.exe'}
function Session {
    return @{type='recover_session';engine_revision='R10';single_test=$true;selected=@(@{pid=123;creation_filetime='456'});allowed_images=@($target.Path);observation_target_seconds=300}
}
function Applied-State {
    $s=New-AmeReductionState $target;Update-AmeReductionState $s (Session)
    Update-AmeReductionState $s @{type='recover_intent';pid=123;elapsed_seconds=60}
    Update-AmeReductionState $s @{type='recover_applied';pid=123;elapsed_seconds=60}
    return $s
}
function Feedback([double]$after=100MB,[double]$seconds=0) {
    return @{type='recover_feedback';pid=123;creation_filetime='456';elapsed_seconds=60+$seconds;seconds_after_action=$seconds;private_resident_before_bytes=300MB;private_resident_after_bytes=$after;private_commit_delta_bytes=0}
}
function Assessment([string]$Reason='resident_reduction_retained_5m') {
    return @{type='recover_assessed';pid=123;creation_filetime='456';image_path=$target.Path;elapsed_seconds=360;seconds_after_action=300;reason=$Reason;remaining_private_resident_reduction_bytes=200MB;minimum_private_resident_reduction_last_60s_bytes=180MB}
}
$temp=Join-Path ([IO.Path]::GetTempPath()) ('ame process api '+[guid]::NewGuid().ToString('N'))
[void][IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($temp)
try{
    $info=New-AmeReductionStartInfo -Root $root -Targets @($target) -Report (Join-Path $temp 'report con spazi.jsonl') -StopFile (Join-Path $temp 'report.stop')
    Check (!$info.UseShellExecute -and $info.CreateNoWindow -and $info.RedirectStandardOutput -and $info.RedirectStandardError) 'Unsafe process start'
    Check ($info.Arguments -like '*"--recover-test"*"123:456"*"D:\Apps con spazi\caffe.exe"*') 'Identity/path not represented'
    foreach($key in @('0:1','4:1','123:0','4294967296:1','123:18446744073709551616','123:456 --observe')){
        $bad=[pscustomobject]@{Key=$key;Path=$target.Path};Reject {New-AmeReductionStartInfo -Root $root -Targets @($bad) -Report x -StopFile y} 'Invalid identity accepted'
    }
    foreach($image in @('app.exe','D:\Apps\bad".exe',"D:\Apps\bad`n.exe",'D:\Apps\')){
        $bad=[pscustomobject]@{Key=$target.Key;Path=$image};Reject {New-AmeReductionStartInfo -Root $root -Targets @($bad) -Report x -StopFile y} 'Invalid image accepted'
    }
    Reject {New-AmeReductionStartInfo -Root $root -Targets @($target,$target) -Report x -StopFile y} 'Single API accepts multiple targets'
    Reject {New-AmeReductionStartInfo -Root $root -Targets @($target) -Seconds 181 -Report x -StopFile y} 'Single API unbounded action window'
    $s=New-AmeReductionState $target;Reject {Update-AmeReductionState $s @{type='recover_applied';pid=123}} 'Action before session verified'
    foreach($wrong in @('pid','creation','image','engine','single')){
        $s=New-AmeReductionState $target;$e=Session
        switch($wrong){'pid'{$e.selected[0].pid=124}'creation'{$e.selected[0].creation_filetime='999'}'image'{$e.allowed_images=@('D:\other.exe')}'engine'{$e.engine_revision='R8'}'single'{$e.single_test=$false}}
        Reject {Update-AmeReductionState $s $e} ('Session mismatch accepted: '+$wrong)
    }
    $s=Applied-State;Check ($s.Applied -and $null -eq $s.ResidentDropBytes -and !$s.Assessed) 'Applied falsely means RAM reduced'
    Update-AmeReductionState $s (Feedback);Check ($s.ResidentDropBytes -eq 200MB -and $s.PrivateCommitDeltaBytes -eq 0) 'Resident and commit confused'
    Update-AmeReductionState $s (Feedback 350MB 5);Check ($s.ResidentDropBytes -eq -50MB) 'Signed RAM rebound lost'
    Update-AmeReductionState $s @{type='recover_target';pid=123;creation_filetime='456';elapsed_seconds=65;private_resident_bytes=360MB}
    Check ($s.ResidentDropBytes -eq -60MB -and $s.Message -like '*5 s*') 'Live per-process telemetry did not update visible RAM'
    Update-AmeReductionState $s @{type='recover_activity_warning';pid=123;elapsed_seconds=65;reason='activity_or_fault_increase_stop_further_actions'}
    Update-AmeReductionState $s @{type='recover_usage_warning';pid=123;elapsed_seconds=65;reason='foreground_return_observation_only'}
    Check ($s.Applied -and !$s.Assessed) 'Usage warnings end valid measurement'
    Update-AmeReductionState $s (Feedback 100MB 300);Update-AmeReductionState $s (Assessment)
    $finalText=$s.Message
    Update-AmeReductionState $s @{type='recover_target';pid=123;creation_filetime='456';elapsed_seconds=360;private_resident_bytes=140MB}
    Check ($s.Message -ceq $finalText) 'Final assessment overwritten by same-cycle telemetry'
    Update-AmeReductionState $s @{type='end'};Complete-AmeReductionState $s 0
    Check ($s.Final -and $s.Assessed -and $s.Phase -eq 'assessed' -and !$s.InternalCacheReleaseVerified -and !$s.CausationEstablished -and !$s.ApplicationLatencyMeasured) 'Final result overclaims'
    $s=Applied-State;$e=Assessment;$e.seconds_after_action=60;Reject {Update-AmeReductionState $s $e} 'Early positive accepted'

    $script:ownedEngine|Add-Member ScriptMethod BeginOutputReadLine {}
    $script:ownedEngine|Add-Member ScriptMethod WaitForExit {param($timeout);$this.HasExited=$true;return $true}
    $script:ownedEngine|Add-Member ScriptMethod Dispose {$this.Disposed=$true}

function Assert($ok,[string]$message){if(!$ok){throw $message}}

[void][IO.Directory]::CreateDirectory($temp)
$report=Join-Path $temp 'must-not-be-created.jsonl'
$cases=@(
    @('--recover-test','--allow-exe','D:\Apps\app.exe'),
    @('--recover-test','--allow-exe','D:\Apps\app.exe','--allow-process','123:456','--allow-process','124:789'),
    @('--recover-test','--allow-exe','D:\Apps\app.exe','--allow-exe','D:\Apps\other.exe','--allow-process','123:456'),
    @('--recover-test','--allow-exe','D:\Apps\app.exe','--allow-process','123:456','--seconds','181'),
    @('--observe','--recover-test','--allow-exe','D:\Apps\app.exe','--allow-process','123:456'),
    @('--recover','--recover-test','--allow-exe','D:\Apps\app.exe','--allow-process','123:456'),
    @('--recover-test','--recover','--allow-exe','D:\Apps\app.exe','--allow-process','123:456'),
    @('--recover-test','--allow-exe','D:\Apps\app.exe','--allow-process','123:bad')
)
try{
    foreach($case in $cases){
        $p=New-Object Diagnostics.Process
        $p.StartInfo.FileName=$engine
        $quoted=foreach($arg in (@($case)+@('--report',$report))){'"'+[string]$arg+'"'}
        $p.StartInfo.Arguments=$quoted -join ' '
        $p.StartInfo.UseShellExecute=$false;$p.StartInfo.CreateNoWindow=$true
        $p.StartInfo.RedirectStandardOutput=$true;$p.StartInfo.RedirectStandardError=$true
        try{
            [void]$p.Start()
            $stdout=$p.StandardOutput.ReadToEndAsync();$stderr=$p.StandardError.ReadToEndAsync()
            Assert ($p.WaitForExit(5000)) 'Parser non termina'

    else{
        $records=@(
            @{seq=1;type='session_start';elapsed_ms=0;phase='setup';trace_version=1;api_abi=1;data_origin='synthetic_test'},
            @{seq=2;type='call';elapsed_ms=1;phase='identity';expression='AmeAttachProcess(group, identity) <script>alert("x")</script>';result=4;windows_error=0},

            @{seq=5;type='event';elapsed_ms=4;phase='memory_notice';kind=4;windows_message=11;pid=0;job_commit_bytes=75497472;commit_notice_bytes=33554432},
            @{seq=6;type='session_end';elapsed_ms=5;phase='capacity';passed=$true;checks=1;error=''}
        )
        $lines=@($records|ForEach-Object {$_|ConvertTo-Json -Compress})
        [IO.File]::WriteAllText($trace,($lines -join "`n")+"`n",(New-Object Text.UTF8Encoding($false)))
    }

    [void](Write-ResourceApiReport -TracePath $trace -OutputPath $report -SourceRoot $root -NativeExitCode 0)

    Assert ($html.Contains('8,0 MiB') -and $html.Contains('72,0 MiB') -and $html.Contains('32,0 MiB')) 'Misure o soglia non riportate'

    Assert ($html.Contains('&lt;script&gt;') -and !$html.Contains('<script>')) 'Contenuto del registro eseguibile nel resoconto'
    Assert ($html.Contains('AssignProcessToJobObject') -and $html.Contains('QueryInformationJobObject') -and $html.Contains('Codice sorgente consultabile')) 'Spiegazione Windows o sorgenti mancanti'
    Assert ($html.Contains('I Job Object restano nella demo') -and $html.Contains('non applica una riduzione RAM')) 'Resoconto promette riduzioni non eseguite'

function Assert($ok,[string]$message){if(!$ok){throw $message}}

$target=[pscustomobject]@{Key='21904:134362787950151731';Pid=21904;Name='chrome.exe';Path='C:\Apps\chrome.exe';Blocked='';PrivateBytes=320MB}
$outcome=[pscustomobject]@{type='recover_assessed';pid=21904;creation_filetime='134362787950151731';image_path=$target.Path;reason='rapid_resident_rebound';seconds_after_action=171.917;best_private_resident_reduction_bytes=319MB;remaining_private_resident_reduction_bytes=0;minimum_private_resident_reduction_last_60s_bytes=0}
try{
    # Reproduce the 5.1 absence of Count on a single custom object even under 7.x.
    $singleRecord=[pscustomobject]@{classification='retained'}
    $singleRecord|Add-Member NoteProperty Count $null
    Assert (($singleRecord|Where-Object classification -eq retained).Count -ne 1 -and
        @($singleRecord|Where-Object classification -eq retained).Count -eq 1) 'PS 5.1 single-result count regression'
    Assert (@($singleRecord|Where-Object classification -eq brief).Count -eq 0 -and
        @(@($singleRecord,$singleRecord)|Where-Object classification -eq retained).Count -eq 2) 'Zero/multiple filtered counts changed'
    Save-StrategyOutcome $path $outcome @($target) 'first.jsonl' $now
    $records=@(Read-StrategyHistory $path $now);$advice=Get-StrategyAdvice $target $records
    Assert ($records.Count -eq 1 -and $advice.CanReleaseTabs -and $advice.Bad -eq 1 -and $advice.Explanation -like '*sconsigliata*') 'Rebound not learned or app-supported route missing'
    Save-StrategyOutcome $path $outcome @($target) 'first.jsonl' $now
    Assert (@(Read-StrategyHistory $path $now).Count -eq 1) 'Same trial counted twice'
    $wrong=[pscustomobject]@{Key='21904:999';Path=$target.Path}
    Save-StrategyOutcome $path $outcome @($wrong) 'wrong.jsonl' $now
    Assert (@(Read-StrategyHistory $path $now).Count -eq 1) 'Unauthorized identity learned'
    $outcome.reason='resident_reduction_retained_5m';$outcome.seconds_after_action=200;$outcome.remaining_private_resident_reduction_bytes=64MB;$outcome.minimum_private_resident_reduction_last_60s_bytes=60MB
    Save-StrategyOutcome $path $outcome @($target) 'too-short.jsonl' $now
    Assert (@(Read-StrategyHistory $path $now).Count -eq 1) 'Early observation promoted to 5m evidence'
    $outcome.seconds_after_action=300
    Save-StrategyOutcome $path $outcome @($target) 'retained.jsonl' $now
    $records=@(Read-StrategyHistory $path $now)
    # Windows PowerShell 5.1 does not reliably expose Count on a single
    # PSCustomObject. Force filtered results into arrays before counting.
    Assert ($records.Count -eq 2 -and @($records|Where-Object classification -eq retained).Count -eq 1) 'Valid observed 5m outcome lost'
    $outcome.reason='resident_reduction_retained_5m_with_activity'
    Save-StrategyOutcome $path $outcome @($target) 'activity.jsonl' $now
    Assert (@(Read-StrategyHistory $path $now|Where-Object classification -eq retained_with_activity).Count -eq 1) 'Activity warning presented as clean benefit'
    $other=[pscustomobject]@{Key='5:8';Name='claude.exe';Path='D:\Different\app.exe';Blocked=''}
    $advice=Get-StrategyAdvice $other @(Read-StrategyHistory $path $now)
    Assert (!$advice.CanReleaseTabs -and $advice.Bad -eq 0 -and $advice.Method -like '*Nessun rilascio*') 'Invented integration or learning crossed app paths'
    Assert (@(Read-StrategyHistory $path $now.AddDays(8)).Count -eq 0) 'Old evidence never expires'
    [IO.File]::WriteAllText($path,'{broken');$failed=$false
    try{Save-StrategyOutcome $path $outcome @($target) 'broken.jsonl' $now}catch{$failed=$true}
    Assert ($failed -and [IO.File]::ReadAllText($path) -eq '{broken') 'Corrupt history silently overwritten'

function Read-ReceivedArguments([string]$arguments) {
    $info=Microsoft.PowerShell.Utility\New-Object Diagnostics.ProcessStartInfo
    $childName=if($env:OS -eq 'Windows_NT'){if($PSVersionTable.PSEdition -eq 'Desktop'){'powershell.exe'}else{'pwsh.exe'}}else{'pwsh'}
    $info.FileName=Join-Path $PSHOME $childName
    $info.Arguments='-NoProfile -File "'+$probe+'" '+$arguments
    $info.UseShellExecute=$false;$info.CreateNoWindow=$true
    $info.RedirectStandardOutput=$true;$info.RedirectStandardError=$true
    $child=Microsoft.PowerShell.Utility\New-Object Diagnostics.Process
    $child.StartInfo=$info
    $childStarted=$false
    try {
        [void]$child.Start()
        $childStarted=$true
        Assert ($child.WaitForExit(10000)) 'Probe argomenti non terminata'
        $output=$child.StandardOutput.ReadToEnd();$stderr=$child.StandardError.ReadToEnd()
        Assert ($child.ExitCode -eq 0) ('Probe argomenti fallita: '+$stderr)
        # Windows PowerShell 5.1 can emit a JSON array as one pipeline object.
        # Enumerate it explicitly instead of counting the wrapper as one argument.
        $parsed=$output|ConvertFrom-Json
        foreach($argument in $parsed){Write-Output ([string]$argument)}
    } finally {if($childStarted -and !$child.HasExited){$child.Kill()};$child.Dispose()}
}

=== FILE: D:\.A2\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0-R22-FIX1\AdvancedMemoryEngine-v0.5.0\tests\test_agent.py ===
import os
import socket
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "agents"))
from cooperative import CacheAgent, MIB, private_session, request
from demo_cache import MapCache


class AgentTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.cache = MapCache(4)
        self.token = "a" * 64
        self.latency = 1.0
        self.agent = CacheAgent(self.cache, self.token, lambda: self.latency).start()

    def tearDown(self):
        self.agent.close()
        self.cache.close()

    def raw(self, message):
        with socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", self.agent.port), timeout=2) as s:
            s.sendall(message)
            return s.recv(512).decode("ascii")

    def test_status_has_no_mutation(self):
        value = request(self.agent.port, self.token)
        self.assertEqual(value[:3], (os.getpid(), 4 * MIB, 0))
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.size_bytes(), 4 * MIB)

    def test_release_and_process_remains_alive(self):
        result = request(self.agent.port, self.token, "RECLAIM", 2 * MIB)
        self.assertEqual(result[:3], (os.getpid(), 2 * MIB, 2 * MIB))
        self.assertEqual(request(self.agent.port, self.token)[0], os.getpid())

    def test_wrong_token_rejected_without_changes(self):
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
            request(self.agent.port, "b" * 64, "RECLAIM", MIB)
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.size_bytes(), 4 * MIB)

    def test_no_execute_or_arbitrary_command(self):
        self.assertIn("ERR request", self.raw(f"AME1 {self.token} EXECUTE 1\n".encode()))
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.size_bytes(), 4 * MIB)

    def test_budget_is_bounded(self):
        for n in [0, -1, 64 * MIB + 1]:
            with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
                request(self.agent.port, self.token, "RECLAIM", n)
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.size_bytes(), 4 * MIB)

    def test_cooldown_cannot_be_bypassed_by_client(self):
        request(self.agent.port, self.token, "RECLAIM", MIB)
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
            request(self.agent.port, self.token, "RECLAIM", MIB)
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.size_bytes(), 3 * MIB)

    def test_busy_application_declines(self):
        self.latency = 21
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
            request(self.agent.port, self.token, "RECLAIM", MIB)
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.size_bytes(), 4 * MIB)

    def test_invalid_latency_declines(self):
        self.latency = float("nan")
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
            request(self.agent.port, self.token, "RECLAIM", MIB)
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.size_bytes(), 4 * MIB)

    def test_multiple_frames_rejected(self):
        line = f"AME1 {self.token} RECLAIM {MIB}\n"
        self.assertIn("ERR frame", self.raw((line + line).encode()))
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.size_bytes(), 4 * MIB)

    def test_closed_map_is_unusable(self):
        mapping = self.cache._maps[-1]
        request(self.agent.port, self.token, "RECLAIM", MIB)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            mapping.read(1)

    def test_map_granularity_respects_budget(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.release(MIB - 1), 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.cache.size_bytes(), 4 * MIB)

    def test_private_session_is_new_and_protected(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            session = Path(temp) / "session"
            token = private_session(session)
            self.assertEqual(len(token), 64)
            self.assertEqual((session / "token.txt").read_text().strip(), token)
            with self.assertRaises(OSError):
                private_session(session)
            if os.name != "nt":
                self.assertEqual(session.stat().st_mode & 0o777, 0o700)
                self.assertEqual((session / "token.txt").stat().st_mode & 0o777, 0o600)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)

function Update-AgentView {

        $failure=Read-AgentJson (Join-Path (Get-AgentDirectory) 'startup-error.json') 4096
        if(!$alive -and $failure -and $failure.utc -and [DateTimeOffset]::Parse($failure.utc) -gt [DateTimeOffset]::Parse($s.heartbeat_utc)){$heading+=' - ULTIMO AVVIO FALLITO: '+$failure.message}

        $result.Text=if($s.last_result){'ULTIMO RISULTATO (rimane visibile): '+$s.last_result.message+"`r`n"+$(if($s.last_result.assessment){'Differenza privata residente con costo agente incluso: {0:+0.0;-0.0;0.0} MiB in meno.' -f ($s.last_result.assessment.NetObservedDropBytes/1MB)})}else{'Nessun intervento con risultato disponibile. Collegamento e autorizzazione del sito non dimostrano un risparmio RAM.'}

    }catch{$status.Text='Stato non leggibile: '+$_.Exception.Message}
